Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated high proficiency on English-centric medical examinations, their performance often declines when faced with non-English languages and multimodal diagnostic tasks. This study protocol describes the development of EuropeMedQA, the first comprehensive, multilingual, and multimodal medical examination dataset sourced from official regulatory exams in Italy, France, Spain, and Portugal. Following FAIR data principles and SPIRIT-AI guidelines, we describe a rigorous curation process and an automated translation pipeline for comparative analysis. We evaluate contemporary multimodal LLMs using a zero-shot, strictly constrained prompting strategy to assess cross-lingual transfer and visual reasoning. EuropeMedQA aims to provide a contamination-resistant benchmark that reflects the complexity of European clinical practices and fosters the development of more generalizable medical AI.

</details>


### [30] [LLM Predictive Scoring and Validation: Inferring Experience Ratings from Unstructured Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14321)
*Jason Potteiger,Andrew Hong,Ito Zapata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究使用GPT-4.1从棒球球迷的开放式文字反馈中预测其0-10分的体验评分，发现67%的预测值在自评分数的±1范围内，两种测量方式存在系统性约1分的差异，这一差异反映了不同构念：自评分数捕捉整体评判，而预测分数量化显著事件的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型能否从球迷的开放式文字反馈中预测其体验评分，以及预测评分与自评分数之间差异的本质——是测量误差还是具有理论意义的构念差异。

Method: 使用GPT-4.1仅基于单条开放式文字反馈预测0-10分体验评分，对约10,000条来自5支MLB球队的球迷反馈进行分析，并进行3次独立评分以检验可靠性。通过相关分析比较预测评分与整体体验评分及各具体方面的相关性。

Result: 67%的预测值落在自评分数±1范围内（36%完全一致）；三次独立运行的一致性极高（87%完全一致，99.9%±1内）。预测评分与整体体验评分高度相关（r=0.82），但系统性低于自评分数约1分。自评分数反映"评判"（整体评价），而预测分数反映"影响"（显著事件：难忘、情感强烈、不寻常或可行动的事件）。

Conclusion: 简单的、未优化的提示即可从文字反馈中方向性预测体验评分。预测评分与自评分数之间的差异是有意义的方法论差异，而非需要消除的误差——两种测量方式捕捉了不同构念，各包含对方缺失的信息。

Abstract: We tasked GPT-4.1 to read what baseball fans wrote about their game-day experience and predict the overall experience rating each fan gave on a 0-10 survey scale. The model received only the text of a single open-ended response. These AI predictions were compared with the actual experience ratings captured by the survey instrument across approximately 10,000 fan responses from five Major League Baseball teams. In total two-thirds of predicted ratings fell within one point of self-reported fan ratings (67% within +/-1, 36% exact match), and the predicted measurement was near-deterministic across three independent scoring runs (87% exact agreement, 99.9% within +/-1). Predicted ratings aligned most strongly with the overall experience rating (r = 0.82) rather than with any specific aspect of the game-day experience such as parking, concessions, staff, etc. However, predictions were systematically lower than self-reported ratings by approximately one point, and this gap was not driven by any single aspect. Rather, our analysis shows that self-reported ratings capture the fan's verdict, an overall evaluative judgment that integrates the entire experience. While predicted ratings quantify the impact of salient moments characterized as memorable, emotionally intense, unusual, or actionable. Each measure contains information the other misses. These baseline results establish that a simple, unoptimized prompt can directionally predict how fans rate their experience from the text a fan wrote and that a gap between the two numbers can be interpreted as a construct difference worth preserving rather than an error to eliminate.

</details>


### [31] [Purging the Gray Zone: Latent-Geometric Denoising for Precise Knowledge Boundary Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14324)
*Hao An,Yibin Lou,Jiayi Guo,Yang Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoDe框架，通过线性探测构建真相超平面，利用几何距离作为置信度信号进行几何去噪，从而解决大语言模型在决策边界附近的标签噪声问题，显著提升模型真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）常因无法准确感知自身知识边界而产生幻觉。现有放弃微调方法通常直接基于响应准确性划分数据集，导致模型在决策边界附近受到严重标签噪声影响，表现出高放弃率或幻觉问题。

Method: 本文从隐空间表示视角揭示决策超平面附近存在"灰色地带"，其内部信念模糊是性能瓶颈的核心。基于此洞察，提出GeoDe框架：①使用线性探测构建真相超平面；②采用几何距离作为置信度信号进行"几何去噪"，过滤模糊边界样本，保留高保真信号用于微调。

Result: 在多个模型（Llama3、Qwen3）和基准数据集（TriviaQA、NQ、SciQ、SimpleQA）上的实验表明，GeoDe显著提升模型真实性，并在分布外（OOD）场景中展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: GeoDe通过几何去噪策略有效解决了放弃微调中的标签噪声问题，为提升LLM真实性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often exhibit hallucinations due to their inability to accurately perceive their own knowledge boundaries. Existing abstention fine-tuning methods typically partition datasets directly based on response accuracy, causing models to suffer from severe label noise near the decision boundaries and consequently exhibit high rates of abstentions or hallucinations. This paper adopts a latent space representation perspective, revealing a "gray zone" near the decision hyperplane where internal belief ambiguity constitutes the core performance bottleneck. Based on this insight, we propose the **GeoDe** (**Geo**metric **De**noising) framework for abstention fine-tuning. This method constructs a truth hyperplane using linear probes and performs "geometric denoising" by employing geometric distance as a confidence signal for abstention decisions. This approach filters out ambiguous boundary samples while retaining high-fidelity signals for fine-tuning. Experiments across multiple models (Llama3, Qwen3) and benchmark datasets (TriviaQA, NQ, SciQ, SimpleQA) demonstrate that GeoDe significantly enhances model truthfulness and demonstrates strong generalization in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. Code is available at https://github.com/Notbesidemoon/GeoDe.

</details>


### [32] [Faithfulness Serum: Mitigating the Faithfulness Gap in Textual Explanations of LLM Decisions via Attribution Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14325)
*Bar Alon,Itamar Zimerman,Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"Faithfulness Serum"：先用反事实提示注入（提示注入）协议量化LLM事后文本解释的"认知忠实性"（epistemic faithfulness），发现无论开源中等模型还是GPT-4o、Gemini 2.0 Flash等闭源模型，其解释普遍不忠实（会掩盖真正影响答案的外部提示）；随后提出一种免训练的注意力层干预方法——用PE-LRP计算问题token级归因热图并按强度α注入后softmax注意力分数（第17–19层），从而使解释锚定在模型内部证据上，在MMLU、CommonsenseQA、SciQ、ARC-C、OpenBookQA及开放式问答上一致提升忠实性（部分情形相对提升近100%），并可缓解职业相关的"信用归因偏差"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已被广泛使用但仍是黑箱，后验文本解释（post-hoc textual explanation）因其能表达抽象、复合概念而优于固定维度的热图工具。然而现有工作多追求"看起来合理、与人类预期一致"的说明（主观忠实性），很少检验解释是否真正反映了模型内部实际依赖的证据（认知忠实性）。同时，CoT研究表明模型的思维链常常并不忠实于其真实意图，且CoT在决策过程中逐步生成、难以作为受控的事后解释；本文因此转向"先决策、后解释"的回溯式说明场景。需要一个可靠的、无需人工真值的忠实性度量手段，以及一种能在不重训模型的前提下提升该忠实性的机制。

Method: 1）评估协议：借鉴CoT监控的提示干预设置，先让模型在无提示下给出初始答案a0，再重新初始化对话并注入四类提示（Unethical Information—未授权访问并由模型自行决定是否使用；Metadata—XML/标签包裹的机读元数据含错误答案；Sycophancy—如"斯坦福教授表示答案是C"；Grader Hack—隐藏校验函数`return answer == "A"`），提示答案从错误选项中随机采样。若a1≠a0即构成反事实样例，说明提示确实影响了决策，忠实的解释必须体现该提示。解释提取用General（"What were the reasons behind your choice?"）与Protocol-Specific（分别说明题内依据与题外提示）两种提示；忠实性用两种互补指标衡量：Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct作LLM裁判（人工验证平均一致率93.4%、Cohen's κ=0.84），以及对Atanasova等CT的改版——按提示类型抽取特征关键词（professor/stanford；access/unauthorized/system；internal/validation/function；metadata/validation/<answer>）做匹配以降低误报。协议还扩展到开放式问答（提示需含完整自然语言答案）。
2）Faithfulness Serum方法（免训练）：模型作答后用PE-LRP（改进的LLM版LRP，捕捉位置信息流）以答案首生成token为参照计算问题token级相关性热图R∈[0,1]^T；在解释解码的每一步，对指定层l∈[l_start,l_end]内指向问题token的post-softmax注意力做A'_i = A_i + α·R_i；干预仅作用于问题token（排除系统提示与生成token），确保引导源自模型原有推理。层区间由logit lens分析选定（答案token logit在第17层后急剧上升，故取17–19层）；α通过distinct-1词法多样性扫描确定（Llama 3.1 8B取0.06，Qwen 2.5 7B取0.1，以40%为退化阈值），并可按样本自适应（TTα：监控distinct-1在35%–45%区间内增减α，最多15步，平均7.2步收敛）。消融包括层区间、相关性来源（LRP vs attention rollout vs layer-to-layer）、解码阶段（首token/首句/全程）、PE-LRP目标token，以及与4种提示工程基线的正交性验证。

Result: 1）不忠实普遍存在：开源与闭源模型在所有提示类型下忠实率都很低（如Llama 3.1 8B General提示LLM裁判下：Unethical 8.6%、Metadata 3.0%、Sycophancy 36.1%、Grader Hack 2.8%；GPT-4o的Metadata类型为0%）。Protocol-Specific提示通常提升忠实率，但GPT-4o出现反常效果。
2）干预有效：Faithfulness Serum在所有模型、基准、提示类型上大幅提升忠实性，MMLU 1000例上如Qwen的Unethical 14.1%→29.6%、Llama的Grader Hack 2.8%→10.7%、Sycophancy 47.0%→65.0%等，部分情形相对提升近100%；CommonsenseQA/SciQ/ARC-C/OpenBookQA全量评估中General设置下改进一致，Protocol-Specific下增益更大，仅3例出现轻微退化（归因于α针对General提示调优）。开放式问答同样一致提升（Metadata 5.9%→34.1%）。
3）TTα优于固定α（如Unethical 15.9%→32.0%，平均7.2次迭代收敛），额外开销仅一次反向传播（PE-LRP），推理期干预几乎无成本。
4）正交性与消融：与两种更强提示工程基线叠加仍一致增益，说明增益非源自提示设计；17–19层优于其他层区间；全程解码优于仅首token/首句干预；LRP相关性来源明显优于attention rollout与layer-to-layer；PE-LRP取首token与取答案token效果相当。
5）信用归因偏差：不同职业提示的可欺骗性与被承认率呈"双重偏差"——专家角色（斯坦福教授、神经外科医生）更易翻转答案且基线归因更高（21.2%、20.1%），街头摊贩等仅2.3%、10.7%；干预后各职业忠实率均提升（教授43.8%、出租车司机13.0%）。

Conclusion: 本文指出事后文本解释的核心缺陷是"认知忠实性缺失"而非流畅性或说服力不足，并给出一套可复现的反事实提示注入基准与免训练的归因引导干预机制（LRP热图→注意力加偏），把解释生成锚定到模型内部证据上，为可解释、可审计、可信的LLM提供了一条有原则的路径。局限包括：仅适用于"先预测后解释"范式，不适用于独立审计或前置安全检查；干预仅触及注意力，未涵盖FFN、残差与归一化动态，可能影响完整性与鲁棒性。伦理上作者提示：揭示可被结构化提示操纵的条件可能暴露新的攻击面，职业偏差结论亦可能被误用，建议配合严格监督与缓解措施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance and have revolutionized NLP, but their lack of explainability keeps them treated as black boxes, limiting their use in domains that demand transparency and trust. A promising direction to address this issue is post-hoc text-based explanations, which aim to explain model decisions in natural language. Prior work has focused on generating convincing rationales that appear to be subjectively faithful, but it remains unclear whether these explanations are epistemically faithful, whether they reflect the internal evidence the model actually relied on for its decision. In this paper, we first assess the epistemic faithfulness of LLM-generated explanations via counterfactuals and show that they are often unfaithful. We then introduce a training-free method that enhances faithfulness by guiding explanation generation through attention-level interventions, informed by token-level heatmaps extracted via a faithful attribution method. This method significantly improves epistemic faithfulness across multiple models, benchmarks, and prompts.

</details>


### [33] [Shuffle the Context: RoPE-Perturbed Self-Distillation for Long-Context Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14339)
*Zichong Li,Chen Liang,Liliang Ren,Tuo Zhao,Yelong Shen,Weizhu Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RoPE-Perturbed Self-Distillation方法，通过扰动RoPE位置编码创建训练样本的不同"视角"，并利用自蒸馏确保模型在不同视角下产生一致预测，从而增强LLM在长上下文任务中的位置鲁棒性，显著提升Llama-3-8B和Qwen-3-4B在RULER基准上的长上下文性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在长上下文场景（如检索增强生成、多文档推理）中易受位置偏差影响，标准长上下文微调策略仍然脆弱，模型准确率高度依赖相关证据的绝对位置，即使控制任务格式和难度后仍表现出高位置方差问题。

Method: 提出RoPE-Perturbed Self-Distillation训练正则化器。核心思想是对同一训练序列的RoPE索引进行扰动，形成替代"视角"（即将上下文不同部分移动到不同位置），然后通过自蒸馏训练模型在不同视角下产生一致预测，从而减少对脆弱位置编码的依赖。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B和Qwen-3-4B的长上下文适配实验中，该方法在长上下文基准上取得一致提升：Llama-3-8B在RULER-64K上提升12.04%，Qwen-3-4B在RULER-256K上提升2.71%，同时增强了超出训练上下文窗口的长度外推能力。

Conclusion: 通过RoPE索引扰动和自蒸馏的一致性约束，可有效鼓励模型依赖语义信号而非脆弱的位置依赖，显著提升长上下文理解和长度外推能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly operate in settings that require reliable long-context understanding, such as retrieval-augmented generation and multi-document reasoning. A common strategy is to fine-tune pretrained short-context models at the target sequence length. However, we find that standard long-context adaptation can remain brittle: model accuracy depends strongly on the absolute placement of relevant evidence, exhibiting high positional variance even when controlling for task format and difficulty.
  We propose RoPE-Perturbed Self-Distillation, a training regularizer that improves positional robustness. The core idea is to form alternative "views" of the same training sequence by perturbing its RoPE indices -- effectively moving parts of the context to different positions -- and to train the model to produce consistent predictions across views via self-distillation. This encourages reliance on semantic signals instead of brittle position dependencies. Experiments on long-context adaptation of Llama-3-8B and Qwen-3-4B demonstrate consistent gains on long-context benchmarks, including up to 12.04% improvement on RULER-64K for Llama-3-8B and 2.71% on RULER-256K for Qwen-3-4B after SFT, alongside improved length extrapolation beyond the training context window.

</details>


### [34] [When PCOS Meets Eating Disorders: An Explainable AI Approach to Detecting the Hidden Triple Burden](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14356)
*Apoorv Prasad,Susan McRoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究开发小型开源语言模型，自动检测多囊卵巢综合征（PCOS）女性在社交媒体中的身体形象困扰、饮食失调和代谢问题的"三重负担"，通过可解释的方式实现共病检测。最佳模型在150个测试样本上达到75.3%的精确匹配准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCOS女性面临身体形象困扰、饮食失调和代谢问题的三重风险显著升高，但现有NLP方法缺乏透明度和共病识别能力。

Method: 从6个子版块收集1,000条PCOS相关帖子，由两名标注员基于Lee等人(2017)临床框架进行标注。使用LoRA微调三个模型（Gemma-2-2B、Qwen3-1.7B、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B），生成带文本证据的结构化解释。

Result: 最佳模型在150个保留测试集上达到75.3%的精确匹配准确率，具有稳健的共病检测能力和强解释性。诊断复杂性增加时性能下降。

Conclusion: 模型适合作为筛查工具而非自主诊断，Open weights模型在保持竞争力的同时提供了透明度和可解释性。

Abstract: Women with polycystic ovary syndrome (PCOS) face substantially elevated risks of body image distress, disordered eating, and metabolic challenges, yet existing natural language processing approaches for detecting these conditions lack transparency and cannot identify co-occurring presentations. We developed small, open-source language models to automatically detect this triple burden in social media posts with grounded explainability. We collected 1,000 PCOS-related posts from six subreddits, with two trained annotators labeling posts using guidelines operationalizing Lee et al. (2017) clinical framework. Three models (Gemma-2-2B, Qwen3-1.7B, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B) were fine-tuned using Low-Rank Adaptation to generate structured explanations with textual evidence. The best model achieved 75.3 percent exact match accuracy on 150 held-out posts, with robust comorbidity detection and strong explainability. Performance declined with diagnostic complexity, indicating their best use is for screening rather than autonomous diagnosis.

</details>


### [35] [The Cost of Language: Centroid Erasure Exposes and Exploits Modal Competition in Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14363)
*Akshay Paruchuri,Ishan Chatterjee,Henry Fuchs,Ehsan Adeli,Piotr Didyk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态大模型在视觉感知任务中表现不佳的结构性原因：语言表征过度压制视觉表征。通过"质心替换"探针和"文本质心对比解码"干预方法，在不需重训练的情况下提升模型性能最高达16.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语言模型在视觉感知任务上系统性表现欠佳，但这一失败的结构性原因尚不清楚。需要一种受控探针来诊断模态依赖问题。

Method: 提出质心替换（centroid replacement）方法：将每个token替换为其最近K-means质心，作为模态依赖的受控探针。跨7个模型、3个架构家族进行实验。基于此不对称性提出文本质心对比解码（text centroid contrastive decoding）：对比去除文本质心结构后的参考模型进行解码。

Result: 擦除文本质心结构导致4倍于擦除视觉质心结构的准确率损失，揭示语言表征即使在需视觉推理的任务中也压制视觉表征。文本质心对比解码在单个任务上最高恢复+16.9%准确率。标准微调模型增益（+5.6%）大于偏好优化模型（+1.5%）。

Conclusion: 模态竞争是结构局部化的，可在推理时无需重训练进行纠正，可作为诊断信号指导未来多模态训练。

Abstract: Multimodal language models systematically underperform on visual perception tasks, yet the structure underlying this failure remains poorly understood. We propose centroid replacement, collapsing each token to its nearest K-means centroid, as a controlled probe for modal dependence. Across seven models spanning three architecture families, erasing text centroid structure costs 4$\times$ more accuracy than erasing visual centroid structure, exposing a universal imbalance where language representations overshadow vision even on tasks that demand visual reasoning. We exploit this asymmetry through text centroid contrastive decoding, recovering up to +16.9% accuracy on individual tasks by contrastively decoding against a text-centroid-erased reference. This intervention varies meaningfully with training approaches: standard fine-tuned models show larger gains (+5.6% on average) than preference-optimized models (+1.5% on average). Our findings suggest that modal competition is structurally localized, correctable at inference time without retraining, and quantifiable as a diagnostic signal to guide future multimodal training.

</details>

*Hyunkyung Park,Arkaitz Zubiaga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对对话中自动事实核查面临的非正式语言挑战，提出通过阶段性去口语化（结合表面规范化和指代消解）生成保守改写候选，并使用语义感知的一致性门控机制（BiCon-Gate）选择改写结果。实验表明该方法在DialFact基准上提升了检索和验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动事实核查研究忽视了对话中频繁出现的口语化表达问题，多轮对话场景下的非正式语言处理不足。

Method: 提出阶段性去口语化方法：1）轻量级表面规范化；2）作用域内指代消解。通过BiCon-Gate语义门控机制，仅在改写候选被对话上下文语义支持时选择改写版本，否则保留原句。

Result: 在DialFact基准上，方法提升了证据检索和事实验证性能，SUPPORTS类别提升尤为显著，优于包括一次性LLM改写在内的多个竞争基线。

Conclusion: 保守改写策略结合语义门控可有效稳定下游事实核查，阶段性处理优于单次LLM生成式改写。

Abstract: Automated fact-checking in dialogue involves multi-turn conversations where colloquial language is frequent yet understudied. To address this gap, we propose a conservative rewrite candidate for each response claim via staged de-colloquialisation, combining lightweight surface normalisation with scoped in-claim coreference resolution. We then introduce BiCon-Gate, a semantics-aware consistency gate that selects the rewrite candidate only when it is semantically supported by the dialogue context, otherwise falling back to the original claim. This gated selection stabilises downstream fact-checking and yields gains in both evidence retrieval and fact verification. On the DialFact benchmark, our approach improves retrieval and verification, with particularly strong gains on SUPPORTS, and outperforms competitive baselines, including a decoder-based one-shot LLM rewrite that attempts to perform all de-colloquialisation steps in a single pass.

</details>

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过语义投影自动将WordNet风格的词汇资源扩展到新语言。给定带标注的英语语料库及其翻译，通过投影英语同义词集到对齐的目标语言词元来生成新义项，并使用双语词典增强对齐器并过滤错误投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言词汇资源（如WordNet）的构建需要大量人工标注，难以快速扩展到新语言。现有方法依赖外部资源或缺乏可解释性，需要一种数据驱动、解释性强且资源需求少的方法来实现跨语言义项生成。

Method: 语义投影法：首先使用预训练对齐器将英语语料库与目标语言翻译对齐；然后引入双语词典增强对齐器质量；最后通过词典过滤纠正错误的义项投影。

Result: 在多语言上实验结果表明，该投影-过滤策略在保持可解释性的同时提升了精确率，且仅需少量外部资源即可运行。

Conclusion: 语义投影结合双语词典过滤是扩展词汇资源的有效途径，兼具数据驱动性与结果可解释性。

Abstract: We study the task of automatically expanding WordNet-style lexical resources to new languages through sense generation. We generate senses by associating target-language lemmas with existing lexical concepts via semantic projection. Given a sense-tagged English corpus and its translation, our method projects English synsets onto aligned target-language tokens and assigns the corresponding lemmas to those synsets. To generate these alignments and ensure their quality, we augment a pre-trained base aligner with a bilingual dictionary, which is also used to filter out incorrect sense projections. We evaluate the method on multiple languages, comparing it to prior methods, as well as dictionary-based and large language model baselines. Results show that the proposed project-and-filter strategy improves precision while remaining interpretable and requiring few external resources. We plan to make our code, documentation, and generated sense inventories accessible.

</details>


### [38] [The Autocorrelation Blind Spot: Why 42% of Turn-Level Findings in LLM Conversation Analysis May Be Spurious](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14414)
*Ferdinand M. Schessl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当前人-LLM多轮对话评估中普遍存在的统计推断错误：连续对话轮次间存在自相关性，但绝大多数评估方法未对此进行校正，导致显著性估计严重膨胀。研究分析了66个轮次级指标，发现42%的"显著"关联在校正后消失，并提出了结合Chelton有效自由度与对话级块Bootstrap的两阶段校正框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮人-LLM对话评估广泛使用轮次级指标（turn-level metrics）衡量安全性、谄媚性、对话质量等属性，但连续轮次间存在统计依赖性。当前几乎所有评估流程都忽视了这一问题，可能导致大量假阳性结果，威胁研究结论的可重复性。

Method: 系统性分析66个轮次级指标在202轮多轮对话（11,639个轮次对，5名德语用户，4个LLM平台）中的自相关结构。采用Chelton (1983)有效自由度方法结合对话级块Bootstrap构建两阶段校正框架，并在预注册的保留数据集上验证。

Result: 标准池化分析产生严重膨胀的显著性估计：42%的"显著"关联在校正后无法存活。无记忆家族（嵌入速度、方向性、差分）聚合膨胀率为14%，而非无记忆家族（热循环、帧距离、词汇/结构、滚动窗口、累积、交互、时间戳）聚合膨胀率达33%，个别类别膨胀率从0%到100%不等。校正框架在验证集上达到57%的复制率，而池化方法仅30%。

Conclusion: 必须采用聚类稳健推断来处理多轮对话评估中的时间依赖性。研究提供了具体设计原则、发表检查清单和开源代码，并建议评估者在统计分析中明确区分记忆性与非记忆性指标家族。

Abstract: Turn-level metrics are widely used to evaluate properties of multi-turn human-LLM conversations, from safety and sycophancy to dialogue quality. However, consecutive turns within a conversation are not statistically independent -- a fact that virtually all current evaluation pipelines fail to correct for in their statistical inference. We systematically characterize the autocorrelation structure of 66 turn-level metrics across 202 multi-turn conversations (11,639 turn pairs, 5 German-speaking users, 4 LLM platforms) and demonstrate that naive pooled analysis produces severely inflated significance estimates: 42% of associations that appear significant under standard pooled testing fail to survive cluster-robust correction. The inflation varies substantially across categories rather than scaling linearly with autocorrelation: three memoryless families (embedding velocity, directional, differential) aggregate to 14%, while the seven non-memoryless families (thermo-cycle, frame distance, lexical/structural, rolling windows, cumulative, interaction, timestamp) aggregate to 33%, with individual category rates ranging from 0% to 100% depending on per-family effect size. We present a two-stage correction framework combining Chelton (1983) effective degrees of freedom with conversation-level block bootstrap, and validate it on a pre-registered hold-out split where cluster-robust metrics replicate at 57% versus 30% for pooled-only metrics. We provide concrete design principles, a publication checklist, and open-source code for the correction pipeline. A survey of ~30 recent papers at major NLP and AI venues that compute turn-level statistics in LLM evaluations finds that only 4 address temporal dependence at all, and 26 do not correct for it.

</details>


### [39] [Three-Phase Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14430)
*Mohammad R. Abu Ayyash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出「三相 Transformer」(3PT)：在标准 SwiGLU+RMSNorm+RoPE+GQA 解码器骨架上，把 d_model 残差流切成 N 条等宽「相位」通道，并在每个 block 中散布少量「相位感知」操作（逐相位 RMSNorm、注意力与 FFN 之间的 2D Givens 旋转、相位对齐的 GQA 头数、以及注入一维 DC 子空间的 Gabriel horn 绝对位置剖面 r(p)=1/(p+1)）。在 123M/WikiText-103 上相对同协议 RoPE-Only 基线取得 −7.20% PPL / −2.62% BPB，仅 +1,536 参数（0.00124%），步数收敛加速 1.93×（墙钟 1.64×，每步开销 +17%）。但相位数 N 更像参数共享旋钮：5.5M 上 N=1 最优，123M 三种子下 N=1 与 N=3 统计不可区分——真正起作用的是「通道化残差流+逐块旋转+逐相位归一化+horn DC 注入」这一组合，而非「三相」这一数字。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机有两层。其一是几何隐喻：平衡三相交流中三个相差 120° 的正弦在任意时刻和为零，且不存在一对反相关相；三相是「把一个循环等分为三」这一结构在数学与工程上的唯一小整数实例（与增三和弦、Park/Clarke 变换同构）。神经崩塌理论、Superposition 等工作表明 120° 等距单纯形是 K=3 平衡类的最优表示几何，因此作者将其「硬编码」进 Transformer 的通道结构。其二是架构搜索层面的动机：现有轻量级结构先验（NormFormer、nGPT、SimpleGPT、Depth-scaled init 等）能跨规模存活，作者希望找一个**参数近乎零成本、可与 RoPE 正交叠加**的残差流结构先验，而不是又一个位置编码或注意力变体。作者明确提出的判别标准是：若三相只是位置编码，则与 RoPE 叠加不可能带来收益（两个 PE 叠加无益）；若它还只是嵌入技巧，则删掉嵌入侧的相位结构后收益应消失——实验正是围绕这两个证伪点展开。

Method: 方法分「结构定义」与「实验链」两部分。

结构定义（canonical N=3）：(1) 三相通道划分——d_model 切成 3 条连续等宽带，不引入学习基、不做 √d 缩放，仅由下游索引方式隐式施加；(2) PhaseRotationLayer——置于 attention 与 FFN 之间且**非残差**（h←PR(h)），每相内按相邻偶奇对做 2D Givens 旋转，角度为可学习的 θ̂∈R^{d_phase/2} 加固定相偏 2πi/N；因是正交映射，梯度无衰减/放大，信息可逆；(3) 深度线性初始化 θ̂(ℓ)=(ℓ+1)π/(2L)，训练中各 θ̂ 可独立偏离；(4) PhaseAwareRMSNorm——用 3 个独立 RMSNorm(d_phase) 替代全局 RMSNorm，参数量与 RMSNorm(d) 完全相同；(5) 相位对齐 GQA——要求 N|n_q、N|n_kv（123M：n_q=12, n_kv=3, d_head=64）；(6) Gabriel's horn——每次前向计算跨相均值 μ̄ 并做 x' = x + (r(t) − μ̄)，把 DC 分量精确钉在 1/(t+1) 上，作为与 RoPE 正交的绝对位置旁路，r 为 buffer 零参数；最终配置 horn_inject=True、zero_mean_enforce=False、use_aux_loss=False。

实验链（15+ 组，全部读训练日志）：5.5M TinyStories 上做 10 步实验——vanilla 基线消融（增益来自嵌入侧而非注意力侧，淘汰注意力侧三相）、RoPE vs 三相的两阶段「先输后赢」转折、6 个 refinement 的累积链与 2^6=64 组正交网格（用 32 对 ON/OFF 边际效应给出每 flag 独立判决）、2k→20k 长程（PhaseAwareRMSNorm 在 2k 为 +1.91 PPL、20k 反转为小幅增益）、逐步删除冗余（sinusoidal PE、phase_scale、√d 缩放）、零和约束/残差旋转的负对照、horn 注入与可学习 horn、5 种子噪声底、7 值 N 扫描；123M WikiText-103 上做 4 组——与匹配 RoPE-Only 基线的 30k 步对比、U 形漂移画像、N=1 vs N=3、3 种子 × 2 个 N 配对检验，以及与外部已发布 checkpoint 的 BPB 定位比较。训练：AdamW、cosine+warmup、bf16 AMP、Flash Attention 2，5.5M 用 RTX 2070，123M 用 Colab G4 RTX Pro 6000。

Result: 主要数值：123M/WikiText-103，30k 步，seed 42——3PT PPL 16.0631 / BPB 1.0855 / val loss 2.7765，RoPE-Only 基线 PPL 17.31 / BPB 1.1148，即 −7.20% PPL、−2.62% BPB，参数 123,490,560 vs 123,489,024（+1,536，0.00124%）；3PT 在 step 14,000 达 PPL 17.45，基线需 27,000 步（1.93× 步数、1.64× 墙钟，每步开销 +17.2%）。5.5M/TinyStories 20k 步：13.9015 vs 17.0564（−18.09%），5 种子均值 13.8500±0.046。机制层面：零和约束取消后相位残差自发稳定在 ~7e-3（123M 跨相均值 [+0.0086,+0.0088,+0.0046]），被解释为 Noether 式守恒律框架的新实例；启用 horn 后 zero_sum_residual 在所有评估点精确等于 N×H_T/T（5.5M: 0.1273；123M: 0.0220，6 次 123M 运行到小数点后 6 位一致），构成 horn 与相位分解正交的解析证明，且与种子、N 无关；12 层时逐块 θ 的 L2 漂移呈 U 形（block 2 最小 0.069，block 0 与 block 11 最大 0.541/1.833），方向在 block 2 翻转，最深层是近均匀 −9.3° 收缩而非逐对特化。消融结论：可学习相位偏置为噪声（120° 由零和+正交+无冗余唯一确定）、残差化旋转变差（+0.06 PPL，block 0 漂移膨胀 6 倍）、aux 零和损失在硬减均值下梯度低于 float32 精度而「失效」、phase dropout/shrink 灾难性、去掉 phase_scale+√d 后 PPL 反降 0.43 且 θ 漂移减少 30–40%。N 扫描：5.5M 上 N=1 最优（13.6268 vs N=3 13.9015，超噪声 6×），N=4/N=3 有一次噪声内反转；123M 单种子 N=3 领先 0.10 PPL（30 个检查点全胜），但 3 种子配对后差值均值 +0.078（偏向 N=1）、样本 std 0.161、SE 0.093，符号随种子翻转——seed 42 恰是 N=3 分布的最好端与 N=1 分布的最差端，单种子比较被人为放大。外部定位：3PT 优于零样本 GPT-2 家族（BPB 1.0855 vs gpt2 1.2215），但输给 WT103 微调过的 GPT-2 系列（distilgpt2-finetuned 0.9993、gpt2-finetuned 0.9770、gpt2-large 0.8366），且存在预训练算力与深度差异双重混淆。

Conclusion: 论文的主张被限定为：3PT 开创了一类「残差流结构先验」干预——以极小参数代价（123M 上 +0.00124%）在标准 SwiGLU/RMSNorm/RoPE/GQA 骨架上强制残差流维持等分相位几何，并与 RoPE 正交组合（RoPE 管相对位置，horn 管绝对位置，DC 隧道是可写的 rank-1 旁路）。它既不是位置编码也不是嵌入论文：嵌入前向最终退化为 token_emb(x) 减跨相均值，贡献来自散布于 block 内的四个相位感知组件的协同。论文也坦承几项限制：主对比仅单种子（虽称 0.0293 BPB 差约为种子噪声 9 倍，但未做基线多种子直接测量）；5.5M 阶段用的是 TinyStories 而非真实语料；无与 nGPT/NormFormer 等同规模现代基线的对照；17% 每步墙钟开销来自未向量化的 per-phase Python 循环（实现问题）；外部基线比较因预训练算力与深度差异不构成干净 A/B；N 的最优值在 123M 处于噪声内。「三相」作为几何隐喻成立，作为数值最优则不成立。作者给出的后续方向：把深度线性初始化换成最小值落在前几块、尾部衰减的可学习非线性调度（U 形给出的最强架构线索）；系统扫描 DC 隧道中的剖面形状（斜坡、指数、对数间隔码、学习标量等）——该正交子空间槽位结论可推广到任何具备可证正交子空间结构的架构；123M 下做更多种子、更完整 N 扫描以判定是否存在真实的尺度依赖交叉；以及 350M–1B 更大规模、更大语料、与现代 RoPE-Only 基线做等算力对照，以判断 5.5M→123M 的差距（18.09%→7.20%）是收缩、持平还是扩大。整体而言，这是一篇实验链完整、自我修正叙事诚实（明确记录 Stage-1 负结果与单种子陷阱）的架构论文，其可信的净结论是「极低成本残差流几何先验 + 可写 DC 绝对位置旁路在 123M 规模上确有稳健增益」，而非「三相是最优相位数」。

Abstract: We present Three-Phase Transformer (3PT), a residual-stream structural prior for decoder-only Transformers on a standard SwiGLU + RMSNorm + RoPE + GQA backbone. The hidden vector is partitioned into N equally-sized cyclic channels, each maintained by phase-respecting ops: a per-channel RMSNorm, a 2D Givens rotation between attention and FFN that rotates each channel by theta + i*(2*pi/N), and a head-count constraint aligning GQA heads with the partition. The architecture is a self-stabilizing equilibrium between scrambling and re-imposition, not a bolted-on module. The partition carves out a one-dimensional DC subspace orthogonal to the channels, into which we inject a fixed Gabriel's horn profile r(p) = 1/(p+1) as an absolute-position side-channel composing orthogonally with RoPE's relative-position rotation. The canonical N=3 borrows its metaphor from balanced three-phase AC, where three sinusoids 120 degrees apart sum to zero with no anti-correlated pair. At 123M parameters on WikiText-103, 3PT achieves -7.20% perplexity (-2.62% bits-per-byte) over a matched RoPE-Only baseline at +1,536 parameters (0.00124% of total), with 1.93x step-count convergence speedup (1.64x wall-clock). N behaves as a parameter-sharing knob rather than a unique optimum: at 5.5M an N-sweep over {1,2,3,4,6,8,12} is near-monotone with N=1 winning; at 123M a three-seed sweep finds N=3 and N=1 statistically indistinguishable. The load-bearing mechanism is the channel-partitioned residual stream, per-block rotation, per-phase normalization, and horn DC injection. We characterize (a) self-stabilization of the geometry without explicit enforcement, a novel instance of the conservation-law framework for neural networks; (b) a U-shaped depth profile of rotation-angle drift at 12 layers; (c) orthogonal composition with RoPE, attention, and FFN.

</details>


### [40] [Hierarchical vs. Flat Iteration in Shared-Weight Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14442)
*Sang-Il Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过HRM-LM模型（使用双速递归对的层级共享权重结构）对比Transformer独立层堆叠架构，发现递归层级结构在表示能力上存在显著差距，即使在参数匹配的1.2B UniTF模型和5次独立运行验证下也无法弥补这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究层级结构化、共享权重的递归机制能否匹配Transformer语言模型中独立层堆叠的表示质量，这是一个关于模型架构效率与表达能力权衡的基础性问题。

Method: HRM-LM将L个独立Transformer层替换为双速递归对：Fast模块每步执行进行局部细化，Slow模块每T步执行进行全局压缩。该递归层级以共享参数方式展开M=N×T步。通过参数匹配的UniTF（1.2B）进行消融实验，跨越5次独立运行验证。

Result: 核心且最稳健的发现是两种方法之间存在明显的经验差距。层级结构化共享权重递归无法达到独立层堆叠的表示质量。

Conclusion: 递归层级结构（HRM-LM）在表示能力上显著劣于独立层堆叠方法，即使通过参数匹配和充分实验验证，这一差距依然存在，表明Transformer架构中独立层的堆叠结构具有更强的表达能力。

Abstract: We present an empirical study of whether hierarchically structured, shared-weight recurrence can match the representational quality of independent-layer stacking in a Transformer-based language model. HRM-LM replaces L independent Transformer layers with a two-speed recurrent pair: a Fast module operating at every step for local refinement, and a Slow module operating every T steps for global compression. This recurrent hierarchy is unrolled for M = N x T steps with shared parameters. The central and most robust finding, supported by a parameter-matched Universal Transformer ablation (UniTF, 1.2B) across five independent runs, is a sharp empirical gap between the two approaches.

</details>


### [41] [MARCA: A Checklist-Based Benchmark for Multilingual Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14448)
*Thales Sales Almeida,Giovana Kerche Bonás,Ramon Pires,Celio Larcher,Hugo Abonizio,Marcos Piau,Roseval Malaquias Junior,Rodrigo Nogueira,Thiago Laitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MARCA（Maritaca AI Research Checklist evAluation）是一个英葡双语（EN/PT）网页搜索基准，包含 52 道人工编写的多实体信息检索问题，每题配有人工校验的 checklist 式评分标准，用于同时衡量答案的正确性与完整性。论文在两种交互框架（Basic：模型直接调用 web_search/web_scrape；Orchestrator：顶层模型通过 delegate_to_subagent 将子问题委派给子代理）下评测了 14 个模型，每题重复 3 次并报告 run 级不确定性。核心发现：模型间性能差距巨大（Checklist Accuracy 从 0.260 到约 0.90）；任务分解/委派通常提升覆盖率，但对本来就很强的模型（如 GPT-4.1）反而可能因沟通开销与全局上下文丢失而略微下降；英→葡的语言迁移差异显著且方向不一致（部分模型在葡语上甚至更高，Qwen-3-235B、Gemini-2.5-Pro 则下降超过 5%）；准确率与成本大体正相关但效率差异明显，存在可选择的性价比前沿。基准已开源于 github.com/maritaca-ai/MARCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多地被当作通用信息源使用，其可靠性取决于能否有效搜索网页、筛选证据并综合出完整答案。已有 WebGPT、BrowseComp、BrowseComp-ZH、BrowseComp-Plus、Deep Research Bench 等基准主要面向英文，多语种（尤其葡萄牙语，虽有 2.5 亿+母语者）评测几乎空白。已有的多语检索资源（mMARCO、MIRACL）只评估段落检索，不涉及端到端的网页交互式智能体；已有的葡语评测（ENEM、律师资格考试、PoETa v2）也不涉及网页搜索与证据锚定回答。因此需要一个平行英/葡的网页搜索基准，使性能差异能被明确归因于语言本身。

Method: 数据集构建：52 道人工编写的多实体问题，覆盖 9 个主题领域，要求枚举多个实体并为每个实体配对正确属性，从而强制多源检索与证据整合；每题配人工 checklist rubric，明确列出期望实体及其必填字段/属性，用于同时判定正确性与完整性；部分题目采用"动态 checklist"，在评测时实时抓取（如 Sabia-2 论文第二作者在 Google Scholar 上的引用数）以反映网页当前状态。基准提供平行英/葡版本，且各语言配对应语言的评分清单（葡语问题用葡语 checklist 判定）。评测框架两种：Basic 框架中单模型直接使用 Serper API 提供的 web_search 与 web_scrape（返回 markdown 正文），上下文保留全部检索内容，每题最多 40 次工具调用迭代；Orchestrator 框架中顶层模型只拥有 delegate_to_subagent 委派工具，不直接访问网页，仅能看到子代理返回的摘录与引用，子代理拥有与 Basic 相同的搜索/抓取工具。评测协议：固定框架与语言，每题执行 k=3 次以刻画随机性；由 GPT-4.1（gpt-4.1-2025-04-14）作为 LLM 裁判，基于问题、答案与 checklist 逐条给出二元满足/不满足判定与简短解释，使用结构化输出解析保证格式一致。指标：Checklist Accuracy = 满足项 C / 总项 T；聚合采用 run 级平均（先对每次 run 计算问题均值，再对 k 次 run 求均值 μ），不确定性用带 Bessel 校正（ddof=1）的样本标准差 σ，报告为 μ±σ；另分析 EN/PT 差异、Basic vs Orchestrator 差异，以及以每题 token 消耗为成本轴的性能-成本权衡（Pareto 前沿）。

Result: 表 1（Checklist Accuracy，均值±run 级标准差）显示：最佳配置接近 0.90（Gemini-3-pro：EN Basic 0.885±0.020，EN Orch 0.897±0.042；GPT-5.2：EN Orch 0.881±0.039，PT Orch 0.872±0.022），最差为 Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite 的 EN Basic 0.260±0.015。多数模型 run 级波动较小（GPT-4.1 0.022、Gemini-3-flash 0.008），Gemini-2.5-Pro 波动较大（σ 最高 0.076），说明其对网页交互与证据整合的随机性更敏感。框架影响：多数模型 Orchestrator 优于 Basic，但幅度差异大——弱/中等模型收益显著（GPT-4.1-mini 0.640→0.719；Sabia-4 0.743→0.832；Qwen-3-235B 0.668→0.753；Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite 0.260→0.317），而部分强模型反而略降（GPT-4.1 0.815→0.779；GPT-5-mini 0.837→0.827），原因可能是编排与子代理间的沟通误差或全局上下文丢失；因此 Orchestrator 尤其适合难以在单一推理上下文中同时追踪多实体的模型。语言影响：性能迁移高度异质，GPT-4.1、GPT-4.1-mini、Sabiazinho-4 在葡语上与英文相当甚至更高（GPT-4.1-mini PT 0.735 vs EN 0.719），而 Qwen-3-235B（EN Orch 0.753→PT 0.678）与 Gemini-2.5-Pro（0.701→0.598）下降超过 5%；作者推测隐式将葡语查询翻译为英文检索在英文来源足够时有效，但在需要葡语本地内容（巴西政府职位、解放者杯细节）时失效。成本：准确率与每题 token 成本总体正相关但关系不平滑，相同准确率的模型成本可相差悬殊，帕累托前沿稀疏，存在明显的"性价比"选型空间。

Conclusion: MARCA 通过"人工问题 + checklist 式 rubric"的组合，实现了对 LLM 网页信息检索能力在正确性与完整性两个维度的细粒度测量，并首次提供可直接对比英/葡差异的平行网页搜索评测。结论指出：（1）交互设计（是否启用任务分解与子代理委派）会实质性影响多实体检索的表现，收益取决于模型自身的上下文整合能力，故评测必须显式声明框架；（2）多语网页交互不仅取决于语言理解，还取决于非英语查询构造、外语页面理解与跨源证据整合，翻译英文基准不足以刻画葡语等本地信息生态；（3）更高的清单覆盖率通常伴随更高推理成本，但各模型的准确率-成本比差异显著，可为实践者提供选型参考。局限方面，样本量偏小（52 题）、LLM 裁判本身存在偏差、仅覆盖英/葡两种语言，且动态 checklist 依赖实时抓取的可复现性风险，均是后续可扩展方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as sources of information, yet their reliability depends on the ability to search the web, select relevant evidence, and synthesize complete answers. While recent benchmarks evaluate web-browsing and agentic tool use, multilingual settings, and Portuguese in particular, remain underexplored. We present \textsc{MARCA}, a bilingual (English and Portuguese) benchmark for evaluating LLMs on web-based information seeking. \textsc{MARCA} consists of 52 manually authored multi-entity questions, paired with manually validated checklist-style rubrics that explicitly measure answer completeness and correctness. We evaluate 14 models under two interaction settings: a Basic framework with direct web search and scraping, and an Orchestrator framework that enables task decomposition via delegated subagents. To capture stochasticity, each question is executed multiple times and performance is reported with run-level uncertainty. Across models, we observe large performance differences, find that orchestration often improves coverage, and identify substantial variability in how models transfer from English to Portuguese. The benchmark is available at https://github.com/maritaca-ai/MARCA

</details>


### [42] [Filling in the Mechanisms: How do LMs Learn Filler-Gap Dependencies under Developmental Constraints?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14459)
*Atrey Desai,Sathvik Nair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨语言模型是否能像人类一样，在不同的句法结构（如wh-疑问句和话题化）中建立共享的填充语-空位依赖表征。研究使用DAS方法分析不同训练数据量下的模型，发现模型可以发展出共享的、对特定项目敏感的机制，但需要比人类多得多的训练数据才能习得类似的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究暗示LLMs可能具有跨句法结构的共享表征，但尚不清楚在发育可行数据量下训练的语言模型是否也具备这种能力。这涉及对语言习得机制和语言特异性归纳偏置的深入理解。

Method: 采用分布式对齐搜索（DAS）方法，分析在BabyLM挑战赛不同数据量下训练的语言模型，评估wh-疑问句和话题化两种结构间的填充语-空位依赖表征转移情况。

Result: 研究结果表明共享的、 item-sensitive（项目敏感）的机制可以在有限的训练数据下发展；然而，语言模型仍需远多于人类的训练数据才能习得可比较的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 研究强调了模型中语言特异性归纳偏置的必要性，提示当前语言模型在数据效率方面与人类认知存在显著差距。

Abstract: For humans, filler-gap dependencies require a shared representation across different syntactic constructions. Although causal analyses suggest this may also be true for LLMs (Boguraev et al., 2025), it is still unclear if such a representation also exists for language models trained on developmentally feasible quantities of data. We applied Distributed Alignment Search (DAS, Geiger et al. (2024)) to LMs trained on varying amounts of data from the BabyLM challenge (Warstadt et al., 2023), to evaluate whether representations of filler-gap dependencies transfer between wh-questions and topicalization, which greatly vary in terms of their input frequency. Our results suggest shared, yet item-sensitive mechanisms may develop with limited training data. More importantly, LMs still require far more data than humans to learn comparable generalizations, highlighting the need for language-specific biases in models of language acquisition.

</details>


### [43] [Psychological Steering of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14463)
*Leonardo Blas,Robin Jia,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于心理学的LLM行为引导框架，通过无界、流畅度约束的语义校准扫描来优化残差注入，相比现有方法在11/14个模型中表现更优，并发现P²与MD混合方法效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差流注入方法存在搜索空间受限、激活空间单位未校准两大缺陷，可能错过最优干预条件；同时，之前研究认为Prompting方法优于表示工程方法，但这一结论存在争议。

Method: 使用IPIP-NEO-120（OCEAN人格模型）评估框架，对6种注入方法进行对比实验；在语义校准单位中进行无界、流畅度约束的扫描；提出MD注入、P²与MD混合注入两种新方法。

Result: MD注入在14个LLM中的11个上优于P²基线，增益3.6%-16.4%；混合方法在13/14个模型上优于两者，增益较P²提升5.6%-21.9%，较MD提升3.3%-26.7%；MD注入符合线性表示假说，可作为可靠且近似线性的心理控制旋钮。

Conclusion: 表示工程是开放域心理引导的新前沿；MD注入具有线性可控性；但其诱导的OCEAN特质协方差模式偏离Big Two模型，说明习得表征与人类心理之间存在差距。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) emulate a consistent human-like behavior that can be shaped through activation-level interventions. This paradigm is converging on additive residual-stream injections, which rely on injection-strength sweeps to approximate optimal intervention settings. However, existing methods restrict the search space and sweep in uncalibrated activation-space units, potentially missing optimal intervention conditions. Thus, we introduce a psychological steering framework that performs unbounded, fluency-constrained sweeps in semantically calibrated units. Our method derives and calibrates residual-stream injections using psychological artifacts, and we use the IPIP-NEO-120, which measures the OCEAN personality model, to compare six injection methods. We find that mean-difference (MD) injections outperform Personality Prompting (P$^2$), an established baseline for OCEAN steering, in open-ended generation in 11 of 14 LLMs, with gains of 3.6\% to 16.4\%, overturning prior reports favoring prompting and positioning representation engineering as a new frontier in open-ended psychological steering. Further, we find that a hybrid of P$^2$ and MD injections outperforms both methods in 13 of 14 LLMs, with gains over P$^2$ ranging from 5.6\% to 21.9\% and from 3.3\% to 26.7\% over MD injections. Finally, we show that MD injections align with the Linear Representation Hypothesis and provide reliable, approximately linear control knobs for psychological steering. Nevertheless, they also induce OCEAN trait covariance patterns that depart from the Big Two model, suggesting a gap between learned representations and human psychology.

</details>


### [44] [CobwebTM: Probabilistic Concept Formation for Lifelong and Hierarchical Topic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14489)
*Karthik Singaravadivelan,Anant Gupta,Zekun Wang,Christopher MacLellan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CobwebTM是一种基于增量概率概念形成算法的低参数终身层次化主题模型，通过将Cobweb算法适应于连续文档嵌入，实现了无需预定义主题数量的在线语义层次构建、动态主题发现和层次组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经方法需要大量调参且存在灾难性遗忘和固定容量问题，经典概率模型缺乏灵活性和流数据适应能力，因此需要一种能终身学习、参数少且可动态调整的主题建模方法。

Method: 将Cobweb增量概念形成算法适应于预训练的连续文档嵌入，在线构建语义层次结构，无需预定义主题数量，支持流数据的动态主题创建和层次组织。

Result: 在多个数据集上实现了强主题一致性、时间稳定性和高质量层次结构。

Conclusion: 增量符号概念形成与预训练表示的结合是一种高效的主题建模方法，能够解决终身学习和动态适应问题。

Abstract: Topic modeling seeks to uncover latent semantic structure in text corpora with minimal supervision. Neural approaches achieve strong performance but require extensive tuning and struggle with lifelong learning due to catastrophic forgetting and fixed capacity, while classical probabilistic models lack flexibility and adaptability to streaming data. We introduce \textsc{CobwebTM}, a low-parameter lifelong hierarchical topic model based on incremental probabilistic concept formation. By adapting the Cobweb algorithm to continuous document embeddings, \textsc{CobwebTM} constructs semantic hierarchies online, enabling unsupervised topic discovery, dynamic topic creation, and hierarchical organization without predefining the number of topics. Across diverse datasets, \textsc{CobwebTM} achieves strong topic coherence, stable topics over time, and high-quality hierarchies, demonstrating that incremental symbolic concept formation combined with pretrained representations is an efficient approach to topic modeling.

</details>


### [45] [PeerPrism: Peer Evaluation Expertise vs Review-writing AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14513)
*Soroush Sadeghian,Alireza Daqiq,Radin Cheraghi,Sajad Ebrahimi,Negar Arabzadeh,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PeerPrism是一个包含20,690篇同行评审的大规模基准数据集，用于区分"创意来源"与"文本来源"，揭示当前LLM检测方法在混合人机协作场景下的局限性。研究发现，现有方法虽能在二元分类任务（人类vs AI）中取得高准确率，但在混合生成模式下预测结果严重分歧，表明当前方法混淆了表面文本实现与实质性学术贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM检测方法将同行评审中的 authorship 视为二元问题（人类vs AI），但实际审查流程中，评估性想法和文本表达可能来自不同来源，形成人机协作的连续谱系。这种方法忽略了现代评审工作的混合本质。

Method: 构建了覆盖完全人类、完全合成及多种混合转换的受控生成模式；在20,690篇同行评审的大规模数据集上进行系统评估；使用最先进的LLM文本检测方法进行基准测试；结合风格计量和语义分析进行深入探究。

Result: 当人类提供创意但使用AI生成表面文本时，检测器频繁产生分歧甚至相互矛盾的分类结果。这表明当前检测方法实际上是在识别表面文本特征而非创意本身。

Conclusion: 同行评审中的LLM检测不能简化为二元归属问题。作者身份应被建模为多维构造，同时涵盖语义推理和风格实现。PeerPrism是首个评估此类场景下人机协作的基准数据集。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in scientific peer review, assisting with drafting, rewriting, expansion, and refinement. However, existing peer-review LLM detection methods largely treat authorship as a binary problem-human vs. AI-without accounting for the hybrid nature of modern review workflows. In practice, evaluative ideas and surface realization may originate from different sources, creating a spectrum of human-AI collaboration.
  In this work, we introduce PeerPrism, a large-scale benchmark of 20,690 peer reviews explicitly designed to disentangle idea provenance from text provenance. We construct controlled generation regimes spanning fully human, fully synthetic, and multiple hybrid transformations. This design enables systematic evaluation of whether detectors identify the origin of the surface text or the origin of the evaluative reasoning. We benchmark state-of-the-art LLM text detection methods on PeerPrism. While several methods achieve high accuracy on the standard binary task (human vs. fully synthetic), their predictions diverge sharply under hybrid regimes. In particular, when ideas originate from humans but the surface text is AI-generated, detectors frequently disagree and produce contradictory classifications. Accompanied by stylometric and semantic analyses, our results show that current detection methods conflate surface realization with intellectual contribution.
  Overall, we demonstrate that LLM detection in peer review cannot be reduced to a binary attribution problem. Instead, authorship must be modeled as a multidimensional construct spanning semantic reasoning and stylistic realization. PeerPrism is the first benchmark evaluating human-AI collaboration in these settings. We release all code, data, prompts, and evaluation scripts to facilitate reproducible research at https://github.com/Reviewerly-Inc/PeerPrism.

</details>


### [46] [Mechanistic Decoding of Cognitive Constructs in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14593)
*Yitong Shou,Manhao Guan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于表征工程（RepE）的认知逆向工程框架，分析大语言模型对社会比较嫉妒的内部加工机制。通过结合评估理论、子空间正交化、回归加权和双向因果干预，从8个LLM中分离出嫉妒的两个心理前因：比较对象优越性（Superiority）和领域自我定义相关性（Relevance），并验证其因果效应。结果表明模型以线性组合方式编码嫉妒，内在表征与人类心理学构念高度一致，且有毒情感状态可被机械检测和精准抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法将模型视为黑箱或仅关注粗粒度基本情绪，对复杂情感状态的认知结构探索不足。社会比较嫉妒作为一种复杂情感，其内部加工机制尚不清晰，亟需新框架突破这一研究空白。

Method: 基于表征工程（RepE）的认知逆向工程框架，结合评估理论（appraisal theory）与多种技术手段：子空间正交化（subspace orthogonalization）分离情感因子、回归加权（regression-based weighting）量化贡献度、双向因果 steering 验证因果效应。

Result: 1）8个LLM（Llama、Qwen、Gemma家族）均以结构化线性组合编码嫉妒；2） Superiority 为基础触发因素，Relevance 为强度调节因子；3）模型内部表征与人类心理学构念广泛一致；4）有毒情感状态可被机械检测并精准抑制。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了复杂情感在LLM中具有可解释的认知结构，为AI安全中多智能体环境的表征监测与干预提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate increasingly sophisticated affective capabilities, the internal mechanisms by which they process complex emotions remain unclear. Existing interpretability approaches often treat models as black boxes or focus on coarse-grained basic emotions, leaving the cognitive structure of more complex affective states underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose a Cognitive Reverse-Engineering framework based on Representation Engineering (RepE) to analyze social-comparison jealousy. By combining appraisal theory with subspace orthogonalization, regression-based weighting, and bidirectional causal steering, we isolate and quantify two psychological antecedents of jealousy, Superiority of Comparison Person and Domain Self-Definitional Relevance, and examine their causal effects on model judgments. Experiments on eight LLMs from the Llama, Qwen, and Gemma families suggest that models natively encode jealousy as a structured linear combination of these constituent factors. Their internal representations are broadly consistent with the human psychological construct, treating Superiority as the foundational trigger and Relevance as the ultimate intensity multiplier. Our framework also demonstrates that toxic emotional states can be mechanically detected and surgically suppressed, suggesting a possible route toward representational monitoring and intervention for AI safety in multi-agent environments.

</details>


### [47] [NLP needs Diversity outside of 'Diversity'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14595)
*Joshua Tint*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场文件，指出NLP领域的多样性进展过度集中于公平性相关主题，并分析了导致边缘化研究者被限制在公平性领域的激励机制、偏见和障碍。文章通过调研NLP研究者的子领域人口统计数据来支持论点，提出了打破反馈循环和解决地理/语言障碍的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决NLP领域多样性研究分布不均的问题——公平性相关研究吸引了大量关注，而其他子领域的多样性工作被忽视。研究者担心这种失衡会进一步边缘化非公平性领域的研究人员。

Method: 调研NLP研究者的子领域人口统计数据，追踪研究人员的职业轨迹，分析激励机制、偏见和障碍如何影响边缘化研究者的研究领域选择。

Result: 发现多样性进展确实集中在公平性领域；识别出多种反馈循环、地理障碍和语言障碍，这些因素共同导致边缘化研究者被引导或限制进入公平性相关研究。

Conclusion: 需要打破强化差异的反馈循环，并解决阻碍NLP研究参与的地理和语言障碍，以确保所有NLP子领域都能实现更大的包容性和公平性。

Abstract: This position paper argues that recent progress with diversity in NLP is disproportionately concentrated on a small number of areas surrounding fairness. We further argue that this is the result of a number of incentives, biases, and barriers which come together to disenfranchise marginalized researchers in non-fairness fields, or to move them into fairness-related fields. We substantiate our claims with an investigation into the demographics of NLP researchers by subfield, using our research to support a number of recommendations for ensuring that all areas within NLP can become more inclusive and equitable. In particular, we highlight the importance of breaking down feedback loops that reinforce disparities, and the need to address geographical and linguistic barriers that hinder participation in NLP research.

</details>


### [48] [CausalDetox: Causal Head Selection and Intervention for Language Model Detoxification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14602)
*Yian Wang,Yuen Chen,Agam Goyal,Hari Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAUSALDETOX是一种利用因果推断识别并干预大语言模型中导致毒性生成的注意力头的新框架，通过概率必要性充分性(PNS)定位关键头，结合推理时干预和微调两种策略实现去毒化，并在新基准PARATOX上验证了5.34%的毒性降低提升和7倍的头选择加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLM)频繁生成有毒内容，带来安全部署风险。现有缓解策略存在质量下降或需高成本人工标注的问题，亟需更精准、高效的毒性干预方法。

Method: (1) 使用概率必要性充分性(PNS)识别导致毒性生成的关键注意力头；(2) 推理时干预策略构建动态输入特定 steering vectors；(3) PNS引导微调策略实现毒性表征的永久遗忘；(4) 提出PARATOX对齐毒性/非毒性句子对基准用于反事实评估。

Result: 相比基线方法，CAUSALDETOX实现高达5.34%的毒性降低提升，同时保持语言流畅性，且在注意力头选择上获得7倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过因果推断定位关键注意力头是一种可行且高效的大模型去毒化方法，结合推理时干预和微调两种策略可有效平衡毒性抑制与生成质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate toxic content, posing significant risks for safe deployment. Current mitigation strategies often degrade generation quality or require costly human annotation. We propose CAUSALDETOX, a framework that identifies and intervenes on the specific attention heads causally responsible for toxic generation. Using the Probability of Necessity and Sufficiency (PNS), we isolate a minimal set of heads that are necessary and sufficient for toxicity. We utilize these components via two complementary strategies: (1) Local Inference-Time Intervention, which constructs dynamic, input-specific steering vectors for context-aware detoxification, and (2) PNS-Guided Fine-Tuning, which permanently unlearns toxic representations. We also introduce PARATOX, a novel benchmark of aligned toxic/non-toxic sentence pairs enabling controlled counterfactual evaluation. Experiments on ToxiGen, ImplicitHate, and ParaDetox show that CAUSALDETOX achieves up to 5.34% greater toxicity reduction compared to baselines while preserving linguistic fluency, and offers a 7x speedup in head selection.

</details>


### [49] [Retrieve, Then Classify: Corpus-Grounded Automation of Clinical Value Set Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14616)
*Sumit Mukherjee,Juan Shu,Nairwita Mazumder,Tate Kernell,Celena Wheeler,Shannon Hastings,Chris Sidey-Gibbons*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RASC通过检索-选择两阶段框架解决临床价值集自动编写的难题：先从VSAC语料库检索相似价值集形成候选池，再用分类器筛选相关代码。理论优势在于将输出空间从完整词汇表压缩到小型候选池。在11,803个VSAC价值集上的大规模基准测试中，基于SAPBert的交叉编码器AUROC达0.852，F1达0.298，显著优于MLP基线和零样本GPT-4o。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床价值集编写（识别定义临床概念的所有标准化词汇代码）是临床质量测量和表型分析中的重复瓶颈。现有方法依赖专家手动编写，而直接使用大语言模型存在结构化临床词汇表庞大、版本控制和预训练记忆不可靠等问题。

Method: 提出检索增强集补全（RASC）方法：①从精心整理的语料库检索K个最相似的现有价值集形成候选池；②对候选代码应用分类器筛选相关代码。理论上检索-选择策略通过将输出空间从完整词汇表压缩到候选池来降低统计复杂度。实验采用交叉编码器（基于SAPBert微调）和MLP分类器，并在11,803个公开VSAC价值集上构建大规模基准。

Result: SAPBert交叉编码器AUROC~0.852，Value-set级别F1~0.298，优于MLP（AUROC~0.799，F1~0.250）；两种方法将每个真阳性对应的无关候选数从12.3（纯检索）降至3.2和4.4。零样本GPT-4o F1仅0.105，且48.6%返回代码不在VSAC中；性能差距随价值集规模增大而扩大，验证了RASC的理论优势。

Conclusion: RASC框架通过检索-选择策略有效缩小输出空间，在大规模临床价值集任务上显著优于纯检索和零样本LLM基线，其优势可跨多个分类器模型类别推广，代码已开源。

Abstract: Clinical value set authoring -- the task of identifying all codes in a standardized vocabulary that define a clinical concept -- is a recurring bottleneck in clinical quality measurement and phenotyping. A natural approach is to prompt a large language model (LLM) to generate the required codes directly, but structured clinical vocabularies are large, version-controlled, and not reliably memorized during pretraining. We propose Retrieval-Augmented Set Completion (RASC): retrieve the $K$ most similar existing value sets from a curated corpus to form a candidate pool, then apply a classifier to each candidate code. Theoretically, retrieve-and-select can reduce statistical complexity by shrinking the effective output space from the full vocabulary to a much smaller retrieved candidate pool. We demonstrate the utility of RASC on 11,803 publicly available VSAC value sets, constructing the first large-scale benchmark for this task. A cross-encoder fine-tuned on SAPBert achieves AUROC~0.852 and value-set-level F1~0.298, outperforming a simpler three-layer Multilayer Perceptron (AUROC~0.799, F1~0.250) and both reduce the number of irrelevant candidates per true positive from 12.3 (retrieval-only) to approximately 3.2 and 4.4 respectively. Zero-shot GPT-4o achieves value-set-level F1~0.105, with 48.6\% of returned codes absent from VSAC entirely. This performance gap widens with increasing value set size, consistent with RASC's theoretical advantage. We observe similar performance gains across two other classifier model types, namely a cross-encoder initialized from pre-trained SAPBert and a LightGBM model, demonstrating that RASC's benefits extend beyond a single model class. The code to download and create the benchmark dataset, as well as the model training code is available at: \href{https://github.com/mukhes3/RASC}{https://github.com/mukhes3/RASC}.

</details>


### [50] [StoryCoder: Narrative Reformulation for Structured Reasoning in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14631)
*Geonhui Jang,Dongyoon Han,YoungJoon Yoo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StoryCoder通过将代码生成问题重构为连贯的自然语言叙事（包括任务概述、约束条件和示例测试用例），在11个模型上实现平均18.7%的零样本pass@10提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅增强推理步骤或注入特定结构，但未改变分散的问题条件本身。人类将碎片化信息组织为连贯解释的思维方式启发了这一研究。

Method: 提出StoryCoder框架，将代码生成问题转化为包含任务概述、约束和测试用例的叙事结构，由选定的算法和风格类型引导。

Result: 在HumanEval、LiveCodeBench和CodeForces上测试11个模型，零样本pass@10平均提升18.7%。叙事重构引导模型采用正确算法策略，减少实现错误，产生更模块化的代码结构。

Conclusion: 叙事连贯性和类型对齐对性能至关重要，结构化问题表示对代码生成的重要性不随模型规模或架构变化。

Abstract: Effective code generation requires both model capability and a problem representation that carefully structures how models reason and plan. Existing approaches augment reasoning steps or inject specific structure into how models think, but leave scattered problem conditions unchanged. Inspired by the way humans organize fragmented information into coherent explanations, we propose StoryCoder, a narrative reformulation framework that transforms code generation questions into coherent natural language narratives, providing richer contextual structure than simple rephrasings. Each narrative consists of three components: a task overview, constraints, and example test cases, guided by the selected algorithm and genre. Experiments across 11 models on HumanEval, LiveCodeBench, and CodeForces demonstrate consistent improvements, with an average gain of 18.7% in zero-shot pass@10. Beyond accuracy, our analyses reveal that narrative reformulation guides models toward correct algorithmic strategies, reduces implementation errors, and induces a more modular code structure. The analyses further show that these benefits depend on narrative coherence and genre alignment, suggesting that structured problem representation is important for code generation regardless of model scale or architecture. Our code is available at https://github.com/gu-ni/StoryCoder.

</details>


### [51] [Pushing the Boundaries of Multiple Choice Evaluation to One Hundred Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14634)
*Nahyun Lee,Guijin Son*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"大规模选项评估协议"(Massive Option Evaluation)，将候选集从传统低选项设置扩展到100个选项，以减少机会命中率并更真实地揭示大语言模型的真实能力。在韩语拼写错误检测任务上的实验表明，传统低选项设置下的高准确率可能高估模型能力，而高密度干扰设置下的表现揭示了常规基准测试难以发现的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型评估中，多选评估广泛使用，但在低选项设置下（如4选1），模型可以通过捷径策略达到接近完美的准确率，从而掩盖其真实能力。作者认为需要一种能在极端干扰密度下压力测试模型可靠性的通用评估框架。

Method: 研究提出大规模选项评估协议，将候选集扩展至100个选项（100选1），在韩语正字法错误检测任务中进行实验。实验采用固定目标、重复重采样和洗牌方法以获得稳定估计。同时设计了填充控制和长度匹配测试以隔离上下文长度的影响。

Result: 实验发现：1) 低选项设置下的高表现可能高估模型能力，高N设置下优势通常减弱；2) 识别出两种主要失败模式——语义混淆和不确定性下的位置偏差（倾向于选择靠前选项）；3) 填充控制和长度匹配测试表明，主要瓶颈在于候选排序能力而非上下文长度。

Conclusion: 大规模选项评估是压力测试模型可靠性的通用框架，能够在极端干扰密度下揭示常规低选项基准难以发现的模型缺陷，为评估LLM真实能力提供了更严格的方法。

Abstract: Multiple choice evaluation is widely used for benchmarking large language models, yet near ceiling accuracy in low option settings can be sustained by shortcut strategies that obscure true competence. Therefore, we propose a massive option evaluation protocol that scales the candidate set to one hundred options and sharply reduces the impact of chance performance. We apply this framework to a Korean orthography error detection task where models must pick the single incorrect sentence from a large candidate set. With fixed targets and repeated resampling and shuffling, we obtain stable estimates while separating content driven failures from positional artifacts. Across experiments, results indicate that strong performance in low option settings can overstate model competence. This apparent advantage often weakens under dense interference at high $N$, revealing gaps that conventional benchmarks tend to obscure. We identify two failure modes, semantic confusion and position bias toward early options under uncertainty. To isolate the effect of context length, we run padding controlled and length matched tests, which suggest that the main bottleneck is candidate ranking rather than context length. Together, these findings support massive option evaluation as a general framework for stress testing model reliability under extreme distractor density, beyond what low option benchmarks can reveal.

</details>


### [52] [Fact4ac at the Financial Misinformation Detection Challenge Task: Reference-Free Financial Misinformation Detection via Fine-Tuning and Few-Shot Prompting of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14640)
*Cuong Hoang,Le-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于大语言模型（LLM）的无外部参考金融虚假信息检测框架，通过结合上下文学习和LoRA微调技术，在RFC-BENCH基准测试中取得第一名的成绩（公开测试集95.4%，私有测试集96.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融虚假信息的泛滥严重威胁市场稳定和投资者信任，但现实场景中往往缺乏外部证据进行交叉验证。现有方法多依赖外部知识库或实时检索，无法应对无参考验证的场景。

Method: 提出综合框架：（1）利用先进LLM的推理能力；（2）结合上下文学习策略（零样本和少样本提示）；（3）采用LoRA参数高效微调方法，使模型更好地对齐金融操纵文本的细微语言特征。

Result: 在RFC-BENCH基准测试的双榜单上均获第一：公开测试集准确率95.4%，私有测试集准确率96.3%，模型参数规模分别为14B和32B。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了仅依靠内部语义理解和上下文一致性即可有效检测金融虚假信息，为金融NLP领域的上下文感知虚假信息检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: The proliferation of financial misinformation poses a severe threat to market stability and investor trust, misleading market behavior and creating critical information asymmetry. Detecting such misleading narratives is inherently challenging, particularly in real-world scenarios where external evidence or supplementary references for cross-verification are strictly unavailable. This paper presents our winning methodology for the "Reference-Free Financial Misinformation Detection" shared task. Built upon the recently proposed RFC-BENCH framework (Jiang et al. 2026), this task challenges models to determine the veracity of financial claims by relying solely on internal semantic understanding and contextual consistency, rather than external fact-checking. To address this formidable evaluation setup, we propose a comprehensive framework that capitalizes on the reasoning capabilities of state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs). Our approach systematically integrates in-context learning, specifically zero-shot and few-shot prompting strategies, with Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to optimally align the models with the subtle linguistic cues of financial manipulation. Our proposed system demonstrated superior efficacy, successfully securing the first-place ranking on both official leaderboards. Specifically, we achieved an accuracy of 95.4% on the public test set and 96.3% on the private test set, highlighting the robustness of our method and contributing to the acceleration of context-aware misinformation detection in financial Natural Language Processing. Our models (14B and 32B) are available at https://huggingface.co/KaiNKaiho.

</details>


### [53] [CURaTE: Continual Unlearning in Real Time with Ensured Preservation of LLM Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14644)
*Seyun Bae,Seokhan Lee,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CURaTE是一种大语言模型持续遗忘方法，通过训练句子嵌入模型判断输入是否对应遗忘请求，从而决定是否回答或拒绝，无需修改LLM参数即可实现有效遗忘和近乎完美的知识保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽略了两个关键问题：(1)更新累积时实用性下降，(2)敏感信息的持续暴露。因此需要一种能够持续、即时执行遗忘且保持模型效用不衰减的方法。

Method: CURaTE首先在专门设计的数据集上训练句子嵌入模型，使其能够形成尖锐的决策边界，以判断给定输入是否对应任何存储的遗忘请求；然后根据输入与遗忘请求的相似度决定是正常回答还是返回拒绝响应。

Result: CURaTE不仅实现了比现有方法更有效的遗忘效果，而且通过避免修改语言模型参数，保持了近乎完美的知识保留能力。

Conclusion: CURaTE是唯一能够实现实时持续遗忘的方法，在有效遗忘与知识保留之间取得了良好平衡，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The inability to filter out in advance all potentially problematic data from the pre-training of large language models has given rise to the need for methods for unlearning specific pieces of knowledge after training. Existing techniques overlook the need for continuous and immediate action, causing them to suffer from degraded utility as updates accumulate and protracted exposure of sensitive information. To address these issues, we propose Continual Unlearning in Real Time with Ensured Preservation of LLM Knowledge (CURaTE). Our method begins by training a sentence embedding model on a dataset designed to enable the formation of sharp decision boundaries for determining whether a given input prompt corresponds to any stored forget requests. The similarity of a given input to the forget requests is then used to determine whether to answer or return a refusal response. We show that even with such a simple approach, not only does CURaTE achieve more effective forgetting than existing methods, but by avoiding modification of the language model parameters, it also maintains near perfect knowledge preservation over any number of updates and is the only method capable of continual unlearning in real-time.

</details>


### [54] [CURA: Clinical Uncertainty Risk Alignment for Language Model-Based Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14651)
*Sizhe Wang,Ziqi Xu,Claire Najjuuko,Charles Alba,Chenyang Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CURA框架，通过双层不确定性目标（个体层面校准和队列感知正则化）对临床语言模型进行不确定性微调，提升风险预测的校准性能和临床可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语言模型在临床风险预测中的应用日益广泛，但其不确定性估计往往校准不良，在临床上不可靠，可能导致过度自信的错误安抚，影响临床决策支持系统的可信度。

Method: CURA框架首先对领域特定的临床语言模型进行微调以获得任务自适应的患者嵌入，然后使用双层不确定性目标对多头分类器进行不确定性微调。具体包括：1）个体层面校准项，将预测不确定性与其错误可能性对齐；2）队列感知正则化器，将风险估计拉向嵌入空间中局部邻域的事件率，并对决策边界附近的模糊队列给予额外权重。该队列感知项可解释为带有邻域信息的软标签交叉熵损失（标签平滑视角）。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV临床风险预测任务和多种临床语言模型上的广泛实验表明，CURA持续改善校准指标，同时不显著损害区分能力。分析表明CURA减少了过度自信的错误安抚，为下游临床决策支持提供了更可信的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: CURA通过结合个体层面校准和队列感知正则化的双层不确定性目标，有效提升了临床语言模型风险预测的校准性能和临床可信度，为临床决策支持系统提供了更可靠的不确定性估计工具。

Abstract: Clinical language models (LMs) are increasingly applied to support clinical risk prediction from free-text notes, yet their uncertainty estimates often remain poorly calibrated and clinically unreliable. In this work, we propose Clinical Uncertainty Risk Alignment (CURA), a framework that aligns clinical LM-based risk estimates and uncertainty with both individual error likelihoods and cohort-level ambiguities. CURA first fine-tunes domain-specific clinical LMs to obtain task-adapted patient embeddings, and then performs uncertainty fine-tuning of a multi-head classifier using a bi-level uncertainty objective. Specifically, an individual-level calibration term aligns predictive uncertainty with each patient's likelihood of error, while a cohort-aware regularizer pulls risk estimates toward event rates in their local neighborhoods in the embedding space and places extra weight on ambiguous cohorts near the decision boundary. We further show that this cohort-aware term can be interpreted as a cross-entropy loss with neighborhood-informed soft labels, providing a label-smoothing view of our method. Extensive experiments on MIMIC-IV clinical risk prediction tasks across various clinical LMs show that CURA consistently improves calibration metrics without substantially compromising discrimination. Further analysis illustrates that CURA reduces overconfident false reassurance and yields more trustworthy uncertainty estimates for downstream clinical decision support.

</details>


### [55] [SPAGBias: Uncovering and Tracing Structured Spatial Gender Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14672)
*Binxian Su,Haoye Lou,Shucheng Zhu,Weikang Wang,Ying Liu,Dong Yu,Pengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SPAGBias框架——首个系统性评估LLM空间性别偏见的方法，结合62种城市微观空间分类、提示词库和三个诊断层（显式、概率、建构），对六种代表性模型进行测试，发现模型中的性别-空间关联远超现实世界分布，且偏见嵌入预训练、指令微调和奖励建模全过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空间中嵌入性别等级制度，而LLM可能被用于城市规划决策，因此需评估LLM是否再生产或放大空间性别偏见

Method: SPAGBias框架：62种城市微观空间分类体系 + 提示词库 + 三层诊断（显式强制选择重采样、概率级token不对称分析、建构性语义叙事角色分析）；故事生成实验；追踪实验（追溯偏见到预训练/指令微调/奖励建模阶段）；下游任务实验

Result: 发现超越公私二分法的细微微观层面的性别-空间映射；情感、用词和社会角色共同塑造"空间性别叙事"；提示设计、温度和模型规模影响偏见表达；模型关联远超现实世界分布；偏见嵌入整个模型流水线

Conclusion: 将社会性别理论与计算分析结合，将偏见研究拓展至空间领域，揭示LLM如何通过语言编码社会性别认知

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being increasingly used in urban planning, but since gendered space theory highlights how gender hierarchies are embedded in spatial organization, there is concern that LLMs may reproduce or amplify such biases. We introduce SPAGBias - the first systematic framework to evaluate spatial gender bias in LLMs. It combines a taxonomy of 62 urban micro-spaces, a prompt library, and three diagnostic layers: explicit (forced-choice resampling), probabilistic (token-level asymmetry), and constructional (semantic and narrative role analysis). Testing six representative models, we identify structured gender-space associations that go beyond the public-private divide, forming nuanced micro-level mappings. Story generation reveals how emotion, wording, and social roles jointly shape "spatial gender narratives". We also examine how prompt design, temperature, and model scale influence bias expression. Tracing experiments indicate that these patterns are embedded and reinforced across the model pipeline (pre-training, instruction tuning, and reward modeling), with model associations found to substantially exceed real-world distributions. Downstream experiments further reveal that such biases produce concrete failures in both normative and descriptive application settings. This work connects sociological theory with computational analysis, extending bias research into the spatial domain and uncovering how LLMs encode social gender cognition through language.

</details>


### [56] [Which bird does not have wings: Negative-constrained KGQA with Schema-guided Semantic Matching and Self-directed Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14749)
*Midan Shim,Seokju Hwang,Kaehyun Um,Kyong-Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了负约束知识图谱问答新任务NEST-KGQA，针对现有方法忽略负约束的问题，设计了PyLF逻辑形式和CUCKOO框架。该框架通过约束感知逻辑形式生成、模式引导语义匹配和自导向优化三阶段流程，提升多约束问答的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前知识图谱问答系统在处理负约束（如"不包括"、"没有"等）方面存在明显缺陷，而现有基准数据集和方法偏重于正向和计算约束，负约束在实际问题中频繁出现但被忽视。

Method: 1) 提出PyLF（Python格式化逻辑形式）以清晰表达否定语义；2) 设计CUCKOO框架，采用三阶段处理：约束感知逻辑形式草稿生成、模式引导语义匹配、自导向优化。优化策略仅在执行结果为空时才触发，降低计算成本。

Result: 实验表明，CUCKOO在传统KGQA和NEST-KGQA基准上均优于基线模型，特别是在少样本设置下表现突出。

Conclusion: 负约束是KGQA的重要但被忽视的维度；PyLF能有效表达否定逻辑；CUCKOO框架在多约束场景下具有鲁棒性和成本效益。

Abstract: Large language models still struggle with faithfulness and hallucinations despite their remarkable reasoning abilities. In Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA), semantic parsing-based approaches address the limitations by understanding constraints in a user's question and converting them into a logical form to execute on a knowledge graph. However, existing KGQA benchmarks and methods are biased toward positive and calculation constraints. Negative constraints are neglected, although they frequently appear in real-world questions. In this paper, we introduce a new task, NEgative-conSTrained (NEST) KGQA, where each question contains at least one negative constraint, and a corresponding dataset, NestKGQA. We also design PyLF, a Python-formatted logical form, since existing logical forms are hardly suitable to express negation clearly while maintaining readability. Furthermore, NEST questions naturally contain multiple constraints. To mitigate their semantic complexity, we present a novel framework named CUCKOO, specialized to multiple-constrained questions and ensuring semantic executability. CUCKOO first generates a constraint-aware logical form draft and performs schema-guided semantic matching. It then selectively applies self-directed refinement only when executing improper logical forms yields an empty result, reducing cost while improving robustness. Experimental results demonstrate that CUCKOO consistently outperforms baselines on both conventional KGQA and NEST-KGQA benchmarks under few-shot settings.

</details>


### [57] [CoPA: Benchmarking Personalized Question Answering with Data-Informed Cognitive Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14773)
*Hang Su,Zequn Liu,Chen Hu,Xuesong Lu,Yingce Xia,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper addresses the evaluation bottleneck for personalized QA in LLMs by mining preference divergence patterns to identify 6 key personalization factors, introducing CoPA—a benchmark with 1,985 user profiles that quantifies alignment between model outputs and user-specific cognitive preferences for more comprehensive evaluation than generic metrics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing personalization evaluation paradigms rely on lexical-level similarity or manual heuristics, lacking sufficient data-driven validation.

Method: Mining Community-Individual Preference Divergence (CIPD) to extract 6 key personalization factors; quantifying alignment between model outputs and user-specific cognitive preferences inferred from interaction patterns.

Result: Introduced CoPA benchmark with 1,985 user profiles enabling fine-grained factor-level assessment for personalized QA evaluation.

Conclusion: CoPA benchmark (1,985 user profiles) provides fine-grained, factor-level evaluation for personalized QA, outperforming generic metrics in discriminative power.

Abstract: While LLMs have demonstrated remarkable potential in Question Answering (QA), evaluating personalization remains a critical bottleneck. Existing paradigms predominantly rely on lexical-level similarity or manual heuristics, often lacking sufficient data-driven validation. We address this by mining Community-Individual Preference Divergence (CIPD), where individual choices override consensus, to distill six key personalization factors as evaluative dimensions. Accordingly, we introduce CoPA, a benchmark with 1,985 user profiles for fine-grained, factor-level assessment. By quantifying the alignment between model outputs and user-specific cognitive preferences inferred from interaction patterns, CoPA provides a more comprehensive and discriminative standard for evaluating personalized QA than generic metrics. The code is available at https://github.com/bjzgcai/CoPA.

</details>


### [58] [Knowing When Not to Answer: Evaluating Abstention in Multimodal Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14799)
*Nishanth Madhusudhan,Vikas Yadav,Alexandre Lacoste*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-AQA基准测试，用于评估视觉-语言模型(VLMs)和多智能体系统(MAS)在多模态场景下的有效弃权能力(EA)。研究发现在标准提示下VLMs几乎不选择弃权，多智能体虽能改善弃权但引入准确性-弃权权衡，且改善多模态弃权需要专门的弃权感知训练而非更好的提示或更多智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLMs和MAS的评估范式假设问题总是可回答的，迫使模型必须给出响应。然而在现实应用中，当证据不足时模型应选择弃权以保证可靠性。现有文本单模态虽已有弃权研究，但多模态场景下的弃权问题仍未被充分探索。

Method: 构建MM-AQA基准测试，通过两个维度的转换(视觉模态依赖性和证据充分性)从可回答实例构造不可回答实例。评估了3个前沿VLMs(闭源和开源)和2种MAS架构，共2079个样本。

Result: (1)标准提示下VLMs几乎不弃权，简单置信度基线就能超越此设置；(2)MAS能改善弃权但引入准确性-弃权权衡；(3)顺序设计匹配或超越迭代变体，说明瓶颈在于误校准而非推理深度；(4)模型在图像或文本证据缺失时会弃权，但对降级或矛盾证据会尝试调和。

Conclusion: 有效的多模态弃权需要专门的弃权感知训练，而非更好的提示策略或更多智能体设计。

Abstract: Effective abstention (EA), recognizing evidence insufficiency and refraining from answering, is critical for reliable multimodal systems. Yet existing evaluation paradigms for vision-language models (VLMs) and multi-agent systems (MAS) assume answerability, pushing models to always respond. Abstention has been studied in text-only settings but remains underexplored multimodally; current benchmarks either ignore unanswerability or rely on coarse methods that miss realistic failure modes. We introduce MM-AQA, a benchmark that constructs unanswerable instances from answerable ones via transformations along two axes: visual modality dependency and evidence sufficiency. Evaluating three frontier VLMs spanning closed and open-source models and two MAS architectures across 2079 samples, we find: (1) under standard prompting, VLMs rarely abstain; even simple confidence baselines outperform this setup, (2) MAS improves abstention but introduces an accuracy-abstention trade-off, (3) sequential designs match or exceed iterative variants, suggesting the bottleneck is miscalibration rather than reasoning depth, and (4) models abstain when image or text evidence is absent, but attempt reconciliation with degraded or contradictory evidence. Effective multimodal abstention requires abstention-aware training rather than better prompting or more agents.

</details>


### [59] [Modeling LLM Unlearning as an Asymmetric Two-Task Learning Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14808)
*Zeguan Xiao,Siqing Li,Yong Wang,Xuetao Wei,Jian Yang,Yun Chen,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将LLM遗忘学习重新定义为非对称双任务问题，以保留为主目标、遗忘为辅助目标。提出保留优先的梯度合成框架，并实例化PCGrad和SAGO两种方法。SAGO在WMDP Bio/Cyber和RWKU基准上表现优异，如WMDP Bio任务中MMLU性能从44.6%提升至96.0%，且遗忘强度相当。核心发现：重塑梯度几何结构比重新平衡损失更能缓解遗忘-保留之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM遗忘学习面临核心挑战：在移除目标知识的同时需保留模型的一般能力。当前方法通常将遗忘和保留视为同等重要，导致两者之间存在显著权衡。本文认为这种对称处理方式存在根本性问题——保留应该是主要目标，遗忘只是辅助。

Method: 提出保留优先的梯度合成框架，将任务特定梯度提取与冲突感知组合解耦。具体包含两个实例化方法：(1) 适配PCGrad解决梯度冲突；(2) 提出SAGO方法，通过构造性符号约束实现更紧密的梯度对齐。两种方法均保证与保留梯度的非负余弦相似度。

Result: 在WMDP Bio/Cyber和RWKU基准上的实验表明：SAGO持续推进Pareto前沿。例如在WMDP Bio任务（SimNPO+GD基线）上，MMLU性能恢复从44.6%（朴素方法）→ 94.0%（+PCGrad）→ 96.0%（+SAGO），同时保持相当的遗忘强度。

Conclusion: 核心洞察：重塑梯度几何结构（而非重新平衡损失）是缓解遗忘-保留权衡的关键。SAGO通过构造性符号约束实现更紧密的梯度对齐，为LLM遗忘学习提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) aims to remove targeted knowledge while preserving general capability. In this paper, we recast LLM unlearning as an asymmetric two-task problem: retention is the primary objective and forgetting is an auxiliary. From this perspective, we propose a retention-prioritized gradient synthesis framework that decouples task-specific gradient extraction from conflict-aware combination. Instantiating the framework, we adapt established PCGrad to resolve gradient conflicts, and introduce SAGO, a novel retention-prioritized gradient synthesis method. Theoretically, both variants ensure non-negative cosine similarity with the retain gradient, while SAGO achieves strictly tighter alignment through constructive sign-constrained synthesis. Empirically, on WMDP Bio/Cyber and RWKU benchmarks, SAGO consistently pushes the Pareto frontier: e.g., on WMDP Bio (SimNPO+GD), recovery of target model MMLU performance progresses from 44.6% (naive) to 94.0% (+PCGrad) and further to 96.0% (+SAGO), while maintaining comparable forgetting strength. Our results show that re-shaping gradient geometry, rather than re-balancing losses, is the key to mitigating unlearning-retention trade-offs.

</details>


### [60] [Domain Fine-Tuning FinBERT on Finnish Histopathological Reports: Train-Time Signals and Downstream Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14815)
*Rami Luisto,Liisa Petäinen,Tommi Grönholm,Jan Böhm,Maarit Ahtiainen,Tomi Lilja,Ilkka Pölönen,Sami Äyrämö*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究在标注数据稀缺的NLP分类任务中，对芬兰语BERT模型进行领域微调的方法，并探索通过观察嵌入空间变化几何特征来预测领域特定预训练收益的可行性，应用于医疗AI场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI领域常面临数据集获取周期长、标注成本高的问题，因此需要探索如何在小样本标注数据条件下有效利用无标注数据进行领域自适应。

Method: 对芬兰语BERT模型在芬兰医学文本数据上进行领域微调；观察并分析领域微调过程中嵌入空间的几何变化特征。

Result: 从芬兰语BERT在医学文本上的领域微调中获得了实验观察；初步探索了通过嵌入几何变化预测领域特定预训练收益的方法。

Conclusion: 为医疗AI场景下处理标注数据稀缺问题提供了领域自适应策略，并通过嵌入几何分析为预训练收益评估提供了新思路。

Abstract: In NLP classification tasks where little labeled data exists, domain fine-tuning of transformer models on unlabeled data is an established approach. In this paper we have two aims.
  (1) We describe our observations from fine-tuning the Finnish BERT model on Finnish medical text data.
  (2) We report on our attempts to predict the benefit of domain-specific pre-training of Finnish BERT from observing the geometry of embedding changes due to domain fine-tuning.
  Our driving motivation is the common\situation in healthcare AI where we might experience long delays in acquiring datasets, especially with respect to labels.

</details>


### [61] [Pangu-ACE: Adaptive Cascaded Experts for Educational Response Generation on EduBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14828)
*Dinghao Li,Wenlong Zhou,Zhimin Chen,Yuehan Peng,Hong Ni,Chengfu Zou,Guoyu Shi,Yaochen Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Pangu-ACE 是一个面向 EduBench（shared-8 教育任务集）的"1B 起草 → 路由校准 → 按需升级到 7B 专家模型"级联系统。作者重写了早期稿件以匹配真实实现与仓库归档产物，并修正了离线评测中把只满足格式检查的"脚手架/占位符"输出误判为成功回答的严重缺陷。在完整中文测试集（7013 样本）上，cascade_final 相比遗留的 rule_v2 系统将确定性质量从 0.457 提升到 0.538、格式有效率从 0.707 提升到 0.866，同时有 19.7% 的请求在 1B 阶段直接接受（7B 调用率降至 80.3%）。但端到端延迟不降反升（26.35s vs 18.33s），因此论文明确不主张延迟收益，只主张"路由选择性"。GPT-5.4 外部基线因 API endpoint/key 配置错误（404、Anthropic 前缀密钥）仍待基础设施修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育场景中不同任务的难度与输出契约差异巨大：简短的问答、评分细则、个性化学习计划、教学材料大纲既不应消耗同等推理预算，也不应用同一套输出契约评判，因此天然适合"样本级（sample-level）而非 token 级"的计算分配。论文的具体动机还包括三点：(1) 重写早期草稿——初稿描述的是量化重型设计，而实际实现并归档的是校准式 1B→7B 级联，论文必须与代码和产物对齐（artifact-first）；(2) 修正离线评测 bug——旧摘要高估了开放式任务质量，因为仅通过表面格式检查（含字面量 draft_answer 占位符、router 脚手架）的输出被当作成功的最终回答；(3) 明确外部基线缺口——GPT-5.4 重评判流程已本地实现，但因 provider endpoint 与密钥无效而无法完成，属于基础设施而非评测代码问题。方法上借鉴 FrugalGPT、RouteLLM 的成本感知路由/级联思想，以及 ReAct、Reflexion、CoT、self-consistency 的分阶段推理与双过程理论，但明确不声称提出新的推理范式。

Method: 基准：shared-8 EduBench 任务集（Q&A、AG、EC、IP、PCC、PLS、QG、TMG；因中英文资产不对称而排除 ES），确定性切分（seed 42 分层，中文 7793 / 英文 8102，冻结 dev/test），主结果使用完整中文测试集 7013 样本；保留的中英文 fast diagnostic 子集（354/368）与消融子集（178）用于补充基线与消融，另有 76 样本的后归档 live pilot。系统管线（Pangu-ACE）：(1) 请求归一化——映射到含任务键、任务族、语言、期望输出格式的共享 schema；(2) 1B tutor-router 生成低成本草稿答案及路由元数据（任务族预测、置信度、格式信号）；(3) 风险校准——与 dev 集上冻结的阈值比较；(4) 专家精修——高风险样本连同 1B 草稿送入按任务族（reasoning/assessment/planning）组织的 7B 专家提示；(5) 校验与修复——归一化输出、拒绝 router 脚手架与占位符、修复畸形字段并归档全部产物。评测协议：artifact-first，一次性推理后保存 prediction/trace JSONL，后续仅在 CPU 侧重算摘要。指标分四类：确定性质量（闭式任务精确/字段级比对；开放式任务要求 valid ∧ substantive，substantive 排除占位符、路由脚手架与畸形嵌套草稿）、格式有效率、路由率（1B 接受率与 7B 调用率，从 trace 提取）、归档端到端延迟。

Result: 主表（中文 7013 样本）：cascade_final 质量 0.538、格式 0.866、延迟 26.35s、7B 调用率 80.3%、1B 直接受 19.7%；对照 rule_v2 质量 0.457、格式 0.707、延迟 18.33s、7B 调用率 100%。即质量 +0.081、格式 +0.159，但延迟劣化 +8s。快速诊断子集：中文 7b_only 0.591 / cascade 0.510（7B 调用 77.1%），英文 7b_only 0.534 / cascade 0.472（7B 调用 85.3%），两种语言下 1b_only 均显著更弱（0.167 / 0.195）；质量-延迟前沿显示当前级联在墙钟时间上不优于 7b_only。路由具有强任务依赖性：IP 由 1B 直接接受 78.0%，PLS 35.5%，Q&A 25.9%；EC 与 QG 全部升级，TMG 升级 99.6%。逐任务增益相对 rule_v2：QG +0.265、TMG +0.366、EC/IP 略有提升，而 PLS 明显下降（约 -0.27）、AG 两系统均差。消融（178 样本）：去掉草稿条件化质量反升至 0.610，去掉专家提示格式最好（0.921）且延迟最低（23.48s），说明当前配置并非严格占优，仅存在优化空间；76 样本 live pilot 中 cascade_final 仍以 0.553 保持质量优先，去掉专家提示以 0.493 质量换 20.38s 延迟与 8732 成本代理。外部参考（gpt-4o 评测下的 1–10 分采样基线，仅作上下文不可直接比较）：DeepSeek R1 9.05、Qwen Max 9.00、DeepSeek V3 8.99。

Conclusion: 论文给出的是一个更窄但更站得住脚的结论：校准后的 1B→7B 管线在真实归档产物上确实优于遗留规则系统（质量 0.457→0.538，格式 0.707→0.866），并展现出明确的任务相关路由选择性；但选择性尚未转化为墙钟加速，当前部署的级联反而更慢，因此效率主张仅限于"路由选择性"。作者明确列出局限：主表仅限中文（唯一完整归档 cascade_final 与 rule_v2 的切分）、1B-only/7B-only 基线仅为诊断性子集而非全量归档、GPT-5.4 基线对齐因 endpoint（api.aicodemirror.com/v1 返回 404）与密钥格式（Anthropic 式 sk-ant- 前缀，不匹配 OpenAI 兼容接口）问题待基础设施修复，并提供了可复现的重评判工作流。下一工程里程碑不是再增加模型角色，而是把路由节省转化为实际延迟收益（融合路由阶段、降低编排开销、缩短专家输出），并修复 provider 配置以完成外部基线对齐；模型侧的优化重点应转向评分提示词与个性化学习模板，而非继续扩展路由栈。

Abstract: Educational assistants should spend more computation only when the task needs it. This paper rewrites our earlier draft around the system that was actually implemented and archived in the repository: a sample-level 1B to 7B cascade for the shared-8 EduBench benchmark. The final system, Pangu-ACE, uses a 1B tutor-router to produce a draft answer plus routing signals, then either accepts the draft or escalates the sample to a 7B specialist prompt. We also correct a major offline evaluation bug: earlier summaries over-credited some open-form outputs that only satisfied superficial format checks. After CPU-side rescoring from saved prediction JSONL, the full Chinese test archive (7013 samples) shows that cascade_final improves deterministic quality from 0.457 to 0.538 and format validity from 0.707 to 0.866 over the legacy rule_v2 system while accepting 19.7% of requests directly at 1B. Routing is strongly task dependent: IP is accepted by 1B 78.0% of the time, while QG and EC still escalate almost always. The current archived deployment does not yet show latency gains, so the defensible efficiency story is routing selectivity rather than wall-clock speedup. We also package a reproducible artifact-first paper workflow and clarify the remaining external-baseline gap: GPT-5.4 re-judging is implemented locally, but the configured provider endpoint and key are invalid, so final sampled-baseline alignment with GPT-5.4 remains pending infrastructure repair.

</details>


### [62] [Exploring and Testing Skill-Based Behavioral Profile Annotation: Human Operability and LLM Feasibility under Schema-Guided Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14843)
*Yufeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将行为侧面（Behavioral Profile, BP）标注重构为一组可分离的"标注技能"，并以"技能文件驱动"流程评估LLM辅助标注。以30个中文隐喻性色彩词派生词的3,134条索引行和14特征BP schema为基础，通过两名标注者在300实例验证集上的两轮"仅schema"协议，将技能划分为直接可操作（5项）、聚焦重标定后可恢复（4项）与结构性欠定义（5项）。GPT-5.4在保留技能上取得准确率0.678、κ=0.665、加权F1=0.695，但可行性是选择性的而非全面的：人机难度在技能层面高度一致（r=0.881），在实例层面（r=0.016）与词项层面（r=-0.142）却不一致，呈现"共享分类学、独立执行"（shared taxonomy, independent execution）模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BP标注要求在同一批语言材料上同时对多个维度（如隐喻色彩词的行为语义特征）进行编码，属多维耦合的人工判断任务，因此长期难以自动化。以往对LLM标注能力的评估多停留在任务级整体指标，未能揭示任务内部不同技能之间的可行性差异；本研究主张将BP标注视为一束（bundle）标注技能而非单一任务，从技能可行性角度重新评估LLM辅助标注。

Method: 语料为30个中文隐喻性色彩词派生词的3,134条concordance lines，配14特征BP schema。方法核心是"技能文件驱动"管线：每个特征由外部定义的schema文件、决策规则与示例共同约束，使技能可被逐一操作与诊断。两名人类标注者对300实例验证子集完成两轮"仅schema"协议（schema-only，即仅依据schema而非具体语境判断），据此把14项技能分类为：直接可操作、经聚焦重标定可恢复、结构性欠定义。在同一设置下评测GPT-5.4与三个本地可部署开源模型。分析指标包括技能保留后的准确率、Cohen's κ、加权F1，人类与模型难度画像在技能／实例／词项三个层级上的相关性，以及模型间与模型—人类的成对一致性（pairwise agreement）。
