Abstract: Large language models frequently produce mutually inconsistent answers when reasoning over multiple related queries. We study case-file logical consistency: maintaining a globally satisfiable belief state across interdependent queries. We introduce a benchmark of 390 multi-query reasoning instances with entailment/contradiction/unknown labels and propose set-level metrics including Case Satisfiability Rate, Contradiction Density and Revision Cost. Our solver-augmented approach extracts commitments, verifies global satisfiability and performs counterexample-guided repair. Across four reasoning domains, our method substantially reduces cross-query contradictions (SetCons: 0.56 to 0.94) while preserving per-query accuracy, demonstrating that global coherence is critical for robust multi-query reasoning.

</details>


### [113] [Dissecting Failure Dynamics in Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14528)
*Wei Zhu,Jian Zhang,Lixing Yu,Kun Yue,Zhiwen Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM推理失败的原因，发现错误通常源于少量早期关键转换点（而非均匀分布），这些点伴随token级熵的局部峰值。基于此发现，提出GUARD框架，通过不确定性信号探测并重定向关键转换，提升推理可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通过扩展推理时思考实现良好性能，但其推理失败机制尚不清楚。当前方法主要关注扩展推理时计算资源，而非理解推理何时及如何首次偏离正确轨道。

Method: 1. 分析模型生成的推理轨迹；2. 识别错误起源的关键转换点；3. 测量token级熵变化；4. 验证从同一中间状态的不同延续能否产生正确解；5. 提出GUARD框架，利用不确定性信号探测和重定向关键转换。

Result: 错误非均匀分布，而是集中在少量早期转换点；这些转换伴随局部熵峰值；同一中间状态的不同延续仍可能得到正确答案；基于失败动态的干预能提升推理可靠性。

Conclusion: 理解推理何时及如何首次偏离至关重要，这补充了现有聚焦于扩展推理时计算的方法；GUARD框架通过不确定性引导的干预证明了针对性干预比全面扩展计算更有效。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance through extended inference-time deliberation, yet how their reasoning failures arise remains poorly understood. By analyzing model-generated reasoning trajectories, we find that errors are not uniformly distributed but often originate from a small number of early transition points, after which reasoning remains locally coherent but globally incorrect. These transitions coincide with localized spikes in token-level entropy, and alternative continuations from the same intermediate state can still lead to correct solutions. Based on these observations, we introduce GUARD, a targeted inference-time framework that probes and redirects critical transitions using uncertainty signals. Empirical evaluations across multiple benchmarks confirm that interventions guided by these failure dynamics lead to more reliable reasoning outcomes. Our findings highlight the importance of understanding when and how reasoning first deviates, complementing existing approaches that focus on scaling inference-time computation.

</details>


### [114] [TRACER: Trace-Based Adaptive Cost-Efficient Routing for LLM Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14531)
*Adam Rida*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACER 是一个开源系统：在生产环境中把 LLM 分类调用留下的「标注轨迹」当作免费训练集，训练轻量级 ML 代理模型（surrogate），并用一个「一致性门（parity gate）」决定是否上线——只有当代理模型与教师 LLM 的一致率 TA ≥ 用户设定阈值 α 时才放行；每次重拟合还会生成五类可解释性产物，刻画「代理处理哪些输入、哪些回退给教师」的路由边界。结果：Banking77（77 类）在 α=0.8/0.85 时覆盖率 100%，α=0.95 时覆盖率 83.2%、TA 0.959；CLINC150（157 个有效类）代理完全替代教师；MNLI 上门控在全部 9 种配置下正确拒绝部署（覆盖率 0），验证了安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产团队普遍用 LLM 做零样本分类以省去人工标注，但每次调用都要付费并累积成本；同时每次调用都会在日志中留下一条「输入—输出」标注对（论文称之为 trace），这是免费且持续增长的训练集。作者的核心观察是：LLM 不只是分类器，更是「标注引擎」。既有方法存在两个缺口：(1) 学习延迟（learning-to-defer）与 LLM 路由/级联方法（Cascade、FrugalGPT、Hybrid LLM、RouteLLM、AutoMix）都要求部署前已有标注数据，而标注预算与标签体系演化恰是生产瓶颈；(2) 现存方法在 LLM 与更便宜的 LLM 之间路由，而 TRACER 路由到成本严格更低的经典 ML；(3) 缺少对「路由边界本身」的可解释性——既有 XAI 只解释单条预测。于是本文聚焦三个开放问题：代理何时足够可靠可上线、它处理什么/回退什么、随数据积累该边界如何演化。

Method: 问题形式化：教师 T: X→Y，每次调用产生 trace (x, T(x))；拟合代理 f 与接受器 a: X→[0,1]，混合分类器 h(x)=f(x) 若 a(x)≥τ，否则 T(x)；目标为在 TA=Pr[f(x)=T(x) | a(x)≥τ] ≥ α 的约束下最大化覆盖率 Cov=Pr[a(x)≥τ]。管线：① 离线用 BGE-large-en-v1.5（1024 维）预计算文本嵌入；② 每次 refit 训练候选池（逻辑回归、SGD 线性分类器、MLP、决策树、随机森林、Extra-Trees），按留出验证集上的教师标签 macro-F1 选最优；③ 为每个候选训练二元接受器——逻辑回归，输入为代理输出概率向量的四个置信特征（top-1 概率、top-2 概率、二者 margin、归一化熵），作者用独立接受器而非直接阈值化 predict_proba，因为接受器能学到「top-1 相同但 margin 大小不同」的可靠性差异；④ τ 通过扫描所有唯一接受器分数、在留出集上取满足 TA≥α 的最大覆盖来标定。两条候选管线族：Global（整体 TA 已超 α 时不做门控）与 L2D（代理+接受器门控），系统取满足 TA 约束下覆盖更高者。③ 一致性门（parity gate）：候选必须在从未用于训练与阈值标定的 shadow 留出集上通过 TA≥α，且还需跨过 5% 覆盖率下限（防止退化管线），否则保守地全量回退教师；该门同时是时序的——第 t 天失败可在数据积累后通过。④ 持续学习飞轮：Day 1 全量走教师并入缓冲，之后 tracer.update() 合并新 trace 并从头重拟合以保证阈值在全集上有效；每次回退调用免费产生一个偏向决策边界的训练样本，无需人工标注。⑤ 每次 refit 生成五类解释产物，解释「回退划分」而非单条预测：切片摘要（按类/长度分箱的处理率与切片 TA）、代表性样本卡（各组质心最近样本）、对比边界对（同教师标签但路由相反的样本对，源自反事实解释）、时间增量（各标签处理率相对上次 refit 的变化，借鉴 DeltaXplainer）、分歧卡（按代理预测类别分组的错误样本）。实验：Banking77（10,003 训练/3,080 测试，教师 GT 准确率 78.7%/81.0%）、CLINC150（18,000/4,500，教师 94.4%/93.1%，教师额外产生 7 个伪标签形成 157 个有效类）、MNLI（以 GT 标签代替教师的宽松负对照）。教师统一为 Claude Sonnet 4.6，使用缓存预测、无实时 API 调用。训练数据按 5 个等量「日批次」增量：Day1 fit()，Day2–5 update()。基线为「置信度阈值回退」：在全部 trace 一次性训练逻辑回归并扫描概率阈值，代表拥有完整后见之明的最简单回退方法。α 扫描 {0.80,0.85,0.90,0.95}。

Result: 三种模式清晰分离。① CLINC150 全量卸载：四个 α 下覆盖率均 100%，系统选择 Global 管线；α=0.95 时校准集 TA 0.952 但测试集 TA 仅 0.930，低于目标（作者标注 †，明确指出「校准—测试差距」这一重要保留意见）；端到端 GT 准确率 92.4%，与纯教师 93.1% 相当；基线 TA 反而更高（0.942 vs 0.930）。② Banking77 覆盖—质量权衡：α≤0.85 覆盖率 100%（Global）；α=0.90 时 L2D 激活，覆盖 96.1%、TA 0.912；α=0.95 覆盖 83.2%、TA 0.959。基线在 α=0.90/0.95 覆盖更高（99.8%/87.8%），但 TRACER 在 α=0.95 的 TA 更紧（0.959 vs 0.951）。端到端 GT 准确率 80.4%（α=0.80）到 81.6%（α=0.95），接近/略高于教师 81.0%。③ MNLI 正确拒绝：α∈{0.85,0.90,0.95} × 数据量{2000,5000,10000} 共 9 种配置下覆盖率均为 0%，即使使用 GT 标签作「慷慨教师」也无法在冻结嵌入上分离蕴含/中立/矛盾，作者归因于句对嵌入无法捕捉组合性推理。飞轮动力学：Banking77 在 α=0.95 下覆盖从 73.4%（2001 traces）→78.2%→74.5%（Day3 因从头重标定回落）→83.4%→82.6%，校准 TA 始终 ≥0.950；α=0.80 时 Day1 即达 100%；CLINC150 在 α=0.95 下 Day1 57.4%、Day2 即 100%。成本测算（Sonnet 4.6 定价 $3/M 输入、$15/M 输出）：Banking77 约 $2.60/千次调用，1 万次/天即 $26/天（$9,500/年），83.2% 覆盖下降至 $4.40/天（省 $7,900/年，83%），α=0.80 时降至近零；CLINC150 完全替代教师后成本归零。产物检视：全量卸载时所有 157 类切片处理率 100%、无边界对（符合路由状态的正确行为）；部分卸载时各类处理率从 78.2%（card_payment_not_recognised）到 96.4%（transaction_charged_twice），长文本处理率降至 88.3%，边界对语义可解释（处理侧 0.94–0.96，回退侧 0.00）；MNLI 无产物生成本身即说明无可行边界。

Conclusion: 结论是：LLM 应被理解为「标注引擎」，其最有价值的产物是每次调用产生的训练信号。TRACER 用「一致性门 + 飞轮 + 边界可解释产物」把这一洞察落地为可运行系统：Banking77 上 α 作为旋钮在 100%（α=0.80）到 83.2%（α=0.95）之间调节覆盖与质量；CLINC150（157 类）在所有质量目标下代理完全替代教师；MNLI 上门控正确拒绝部署，体现「安全内建」。作者强调 TRACER 不主张优于教师，而是以零边际推理成本「匹配」教师，并指出被回退的难样本天然构成最有价值的标注集，可反向支撑主动标注。局限与后续：CLINC150 的校准—测试差距（建议保守设 α 或引入 conformal 校准）；仅用单一 BGE 编码器，未做编码器对比；基线仅为最简单的置信度阈值回退，未与 RouteLLM、蒸馏等强方法比较；两个正向基准均为意图分类，噪声标签、长文本、多标签、组合推理任务需在累积 trace 上微调编码器；XAI 产物仅做定性检视，缺用户研究；每次更新从头重拟合而非增量更新，规模化时计算成本高。

Abstract: Every call to an LLM classification endpoint produces a labeled input-output pair already retained in production logs. These pairs constitute a free, growing training set: a lightweight surrogate trained on them can absorb a significant portion of future traffic at near-zero marginal inference cost. The open questions are when the surrogate is reliable enough to deploy, what it handles versus defers, and how that boundary evolves as data accumulates.
  We introduce TRACER (Trace-based Adaptive Cost-Efficient Routing), an open-source system that trains ML surrogates on an LLM's own production traces and governs deployment through a parity gate: the surrogate is activated only when its agreement with the LLM exceeds a user-specified threshold α. To make the routing boundary transparent, TRACER generates interpretability artifacts describing which input regions the surrogate handles, where it plateaus, and why it defers.
  On a 77-class intent benchmark with a Sonnet 4.6 teacher, TRACER achieves 83-100% surrogate coverage depending on the quality target α; on a 150-class benchmark, the surrogate fully replaces the teacher. On a natural language inference task, the parity gate correctly refuses deployment because the embedding representation cannot support reliable separation. The system is available as open-source software.

</details>


### [115] [MARS$^2$: Scaling Multi-Agent Tree Search via Reinforcement Learning for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14564)
*Pengfei Li,Shijie Wang,Fangyuan Li,Yikun Fu,Kaifeng Liu,Kaiyan Zhang,Dazhi Zhang,Yuqiang Li,Biqing Qi,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARS²提出将多智能体协作与树搜索结合的统一强化学习框架，通过将搜索树建模为可学习的多智能体交互环境，解决单智能体RL在推理密集型任务中轨迹多样性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习在代码生成等推理密集型任务中表现受限，主要原因是：(1)单智能体策略先验约束了探索空间，导致轨迹多样性不足；(2)现有搜索增强方法虽引入结构化探索，但仍受单智能体限制；(3)多智能体方法虽能获取更多样化探索信号，但通常与结构化搜索解耦。这些问题共同导致性能提升出现瓶颈。

Method: MARS²框架包含三个核心设计：(1)共享树结构搜索环境：将搜索树建模为可学习的多智能体交互环境，支持异构智能体在统一拓扑中协作生成和优化候选解；(2)路径级群体优势函数：基于树一致性 reward shaping的路径级优势 formulation，实现复杂搜索轨迹间的有效信用分配；(3)独立优化但协作搜索的机制：多个独立优化的智能体在共享搜索拓扑中协同工作。

Result: 在代码生成基准测试上的实验表明，MARS²在多样化的模型组合和训练设置下均实现一致的性能提升，证明了多智能体协作与树搜索结合对增强强化学习的有效性。

Conclusion: MARS²成功将多智能体协作与树搜索统一于单一RL框架，路径级信用分配机制有效解决了复杂搜索轨迹中的奖励分配问题。该工作为提升强化学习在推理密集型任务中的探索能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) paradigms have demonstrated strong performance on reasoning-intensive tasks such as code generation. However, limited trajectory diversity often leads to diminishing returns, which constrains the achievable performance ceiling. Search-enhanced RL alleviates this issue by introducing structured exploration, which remains constrained by the single-agent policy priors. Meanwhile, leveraging multiple interacting policies can acquire more diverse exploratory signals, but existing approaches are typically decoupled from structured search. We propose \textbf{MARS$^2$} (Multi-Agent Reinforced Tree-Search Scaling), a unified RL framework in which multiple independently-optimized agents collaborate within a shared tree-structured search environment. MARS$^2$ models the search tree as a learnable multi-agent interaction environment, enabling heterogeneous agents to collaboratively generate and refine candidate solutions within a shared search topology. To support effective learning, we introduce a path-level group advantage formulation based on tree-consistent reward shaping, which facilitates effective credit assignment across complex search trajectories. Experiments on code generation benchmarks show that MARS$^2$ consistently improves performance across diverse model combinations and training settings, demonstrating the effectiveness of coupling multi-agent collaboration with tree search for enhancing reinforcement learning. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/TsinghuaC3I/MARTI.

</details>


### [116] [Enhancing Mental Health Counseling Support in Bangladesh using Culturally-Grounded Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14576)
*Md Arid Hasan,Azhagu Meena SP,Aditya Khan,Abu Md Akteruzzaman Bhuiyan,Helal Uddin Ahmed,Joysree Debi,Farig Sadeque,Annie En-Shiun Lee,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究比较了检索增强生成（RAG）与知识图谱（KG）两种方法，用于将临床验证知识整合到LLM中以提升心理咨询质量，结果表明KG方法在上下文相关性、临床适当性和实用性方面均优于RAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在心理健康与咨询应用中生成的响应普遍缺乏文化敏感性、上下文基础和临床适当指导，需要系统性地将领域特定临床验证知识融入LLM以弥合这一差距。

Method: 研究构建了经临床验证的手动知识图谱，捕捉压力源、干预措施与结果之间的因果关系，由多学科团队贡献。采用BERTScore F1、SBERT余弦相似度及五项指标的人工评估，对比评估了多种LLM在RAG与KG两种设置下的表现。

Result: KG方法在上下文相关性、临床适当性和实际可用性方面始终优于单纯的RAG方法，证明了结构化、专家验证的知识在解决LLM咨询任务局限性中的关键作用。

Conclusion: 结构化专家验证知识对改善LLM心理咨询能力至关重要，KG方法能够有效弥补表面相似度评估无法捕捉的深层咨询质量缺陷。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise in generating supportive responses for mental health and counseling applications. However, their responses often lack cultural sensitivity, contextual grounding, and clinically appropriate guidance. This work addresses the gap of how to systematically incorporate domain-specific, clinically validated knowledge into LLMs to improve counseling quality. We utilize and compare two approaches, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and a knowledge graph (KG)-based method, designed to support para-counselors. Our KG is constructed manually and clinically validated, capturing causal relationships between stressors, interventions, and outcomes, with contributions from multidisciplinary people. We evaluated multiple LLMs in both settings using BERTScore F1 and SBERT cosine similarity, as well as human evaluation across five metrics, which is designed to directly measure the effectiveness of counseling beyond similarity at the surface level. The results show that KG-based approaches consistently improve contextual relevance, clinical appropriateness, and practical usability compared to RAG alone, demonstrating that structured, expert-validated knowledge plays a critical role in addressing LLMs limitations in counseling tasks.

</details>


### [117] [Prompt Optimization Is a Coin Flip: Diagnosing When It Helps in Compound AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14585)
*Xing Zhang,Guanghui Wang,Yanwei Cui,Wei Qiu,Ziyuan Li,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在复合 AI 系统（多智能体流水线）中，端到端提示词优化（TextGrad、DSPy、GPTSwarm）的效果"统计上与抛硬币无异"：Claude Haiku 4.5 上 72 次优化运行中 49% 低于零样本，Nova Lite 上更差。作者用 18,000 次网格评测 + 144 次优化运行检验其两大隐含假设，结果显示智能体间交互效应从不显著（p>0.52、全部 F<1.0），联合优化不必要；单个提示词优化仅在任务存在"可利用输出结构"（模型会但默认不输出的 JSON/格式化结构）时才有效（HelpSteer2 上六种方法全部超越零样本，最佳 +6.8 分）。最终提出两阶段诊断协议：约 $80 的 ANOVA 耦合预检 + 约 $5、10 分钟的 headroom 测试，把"抛硬币"变成有依据的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端提示词优化工具（TextGrad、DSPy、GPTSwarm、Helix）隐含两个从未被实证检验的假设：假设A（耦合）——智能体提示词相互影响，因此必须联合优化；假设B（值得优化）——单个智能体提示词的改变能在现实预算下显著影响系统输出。现有基准比较还存在任务/预算不一致、缺乏等算力对比、忽视模型特异性等问题。实际观察显示优化结果高度不稳定（近半数运行劣于零样本），且专家对耦合强度的先验预测被证明是错的（预期耦合最强的 HotpotQA 反而交互最小）。因此需要一个可证伪、可复用的诊断方法，回答"何时优化才有价值"以及"是否真的需要昂贵的联合优化"。

Method: 研究一（测假设A）：构造 Agent A→Agent B 两级前馈流水线，每智能体生成 K=10 个候选系统提示词，穷举 10×10=100 种组合，每格 n=30 个样本，构成 18,000 次网格评测；采用带问题区组的两因素 ANOVA 将总方差分解为问题难度、A 主效应、B 主效应、A×B 交互、残差五项；执行模型为 Claude Haiku 4.5 与 Amazon Nova Lite，Claude Sonnet 4.6 担任裁判；任务为 HotpotQA、MBPP、XSum。另做减去行列效应后的残差景观邻点自相关分析，以及 1,000 次预算等同模拟对比联合搜索与独立搜索；并对交互项 df=81、主效应 df=9 做自由度校正解释。研究二（测假设B）：六种单智能体优化方法（APE、OPRO、EvoPrompt、PromptBreeder、DSPy-style bootstrap、自研 PROSE）与零样本/人工基线对比，等算力（各评估约 100 个候选提示词），4 任务（Feedback-Bench、HelpSteer2、WildBench、XSum），训练 20 题、测试 100 题、每条件 3 次重复、两个执行模型，共 144 次优化运行；分析 train-test 过拟合差、方差来源（"可利用输出结构"）并据此提出 headroom 测试。

Result: 1) 耦合不存在：六个模型×任务条件下 A×B 交互项全部不显著（p>0.52、F<1.0），仅占方差 0.18–2.15%，其均方不超过残差均方；问题难度解释 19–91% 方差，远超任何提示词效应。分数矩阵呈纯行列条带、无对角协同，联合最优与独立最优差距仅 0.0–3.3 分，预算等同模拟显示独立搜索在各预算下均匹敌联合搜索；残差邻点自相关 ρ∈[−0.12,+0.05]，说明交互景观是随机噪声，梯度类方法（TextGrad）无可传播的平滑信号。2) 优化成败如抛硬币：Haiku 上 72 次运行中 49% 低于零样本（二项 p=0.91，无法拒绝"增益对称于零"），Nova Lite 上 24 个方法×任务均值中 14 个低于零样本；FB/WB/XSum 平均增益为负（−0.20/−0.82/−0.17），迭代方法存在最高 +5.6 分的 train-test 过拟合而非迭代的 APE 无此问题。3) 唯一成功条件是"会但默认不输出"：HelpSteer2 需基于评分细则的 JSON 结构化输出，模型能生成却不默认生成，六种方法在 Haiku 上全部超越零样本（最佳 EvoPrompt +6.8），其余任务最佳增益仅 +1.1/+0.7/+0.6，落在噪声底之内。4) 模型特异性主导：瓶颈智能体随模型翻转（Haiku 上 B 主导 HotpotQA，Nova 上 A 主导 XSum）；HS2 在 Haiku 上 6/6 成功而在 Nova 仅 1/6，FB 反向从 1/6 变为 4/6。5) 机制解释：指令微调将输入措辞压缩进窄输出分布，Agent B 的方差由语义内容而非措辞风格主导，流水线表现为"独立鲁棒函数的组合"，从而消除措辞敏感性。6) 自研 PROSE（组件级演化 + 风险感知适应度）未显示相对简单方法的稳健性优势。7) 成本对比：两阶段诊断合计约 $85、1–2 天，而 DSPy 约 $1–5K、TextGrad 约 $5–10K。

Conclusion: 提示词优化并非普遍无用，但高度有条件：联合优化在两智能体、中端模型、前馈流水线下没有必要（独立逐智能体优化足够），单个提示词优化只有在存在"可利用输出结构"（模型有能力但默认不产出的输出格式/结构）时才带来实质收益，且效果对执行模型高度敏感——优化策略的保质期短于模型迭代周期，需作为经常性支出重复执行。本文的核心贡献不是负面结论，而是方法论：ANOVA 方差分解提供了可证伪的"组件是否可组合"检验协议（交互 F>1 则需联合优化，F<1 则不需要），配合 headroom 测试（10–20 个候选、最佳增益 >2 分则用 APE 式生成-排序，<2 分则直接用零样本），可低成本地把随机性决策转为有依据的决策，并有望推广为多智能体系统结构化评测范式。作者明确列出局限：整条提示词替换的粗粒度设定、中端模型、20 题训练集、仅 XSum 跨研究重叠、单一双智能体前馈架构；共享状态、反馈回路、三级以上流水线与结构化数据通信等更可能产生耦合的场景均待检验。

Abstract: Prompt optimization in compound AI systems is statistically indistinguishable from a coin flip: across 72 optimization runs on Claude Haiku (6 methods $\times$ 4 tasks $\times$ 3 repeats), 49% score below zero-shot; on Amazon Nova Lite, the failure rate is even higher. Yet on one task, all six methods improve over zero-shot by up to $+6.8$ points. What distinguishes success from failure? We investigate with 18,000 grid evaluations and 144 optimization runs, testing two assumptions behind end-to-end optimization tools like TextGrad and DSPy: (A) individual prompts are worth optimizing, and (B) agent prompts interact, requiring joint optimization. Interaction effects are never significant ($p > 0.52$, all $F < 1.0$), and optimization helps only when the task has exploitable output structure -- a format the model can produce but does not default to. We provide a two-stage diagnostic: an \$80 ANOVA pre-test for agent coupling, and a 10-minute headroom test that predicts whether optimization is worthwhile -- turning a coin flip into an informed decision.

</details>


### [118] [GDPR Auto-Formalization with AI Agents and Human Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14607)
*Ha Thanh Nguyen,Wachara Fungwacharakorn,Sabine Wehnert,May Myo Zin,Yuntao Kong,Jieying Xue,Michał Araszkiewicz,Randy Goebel,Ken Satoh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究使用大语言模型（LLM）自动形式化GDPR条款，采用多智能体工作流配合人类验证模块，验证了结构化验证和定向人工监督对可靠法律形式化的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDPR等法规的自动形式化面临法律文本复杂性和上下文敏感性的挑战。当前完全自主化的方法难以处理法律细节，因此需要探索人机协作的方式。

Method: 采用角色专业化工作流，多智能体LLM系统通过迭代反馈生成法律场景、形式化规则和原子事实；配备独立验证模块，包括人类审查员对表示正确性、逻辑一致性和法律正确性的评估。

Result: 成功构建了高质量的GDPR自动形式化数据集，并识别了成功案例与问题案例的特征。

Conclusion: 结构化验证和针对性人工监督对于可靠的法律形式化至关重要，尤其在涉及法律细微差别和上下文敏感推理的场景中。

Abstract: We study the overall process of automatic formalization of GDPR provisions using large language models, within a human-in-the-loop verification framework. Rather than aiming for full autonomy, we adopt a role-specialized workflow in which LLM-based AI components, operating in a multi-agent setting with iterative feedback, generate legal scenarios, formal rules, and atomic facts. This is coupled with independent verification modules which include human reviewers' assessment of representational, logical, and legal correctness. Using this approach, we construct a high-quality dataset to be used for GDPR auto-formalization, and analyze both successful and problematic cases. Our results show that structured verification and targeted human oversight are essential for reliable legal formalization, especially in the presence of legal nuance and context-sensitive reasoning.

</details>


### [119] [El Agente Forjador: Task-Driven Agent Generation for Quantum Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14609)
*Zijian Zhang,Aiwei Yin,Amaan Baweja,Jiaru Bai,Ignacio Gustin,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"El Agente Forjador"多智能体框架，使通用编码智能体能够自主生成、验证和复用计算工具。通过24个量子化学和量子动力学任务的评估，证明了工具生成与复用机制相比直接问题求解显著提升准确性，且更强的智能体构建的工具可被较弱智能体复用以降低成本并提高质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体系统依赖静态、人工策划的工具集，阻碍了对新领域的适应和库的演进。这限制了AI辅助科学发现的可扩展性和通用性。

Method: 提出四阶段工作流的多智能体框架：工具分析、工具生成、任务执行和迭代解决方案评估。评估涵盖三种运行模式：逐任务零样本工具生成、课程式构建工具集的复用、以及直接问题求解基线。在24个任务上测试了5种编码智能体配置。

Result: 工具生成与复用框架始终优于基线方法；复用由强编码智能体构建的工具可降低API成本并显著提升弱编码智能体的解决方案质量；不同领域生成的工具可组合用于混合任务。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的智能体可利用其科学知识和编码能力自主构建可复用的科学工具，表明智能体能力应由其设计解决的问题定义，而非显式工程实现。这一范式有望推动AI赋能科学发现的新发展。

Abstract: AI for science promises to accelerate the discovery process. The advent of large language models (LLMs) and agentic workflows enables the expediting of a growing range of scientific tasks. However, most of the current generation of agentic systems depend on static, hand-curated toolsets that hinder adaptation to new domains and evolving libraries. We present El Agente Forjador, a multi-agent framework in which universal coding agents autonomously forge, validate, and reuse computational tools through a four-stage workflow of tool analysis, tool generation, task execution, and iterative solution evaluation. Evaluated across 24 tasks spanning quantum chemistry and quantum dynamics on five coding agent setups, we compare three operating modes: zero-shot generation of tools per task, reuse of a curriculum-built toolset, and direct problem-solving with the coding agents as the baseline. We find that our tool generation and reuse framework consistently improves accuracy over the baseline. We also show that reusing a toolset built by a stronger coding agent can reduce API cost and substantially raises the solution quality for weaker coding agents. Case studies further demonstrate that tools forged for different domains can be combined to solve hybrid tasks. Taken together, these results show that LLM-based agents can use their scientific knowledge and coding capabilities to autonomously build reusable scientific tools, pointing toward a paradigm in which agent capabilities are defined by the tasks they are designed to solve rather than by explicitly engineered implementations.

</details>


### [120] [CoDaS: AI Co-Data-Scientist for Biomarker Discovery via Wearable Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14615)
*Yubin Kim,Salman Rahman,Samuel Schmidgall,Chunjong Park,A. Ali Heydari,Ahmed A. Metwally,Hong Yu,Xin Liu,Xuhai Xu,Yuzhe Yang,Maxwell A. Xu,Zhihan Zhang,Cynthia Breazeal,Tim Althoff,Petar Sirkovic,Ivor Rendulic,Annalisa Pawlosky,Nicolas Stroppa,Juraj Gottweis,Elahe Vedadi,Alan Karthikesalingam,Pushmeet Kohli,Vivek Natarajan,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Hae Won Park,Hamid Palangi,Daniel McDuff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoDaS是一个多智能体AI系统，用于从可穿戴设备数据中发现生物标志物。该系统在9,279名参与者中识别出41个心理健康和25个代谢结局的候选数字生物标志物，发现睡眠节律不稳定与抑郁相关，心血管健身指数与代谢健康相关，并将这些特征纳入预测模型取得小幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scientific discovery in digital health requires converting continuous physiological signals from wearable devices into clinically actionable biomarkers, but this process lacks systematic, traceable methods.

Method: Multi-agent AI system (CoDaS) combining hypothesis generation, statistical analysis, adversarial validation, and literature-grounded reasoning with human oversight, applied to large-scale wearable datasets across three cohorts (9,279 participant-observations).

Result: Identified 41 candidate digital biomarkers for mental health and 25 for metabolic outcomes; validated circadian instability features (sleep duration/onset variability) for depression across cohorts; derived cardiovascular fitness index (steps/resting heart rate) and recovered hepatic function ratio (AST/ALT) as known insulin resistance correlate.

Conclusion: CoDaS enables systematic and traceable hypothesis generation and prioritization for biomarker discovery from large-scale wearable data. The system identified circadian instability features related to depression and a cardiovascular fitness index for metabolic outcomes, with modest but consistent improvements in predictive performance (ΔR² = 0.040 for depression, ΔR² = 0.021 for insulin resistance).

Abstract: Scientific discovery in digital health requires converting continuous physiological signals from wearable devices into clinically actionable biomarkers. We introduce CoDaS (AI Co-Data-Scientist), a multi-agent system that structures biomarker discovery as an iterative process combining hypothesis generation, statistical analysis, adversarial validation, and literature-grounded reasoning with human oversight using large-scale wearable datasets. Across three cohorts totaling 9,279 participant-observations, CoDaS identified 41 candidate digital biomarkers for mental health and 25 for metabolic outcomes, each subjected to an internal validation battery spanning replication, stability, robustness, and discriminative power. Across two independent depression cohorts, CoDaS surfaced circadian instability-related features in both datasets, reflected in sleep duration variability (DWB, ρ= 0.252, p < 0.001) and sleep onset variability (GLOBEM, ρ= 0.126, p < 0.001). In a metabolic cohort, CoDaS derived a cardiovascular fitness index (steps/resting heart rate; ρ= -0.374, p < 0.001), and recovered established clinical associations, including the hepatic function ratio (AST/ALT; ρ= -0.375, p < 0.001), a known correlate of insulin resistance. Incorporating CoDaS-derived features alongside demographic variables led to modest but consistent improvements in predictive performance, with cross-validated ΔR^2 increases of 0.040 for depression and 0.021 for insulin resistance. These findings suggest that CoDaS enables systematic and traceable hypothesis generation and prioritization for biomarker discovery from large-scale wearable data.

</details>


### [121] [A Parallel Approach to Counting Exact Covers Based on Decomposability Property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14627)
*Liangda Fang,Yaohui Luo,Delong Li,Xuanxiang Huang,Quanlong Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对NP难问题——精确覆盖问题，提出比ZBDD更紧凑的决策ZDNNF表示方法，并设计并行算法DXD通过动态更新连通分量来构造所有精确覆盖的表示，实验表明该算法优于现有最佳方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确覆盖问题是经典的NP难问题，具有广泛的AI应用场景。现有基于ZBDD（零抑制二元决策图）的DXZ算法在表示效率上存在局限性，需要更紧凑的数据结构来表示所有精确覆盖解。

Method: 1) 提出decision-ZDNNF（决策零抑制分解否定范式），这是ZDNNF的变体，比ZBDD严格更紧凑；2) 设计并行算法DXD构建decision-ZDNNF表示所有精确覆盖；3) 通过动态更新连通分量进一步优化DXD算法。

Result: decision-ZDNNF在表达能力上严格强于ZBDD；改进的DXD算法在性能上优于所有现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 决策ZDNNF是比ZBDD更有效的精确覆盖表示结构，动态更新连通分量的优化策略显著提升了并行算法的效率，为精确覆盖问题的求解提供了更优方案。

Abstract: The exact cover problem is a classical NP-hard problem with broad applications in the area of AI. Algorithm DXZ is a method to count exact covers representing by zero-suppressed binary decision diagrams (ZBDDs). In this paper, we propose a zero-suppressed variant of decision decomposable negation normal form (in short, decision-ZDNNF), which is strictly more succinct than ZBDDs. We then design a novel parallel algorithm, namely DXD, which constructs a decision-ZDNNF representing the set of all exact covers. Furthermore, we improve DXD by dynamically updating connected components. The experimental results demonstrate that the improved DXD algorithm outperforms all of state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [122] [Learning to Draw ASCII Improves Spatial Reasoning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14641)
*Shiyuan Huang,Li Liu,Jincheng He,Leilani H. Gilpin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM学习构建ASCII布局能否提升空间理解能力。发现LLM存在"读写不对称"现象：解读ASCII布局能力强，但生成能力弱。通过在Text2Space数据集上训练布局构建任务，发现学习生成布局可显著提升空间推理能力，且该能力可泛化到外部基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过绘图组织思维，绘图行为本身能加深理解。本研究探索LLM是否也遵循类似原则——学习从空间描述构建显式视觉布局能否真正提升空间理解能力。核心问题是：区分空间表征构建失败与空间推理失败这两种不同类型的错误。

Method: 构建Text2Space数据集，将自然语言描述与真值ASCII网格布局及空间问答配对。ASCII具有人类可读、完全处于语言模型token空间、结构可验证等优势。实验对比LLM解读与生成ASCII的能力，并在Text→ASCII构建任务上微调模型，探索构建训练与理解训练的结合效果。

Result: 发现显著的"读写不对称"现象：LLM解读ASCII能力强，但生成能力弱，且生成错误会传播导致推理错误。关键发现是：在Text→ASCII任务上训练，即使推理时不输出ASCII，仅训练构建能力就能显著提升纯文本空间推理。结合构建与理解训练可进一步放大收益，且改进可迁移到三个外部空间推理基准。

Conclusion: 与人类素描强化空间思维类似，学习构建显式布局能够赋予LLM可泛化的空间理解能力，且该能力超越训练格式本身。这一发现表明，空间表征的"生成"过程本身可能对认知理解具有重要价值。

Abstract: When faced with complex spatial problems, humans naturally sketch layouts to organize their thinking, and the act of drawing further sharpens their understanding. In this work, we ask whether a similar principle holds for Large Language Models (LLMs): can learning to construct explicit visual layouts from spatial descriptions instill genuine spatial understanding? We introduce Text2Space, a dataset that pairs natural language descriptions with ground-truth ASCII grid layouts and spatial QA pairs, enabling us to separate failures in constructing spatial representations from failures in reasoning over them. We adopt ASCII because it is human-readable, operates entirely within the token space of language models, and encodes spatial relations in a structurally verifiable form. Our evaluation reveals a pronounced "Read-Write Asymmetry": LLMs interpret ASCII representations effectively but struggle to produce them from text, and these construction errors propagate to incorrect answers downstream. To address this limitation, we train models on layout construction (Text$\rightarrow$ASCII) and find that it significantly improves spatial reasoning from text alone, even without producing any ASCII at inference time. Combining construction with comprehension training further amplifies these gains. Crucially, these improvements transfer to three external spatial reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating that, much as sketching sharpens human spatial thinking, learning to construct explicit layouts instills spatial understanding that generalizes beyond the training format.

</details>


### [123] [Targeted Exploration via Unified Entropy Control for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14646)
*Chen Wang,Lai Wei,Yanzhi Zhang,Chenyang Shao,Zedong Dan,Weiran Huang,Ge Lan,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UEC-RL框架解决强化学习中的熵崩溃问题，通过对困难样本增强探索、对收敛过程稳定控制，在LLM和VLM推理任务上取得显著提升，在Geometry3K上比GRPO相对提升37.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO方法存在熵崩溃问题，导致策略过早收敛并丧失多样性；现有探索方法引入额外偏差或方差，难以保持优化稳定性。

Method: UEC-RL包含两个核心机制：1) 探索激活器——对困难prompt增强探索以发现潜在有价值的推理轨迹；2) 稳定器——防止熵无限增长，保持训练稳定。

Result: 在LLM和VLM推理任务上，UEC-RL在Pass@1和Pass@k指标上均优于RL基线；在Geometry3K上比GRPO相对提升37.9%。

Conclusion: UEC-RL能够在需要时扩展搜索空间，同时保持稳健的优化过程，是大规模模型RL推理扩展的关键技术。

Abstract: Recent advances in reinforcement learning (RL) have improved the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs). However, the widely used Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) consistently suffers from entropy collapse, causing the policy to converge prematurely and lose diversity. Existing exploration methods introduce additional bias or variance during exploration, making it difficult to maintain optimization stability. We propose Unified Entropy Control for Reinforcement Learning (UEC-RL), a framework that provides targeted mechanisms for exploration and stabilization. UEC-RL activates more exploration on difficult prompts to search for potential and valuable reasoning trajectories. In parallel, a stabilizer prevents entropy from growing uncontrollably, thereby keeping training stable as the model consolidates reliable behaviors. Together, these components expand the search space when needed while maintaining robust optimization throughout training. Experiments on both LLM and VLM reasoning tasks show consistent gains over RL baselines on both Pass@1 and Pass@$k$. On Geometry3K, UEC-RL achieves a 37.9\% relative improvement over GRPO, indicating that it sustains effective exploration without compromising convergence and underscoring UEC-RL as a key for scaling RL-based reasoning in large models. Our code is available at https://github.com/597358816/UEC-RL.

</details>


### [124] [AgentGA: Evolving Code Solutions in Agent-Seed Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14655)
*David Y. Y. Tan,Kellie Chin,Jingxian Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentGA 提出"智能体种子空间"（agent-seed space）搜索范式：不直接修改代码产物，而是进化每次全新自主运行的初始状态（任务提示 + 可选父代归档），由种群级遗传算法在进化层决定继承与选择，LangGraph 长程智能体在隔离工作区中自行决策。Weco-Kaggle Lite 16 项竞赛上平均 Exceeds % of Human 达 71.90%（AIDE 为 51.38%），15/16 场胜出；在 1680 场父子锦标赛中，带父代归档的子代胜率 51.9%，而从零开始的 Initial 仅 8.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代码搜索系统分为三类：产物中心（FunSearch、ReEvo、AlphaEvolve、AIDE，优化提示/启发式/代码片段）、有界智能体（DS-Agent、AutoKaggle、SE-Agent，在固定脚手架内优化任务执行）、自主智能体（autoresearch、Agent K，单谱系开放式求解）。作者指出尚无工作同时做到：种群级进化 + 自主工作区智能体 + 以"新一次自主运行的初始状态"为优化对象。已有自主系统均为单谱系（单一工作区持续修改），而产物中心系统虽优化产物却不携带过程性知识。动机即：如何在每次运行都重置工作区（避免工作区无限膨胀）的同时，仍把先前运行的"执行先验"（哪些方向有希望、哪些失败值得规避）有效传递给后代。

Method: 1) 形式化：设解空间 S、方向归一化适应度 h；优化对象为智能体种子 C，种子 c0 = (prompt(task_type, context), {parent_archive_j})，求解 argmax E_{τ~Agent(c0)}[h(s_τ)]——同一种子可导出不同轨迹，故优化的是随机的"种子→解"映射而非确定性编辑路径。2) 两层架构：进化层维护 n=5 的精英池，每代每个槽位由采样到的任务算子与所选父代档案生成一个子代；子代仅与上一代同槽位精英进行确定性 1:1 锦标赛（方向由指标决定），保留并行谱系并直接度量继承是否有效。智能体层为 LangGraph 扩展 ReAct 循环，含 Planning、Agent、Tools、Compression、Verification、Finalize 六节点；父代材料经策展的 Previous Experiments/ 目录（非递归、剔除重型中间产物）放入工作区，但不强制注入上下文，智能体可选择性查阅、延后使用或完全忽略。3) 六个任务型遗传算子替代经典交叉/变异：Initial(n_p=0)、Continue(n_p≥1)、Ablation(n_p=1，逐组件增删以隔离因果贡献)、Merge(n_p=2，双亲组件组合)、EDA(n_p=1，先析数据再提特征/建模改动)、Jumpstart(n_p=1+外部参考，文中因 GA_PROB_JUMPSTART=0 未启用）；每次调用配置 5 次实验预算。4) 修改版 Hedge 在线分配算子概率：以方向感知的父子改进 Δ_j 为奖励（正值恒代表子胜过父），观察算子少于两个则跳过更新，均值转秩以抑制量纲影响，重要性加权截断 κ=4.0，学习率 η=0.15，softmax 后强制概率上下限。5) 停止策略：最大 30 轮或最优精英分连续 5 轮无严格改进（patience=5）。工程实现分为两个仓库：agentga-ml_geneticalgorithm（数据集无关的进化层）与 agentga-ml_workflow（领域工作流层，打包为按数据集打标的 Docker 镜像），每轮每槽位启动一个容器跑 train-agent.py；四个可热替换目录（数据加载、结果无关特征工程、结果相关特征工程、模型）供智能体编辑。

Result: 主结果：16 项 Weco-Kaggle Lite 竞赛全部报告，AgentGA 平均 Exceeds % of Human = 71.90%，AIDE 参考为 51.38%，提升 +20.52 pp，15/16 场胜出（唯一负例 cat-in-the-dat，-3.73 pp），单侧二项符号检验 p=2.59×10⁻⁴。单场最佳：playground-series-s3e22 排名第 263/1543（Exceeds 82.96%，AIDE 为 1142 名）。相对基线表：AutoGPT(LangChain)+GPT-4 Turbo 32.34%、H2O AutoML 35.34%、Human+ChatGPT 41.17%、AIDE 51.38%、AgentGA(Kimi K2.5) 71.90%。与 autoresearch（Claude Opus 4.7 xhigh）两场正面对比（bike-sharing-demand、TPS Aug 2022）：AgentGA 90.73% vs autoresearch 84.43% vs AIDE 85.58%，且 scored evaluations 更少（750/375 vs 1500/1000）。继承有效性（内部锦标赛）：1680 场父子对局中，父代条件化算子整体胜率 51.9%、中位相对增益非负；Initial 从零开始仅胜 8.6%（11/128）、中位增益 -2.07%；分算子看 Merge 72%（167/232）、Continue 51%（201/391）、EDA 47%（381/807）、Ablation 47%（57/122）。计算成本：共 352 轮迭代、8800 次 scored evaluations；单次智能体调用 56 步、15 分 10 秒、1.87M prompt tokens（含 1.56M 缓存）、34252 completion tokens、产出 5 次计分实验；4 台 Threadripper 7960X 工作站纯 CPU、无 GPU。案例研究（tabular-playground-series-aug-2022，15 轮清晰迭代）展示了探索—回退—重组的非单调搜索动态。

Conclusion: 作者主张：自主系统可以在比"提示"或"代码补丁"更粗的粒度上优化，被继承的是一种"执行先验"（哪些方向已被看好、哪些失败应规避、哪些产物值得优先关注），它足够结构化以改善后代，又足够松散以保留运行内自主性；因此 agent-seed 优化可迁移到其他具备可靠评估器的代码生成领域。系统将领域无关的进化层与领域特定的工作流层解耦，新增领域只需新工作流镜像。主要局限：长程智能体的算力/时延成本高；种子质量仅通过随机后代间接观测（轨迹依赖）；依赖有界可自动化的适应度信号与领域工作流层；未做完整析因消融，Hedge 分配、种群规模、档案继承、任务模板、工作流层的边际贡献未在同等算力下隔离；AIDE 对比非模型/脚手架匹配（GPT-4 Turbo vs Kimi K2.5，产物 MCTS vs 长程智能体），故因果主张依赖方法内锦标赛分析而非表 1；Exceeds % of Human 为排名指标而非校准效应量；基准来自公开 Kaggle 竞赛，存在训练数据污染与排行榜追逐风险（作者建议部署时使用沙箱工具、审计日志、预算上限与任务规则）。另需注意：摘要版本称"10 次基准运行、1135 次父子对比"，而正文为 16 场竞赛、1680 次锦标赛，两者存在版本不一致。

Abstract: We present AgentGA, a framework that evolves autonomous code-generation runs by optimizing the agent seed: the task prompt plus optional parent archives that initialize a fresh workspace. The outer loop searches over these reusable starting conditions rather than editing code directly. Each generation launches a fresh autonomous run from a reset workspace, while selected parent archives provide inherited artifacts that descendants can inspect and reuse. AgentGA couples a population-level genetic algorithm with long-horizon agents; selection uses deterministic 1:1 elite tournaments and operator allocation is adapted online with a modified Hedge controller. We instantiate the approach for tabular AutoML on the 16-competition Weco-Kaggle Lite benchmark. On the 10 benchmark runs reported here, AgentGA averages 74.52% Exceeds % of Human versus 54.15% for AIDE. Across 1135 parent-child comparisons, descendants given parent archives outperform runs started from scratch, indicating that inherited artifacts improve later autonomous runs. These findings support agent-seed optimization as a practical design point for autonomous code-search systems.

</details>


### [125] [Rethinking Patient Education as Multi-turn Multi-modal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14656)
*Zonghai Yao,Zhipeng Tang,Chengtao Lin,Xiong Luo,Benlu Wang,Juncheng Huang,Chin Siang Ong,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedImageEdu是一个用于评估多轮、证据驱动的放射学患者教育多模态AI智能体的新基准。该基准包含150个病例，通过DoctorAgent与PatientAgent的多轮交互生成带图像的多模态响应，并从5个维度评估：咨询质量、安全性、语言质量、绘图质量和图文响应质量。研究发现当前模型在视觉基础、对话安全性和情绪交互处理方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学多模态基准主要关注静态任务（如图像问答、报告生成），而患者教育需要更复杂的能力：跨图像识别相关证据、向患者指示关注点、用通俗语言解释发现、处理困惑或情绪困扰。然而大多数患者教育工作仍是纯文本的，尽管图文结合的解释可能更好地支持理解。

Method: MedImageEdu采用DoctorAgent与PatientAgent多轮交互架构。系统提供放射学报告（含文本和图像），DoctorAgent根据隐藏的患者画像（教育水平、健康素养、人格特质）进行响应。当患者问题需要视觉支持时，DoctorAgent可向绘图工具发出基于报告、图像和当前问题的绘图指令，工具返回图像后，DoctorAgent生成包含图像和通俗语言解释的多模态响应。

Result: 对多个开源和闭源视觉-语言模型智能体的评估揭示了三个一致性问题：(1)流畅的语言输出往往超越忠实视觉基础能力；(2)安全性是跨疾病类别中最弱的维度；(3)情绪紧张的交互比低教育或低健康素养的交互更难处理。

Conclusion: MedImageEdu提供了一个受控测试平台，用于评估多模态智能体是否能从证据教学而非仅从文本回答问题，为医学教育场景下AI系统的多模态理解和生成能力提供了标准化评估框架。

Abstract: Most medical multimodal benchmarks focus on static tasks such as image question answering, report generation, and plain-language rewriting. Patient education is more demanding: systems must identify relevant evidence across images, show patients where to look, explain findings in accessible language, and handle confusion or distress. Yet most patient education work remains text-only, even though combined image-and-text explanations may better support understanding. We introduce MedImageEdu, a benchmark for multi-turn, evidence-grounded radiology patient education. Each case provides a radiology report with report text and case images. A DoctorAgent interacts with a PatientAgent, conditioned on a hidden profile that captures factors such as education level, health literacy, and personality. When a patient question would benefit from visual support, the DoctorAgent can issue drawing instructions grounded in the report, case images, and the current question to a benchmark-provided drawing tool. The tool returns image(s), after which the DoctorAgent produces a final multimodal response consisting of the image(s) and a grounded plain-language explanation. MedImageEdu contains 150 cases from three sources and evaluates both the consultation process and the final multimodal response along five dimensions: Consultation, Safety and Scope, Language Quality, Drawing Quality, and Image-Text Response Quality. Across representative open- and closed-source vision-language model agents, we find three consistent gaps: fluent language often outpaces faithful visual grounding, safety is the weakest dimension across disease categories, and emotionally tense interactions are harder than low education or low health literacy. MedImageEdu provides a controlled testbed for assessing whether multimodal agents can teach from evidence rather than merely answer from text.

</details>


### [126] [Acceptance Dynamics Across Cognitive Domains in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14682)
*Saif Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一项关于树状投机解码（speculative decoding）接受率的经验研究，探索任务认知特性如何影响接受概率。研究使用TinyLlama-1.1B作为草稿模型，Llama-2-7B-Chat-GPTQ作为目标模型，在代码生成、数学推理、逻辑推理和开放域聊天四个NLP基准领域收集99,768个投机节点，发现任务类型比树深度更能预测接受率，且仅聊天领域平均每步接受长度超过1.0个token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码虽被广泛研究，但任务认知特性如何影响接受概率尚缺乏系统探索。现有工作主要关注解码加速技术本身，而忽视了不同任务域对接受动态的差异化影响。

Method: 使用TinyLlama-1.1B（草稿模型）与Llama-2-7B-Chat-GPTQ（目标模型）构建树状投机解码系统；通过200个prompt在四个NLP领域（代码生成、数学推理、逻辑推理、开放域聊天）收集99,768个投机节点；分析指标包括：领域接受率、期望接受长度、深度-接受率曲线、熵-接受率相关性。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 任务类型是比树深度更强的接受率预测因子；(2) 仅聊天领域平均每步接受长度超过1.0 token；(3) 熵-接受率相关性在所有领域均为弱负相关（ρ ∈ [-0.20, -0.15]）；(4) 反直觉地，聊天领域产生最高熵却也呈现最高接受率，归因于RLHF对齐语域的词汇可预测性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了任务域对投机解码接受动态的显著影响，为领域感知的投机预算分配和草稿模型选择策略提供了直接指导。聊天任务的特殊性表明，RLHF对齐可能通过提高词汇层面的可预测性来优化投机解码效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) inference. It uses a small draft model to propose a tree of future tokens. A larger target model then verifies these tokens in a single batched forward pass. Despite the growing body of work on speculative methods, the degree to which the cognitive characteristics of a task affect acceptance probability remains largely unexplored. We present an empirical study of tree-based speculative decoding acceptance dynamics. Our study spans four well-established NLP benchmark domains: code generation, mathematical reasoning, logical reasoning, and open-ended chat. For this, we use TinyLlama-1.1B as the draft model against Llama-2-7B-Chat-GPTQ as the target. Over 99,768 speculative nodes collected from 200 prompts, we derive per-domain acceptance rates, expected accepted lengths, depth-acceptance profiles, and entropy-acceptance correlations. We find that task type is a stronger predictor of acceptance than tree depth. Furthermore, only the chat domain consistently yields an expected accepted length exceeding 1.0 token per step. We also show that the entropy-acceptance correlation is consistently negative but weak across all domains (rho in [-0.20, -0.15]). Counterintuitively, chat produces the highest entropy yet the highest acceptance rate. We attribute this divergence to the lexical predictability of RLHF-aligned register. These findings have direct implications for domain-aware speculation budgets and draft-model selection strategies. Index Terms--speculative decoding, large language model inference, tree attention, draft model, acceptance probability, LLM efficiency

</details>


### [127] [DR$^{3}$-Eval: Towards Realistic and Reproducible Deep Research Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14683)
*Qianqian Xie,Qingheng Xiong,He Zhu,Tiantian Xia,Xueming Han,Fanyu Meng,Jiakai Wang,Zhiqi Bai,Chengkang Jiang,Zhaohui Wang,Yubin Guo,Yuqing Wen,Jiayang Mao,Zijie Zhang,Shihao Li,Yanghai Wang,Yuxiang Ren,Junlan Feng,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DR³-Eval 是一个面向深度研究智能体（DRA）的真实、可复现、多模态评测基准：任务由真实用户提供的多模态文件反向构造，并为每个任务配套一个包含支持性文档、干扰文档与噪声的静态沙盒语料（32k–512k tokens 五档），配套五维评测指标（IR、FA、CC、IF、DQ）与多智能体系统 DR³-Agent。作者在 GPT-4.1、Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini-2.5-Pro、GLM-4.6/4.7、Qwen3 系列上实验，显示该基准难度很高，随上下文增长性能普遍下降，且幻觉是最主要的失败原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体（DRA）需要完成规划、迭代检索、多模态理解与长篇报告生成，但现有评测存在"真实性—可控性—可评性"的根本张力：依赖实时网络的基准（DeepResearch Bench、LiveResearch Bench、DEER 等）随时间波动、结果不可复现且答案可解性模糊；沙盒/本地语料基准（DeepResearchGym、BrowseComp-Plus）虽稳定但任务多为纯文本查询、缺少真实用户研究材料；DRBench 虽引入用户文件却依赖实时搜索、聚焦企业域且未显式建模噪声与误导信息。同时，任务从"单答案"转向"报告生成"后，传统指标失效，需要与人类判断对齐的 LLM-as-a-judge 细粒度评估。因此需要一个同时具备多模态真实用户材料、噪声密集但静态可验证的沙盒环境、以及唯一可验证解答路径的基准。

Method: 数据构建五阶段：（1）真实需求落地——招募付费志愿者提供 100 组内在相关材料（文本、结构化数据、静态图像、动态音视频），中英各 50 组，覆盖技术、经济、人文三大域 13 个子域，并经自动 PII 脱敏 + 人工交叉复核；（2）搜索路径蒸馏——用 Gemini-2.5-Pro 做"发散-收敛"两阶段生成 10 个候选关键词，再划分为 Signal Keywords（指向正确解路径）与 Noise Keywords（主题相关但导向无关/误导信息）；（3）构建每任务独立静态沙盒——按关键词各检索至多 100 条网页，去重、统一爬取清洗后人工标注为支持性网页、干扰性网页（过时/片面/不准确）与噪声网页，并按 32k/64k/128k/256k/512k 五档上下文长度调节干扰与噪声占比（三类文档随机打乱混合）；（4）反向构造查询——基于预定的证据文档与 signal 关键词合成查询，保证答案唯一可验证且必须联合用户文件与网页证据才能解答；（5）四维质量控制——隐式引导（不得逐字泄露关键词）、leave-one-out 合成必要性（单步公开搜索可解则剔除）、洞察新颖性（核心事实可直接检索到则剔除）、可解释唯一性人工审查；280 候选最终保留 100（通过率 35.7%）。评测框架五指标：Information Recall（IR_UF/IR_SC，由 Gemini-2.5-Flash 抽取人工核验的 insight 集合，判定 cov∈{1,0.5,0} 后计算严格全覆盖比例）、Citation Coverage（对必要文档集的引用交并比）、Factual Accuracy（claim-source 蕴含判定，文本用 GPT-5.1、音视频用 Gemini-2.5-Pro）、Instruction Following（由查询生成并人工核验的 checklist 满足率）、Depth Quality（基于 rubric 的专家评审式打分），评估器温度设为 0。系统层面提出 DR³-Agent：基于 MiroFlow 的分层多智能体架构，主智能体集成感知工具与 Python 环境、动态"Plan-Act-Observe"循环，RAG 子智能体用 text-embedding-3-small 做 ReAct 式迭代密检索，文件阅读子智能体做细粒度关键词与页码定位；主/子智能体轮次上限分别为 10/5/3。

Result: 主实验（64k/128k/512k）：Claude Sonnet 4 综合最优（512k 平均 65.6），GLM-4.7 次之（64.1），Qwen3-30B-A3B 最弱（42.8）；同族内规模效应依然成立。上下文从 64k 增至 512k 时所有模型性能普遍下降，主要体现在 IR_SC 与 CC，FA 相对稳定（更反映获得证据后的推理能力）。指令遵循好不等于事实准确性好（Qwen3-235B、GPT-4.1 的 IF 尚可但 FA 很低）。稳定性：10,000 次 bootstrap 显示前两名 95% 置信区间不重叠，Wilcoxon p=0.0046 显著；重复评估总分方差 0.874，Kendall τ=0.969、Spearman ρ=0.991，三次重复标准差 0.83/0.85/1.33。沙盒 vs 实时网页（英文子集，Qwen3-235B 与 Gemini-2.5-Pro）平均分差异仅 −0.3 与 +0.7，CC 一致性最高，无明显系统偏差，说明沙盒可作为可靠替代。移除干扰文档后所有模型性能显著提升；移除支持文档后与"无语料"几乎相同，证明语料中不含其他可利用信息。人类一致性：DR³-Eval 自动评分与专家的 Pearson r=0.78、Spearman ρ=0.73、成对一致率 0.89（人-人分别为 0.83/0.76/0.91），claim 抽取 P=0.924、R=0.960；更换评审模型（Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini-2.5-Pro、Qwen-Max）平均 Spearman ρ=0.924，多模态评审替换后 ρ=0.864、平均分差 <2 分（p>0.05）。检索器对比中 OpenAI-Emb 最优、Qwen-Emb 略低、BM25 显著更差；RAG 轮次增至 5–7 时 IR/CC 提升但存在过峰轻微回落；与 DeerFlow 对比时 DR³-Agent 在碎片化多模态证据整合与指令坚持上更优。错误归因（5 模型×100 报告）显示幻觉是多数模型的首要失败原因，检索与推理错误分布因模型而异，说明瓶颈在证据利用的稳定性而非仅在证据获取。

Conclusion: DR³-Eval 通过"真实用户多模态材料 + 反向构造任务 + 每任务静态可控沙盒 + 五维人机对齐评测"三者的结合，同时缓解了深度研究评测中真实性、可控性与可评测性之间的张力，为长程研究能力提供了可复现、可验证的测试平台。实验表明当前 SOTA LLM 在该基准上仍面临巨大挑战，暴露出检索鲁棒性与幻觉控制两类系统性失效模式，提示未来研究应重点关注"证据使用的稳定性"以及在信息过载下保持指令遵循与事实忠实。局限方面：规模仅 100 个任务、语言/领域分布有限、沙盒由关键词抓取构建而与真实检索策略仍有差异（尽管实验显示偏差不显著），且评估依赖 LLM-as-a-judge（虽验证了较强的一致性与稳健性）。

Abstract: Deep Research Agents (DRAs) aim to solve complex, long-horizon research tasks involving planning, retrieval, multimodal understanding, and report generation, yet their evaluation remains challenging due to dynamic web environments and ambiguous task definitions. We propose DR$^{3}$-Eval, a realistic and reproducible benchmark for evaluating deep research agents on multimodal, multi-file report generation. DR$^{3}$-Eval is constructed from authentic user-provided materials and paired with a per-task static research sandbox corpus that simulates open-web complexity while remaining fully verifiable, containing supportive documents, distractors, and noise. Moreover, we introduce a multi-dimensional evaluation framework measuring Information Recall, Factual Accuracy, Citation Coverage, Instruction Following, and Depth Quality, and validate its alignment with human judgments. Experiments with our developed multi-agent system DR$^{3}$-Agent based on multiple state-of-the-art language models demonstrate that DR$^{3}$-Eval is highly challenging and reveals critical failure modes in retrieval robustness and hallucination control. Our code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [128] [M2-PALE: A Framework for Explaining Multi-Agent MCTS--Minimax Hybrids via Process Mining and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14687)
*Yiyu Qian,Liyuan Zhao,Tim Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出M2-PALE框架，通过将浅层Minimax搜索融入MCTS的rollout阶段来增强战略深度，并利用过程挖掘技术和LLM生成可解释的决策逻辑说明。实验验证于西洋跳棋环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MCTS的高度选择性树构建策略可能导致关键棋步遗漏和对战术陷阱的脆弱性，且MCTS产生的复杂搜索树使得决策逻辑难以理解，给开发者和用户带来挑战。

Method: 1) 在多智能体MCTS的rollout阶段引入浅层全宽Minimax搜索以增强战略深度；2) 采用过程挖掘技术（Alpha Miner、iDHM、Inductive Miner）从智能体执行轨迹中提取行为工作流；3) 使用LLM将过程模型合成为人类可读的因果和远程解释。

Result: 在小型西洋跳棋环境中验证了方法的有效性，建立了可扩展的混合智能体解释基础。

Conclusion: M2-PALE框架能够有效解释结合MCTS和Minimax的混合决策智能体的行为，为复杂战略领域的可解释人工智能提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Monte-Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) is a fundamental sampling-based search algorithm widely used for online planning in sequential decision-making domains. Despite its success in driving recent advances in artificial intelligence, understanding the behavior of MCTS agents remains a challenge for both developers and users. This difficulty stems from the complex search trees produced through the simulation of numerous future states and their intricate relationships. A known weakness of standard MCTS is its reliance on highly selective tree construction, which may lead to the omission of crucial moves and a vulnerability to tactical traps. To resolve this, we incorporate shallow, full-width Minimax search into the rollout phase of multi-agent MCTS to enhance strategic depth. Furthermore, to demystify the resulting decision-making logic, we introduce \textsf{M2-PALE} (MCTS--Minimax Process-Aided Linguistic Explanations). This framework employs process mining techniques, specifically the Alpha Miner, iDHM, and Inductive Miner algorithms, to extract underlying behavioral workflows from agent execution traces. These process models are then synthesized by LLMs to generate human-readable causal and distal explanations. We demonstrate the efficacy of our approach in a small-scale checkers environment, establishing a scalable foundation for interpreting hybrid agents in increasingly complex strategic domains.

</details>


### [129] [SynHAT: A Two-stage Coarse-to-Fine Diffusion Framework for Synthesizing Human Activity Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14705)
*Rongchao Xu,Lin Jiang,Dahai Yu,Ximiao Li,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SynHAT框架，利用 coars e-to-fine 策略和去噪扩散模型合成真实、隐私保护的人活动轨迹数据，解决了轨迹数据获取困难及生成模型计算成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人活动轨迹(HATs)对人类移动性建模和POI推荐等应用至关重要，但隐私问题限制了大规模真实数据的获取；同时，现有生成模型面临捕捉复杂时空依赖和计算效率低下的双重挑战。

Method: 提出两阶段粗到细合成框架：(1) Stage 1开发Coarse-HADiff，使用带双Drift-Jitter分支的Latent Spatio-Temporal U-Net建模粗粒度时空依赖；(2) Stage 2通过行为模式提取、Fine-HADiff和语义对齐三步从Stage 1输出生成细粒度轨迹。

Result: 在4个城市3个国家的数据集上，SynHAT在空间指标上提升52%，时间指标上提升33%，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SynHAT通过粗到细策略和创新的时空去噪扩散架构，有效平衡了数据保真度、效用、隐私保护和可扩展性，为隐私敏感场景下的轨迹数据合成提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Human activity traces (HATs) are critical for many applications, including human mobility modeling and point-of-interest (POI) recommendation. However, growing privacy concerns have severely limited access to authentic large-scale HAT datasets. Recent advances in generative AI provide new opportunities to synthesize realistic and privacy-preserving HATs for such applications. Yet two major challenges remain: (i) HATs are highly irregular and dynamic, with long and varying time intervals, making it difficult to capture their complex spatio-temporal dependencies and underlying distributions; and (ii) generative models are often computationally expensive, making long-term, fine-grained HAT synthesis inefficient. To address these challenges, we propose SynHAT, a computationally efficient coarse-to-fine HAT synthesis framework built on a novel spatio-temporal denoising diffusion model. In Stage 1, we develop Coarse-HADiff, which models the overall spatio-temporal dependencies of coarse-grained latent spatio-temporal traces. It incorporates a novel Latent Spatio-Temporal U-Net with dual Drift-Jitter branches to jointly model smooth spatial transitions and temporal variations during denoising. In Stage 2, we introduce a three-step pipeline consisting of Behavior Pattern Extraction, Fine-HADiff, which shares the same architecture as Coarse-HADiff, and Semantic Alignment to generate fine-grained latent spatio-temporal traces from the Stage 1 outputs. We extensively evaluate SynHAT in terms of data fidelity, utility, privacy, robustness, and scalability. Experiments on real-world HAT datasets from four cities across three countries show that SynHAT substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving 52% and 33% improvements on spatial and temporal metrics, respectively.

</details>


### [130] [HWE-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Real-World Hardware Bug Repair Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14709)
*Fan Cui,Hongyuan Hou,Zizhang Luo,Chenyun Yin,Yun Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HWE-Bench是首个用于评估LLM智能体在真实硬件bug修复任务中的大规模仓库级基准测试，包含417个任务实例，涵盖6个主流开源项目（Verilog/SystemVerilog和Chisel），覆盖RISC-V核、SoC和安全根信任。最佳智能体在小型核上超过90%准确率，但在复杂SoC项目上低于65%，揭示了硬件设计任务中LLM的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件设计基准测试仅评估孤立的组件级任务（如从规格生成HDL模块），缺乏仓库级评估，无法反映LLM在实际硬件开发中的真实能力。真实硬件开发涉及复杂的多文件协作、跨层次调试和原生验证流程的协调。

Method: 构建HWE-Bench：(1) 从6个主流开源项目的真实历史bug-fix PR中提取417个任务实例；(2) 每个任务封装在容器化环境中；(3) 通过项目原生仿真和回归流程验证正确性；(4) 采用高度自动化的流水线实现新仓库的便捷扩展。

Result: 评估了7种LLM与4种智能体框架的组合：最佳智能体总体解决70.7%的任务；小型核项目超过90%准确率；复杂SoC级项目低于65%。性能差距大于软件基准测试中的常见差距。难度主要取决于项目规模和bug类型分布，而非单纯代码量。

Conclusion: 失败分析揭示三个关键瓶颈：(1) 故障定位；(2) 硬件语义推理；(3) 跨工件协调（RTL、配置、验证组件）。这些发现为开发更强大的硬件感知智能体提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for hardware design primarily evaluate Large Language Models (LLMs) on isolated, component-level tasks such as generating HDL modules from specifications, leaving repository-scale evaluation unaddressed. We introduce HWE-Bench, the first large-scale, repository-level benchmark for evaluating LLM agents on real-world hardware bug repair tasks. HWE-Bench comprises 417 task instances derived from real historical bug-fix pull requests across six major open-source projects spanning both Verilog/SystemVerilog and Chisel, covering RISC-V cores, SoCs, and security roots-of-trust. Each task is grounded in a fully containerized environment where the agent must resolve a real bug report, with correctness validated through the project's native simulation and regression flows. The benchmark is built through a largely automated pipeline that enables efficient expansion to new repositories. We evaluate seven LLMs with four agent frameworks and find that the best agent resolves 70.7% of tasks overall, with performance exceeding 90% on smaller cores but dropping below 65% on complex SoC-level projects. We observe larger performance gaps across models than commonly reported on software benchmarks, and difficulty is driven by project scope and bug-type distribution rather than code size alone. Our failure analysis traces agent failures to three stages of the debugging process: fault localization, hardware-semantic reasoning, and cross-artifact coordination across RTL, configuration, and verification components, providing concrete directions for developing more capable hardware-aware agents.

</details>


### [131] [SGA-MCTS: Decoupling Planning from Execution via Training-Free Atomic Experience Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14712)
*Xin Xie,Dongyun Xue,Wuguannan Yao,Mingxiao Feng,Wengang Zhou,Xiang Qi,Houqiang Li,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SGA-MCTS通过将LLM规划转化为非参数化检索问题来解决推理延迟与泛化能力之间的权衡。离线阶段利用MCTS探索解空间并蒸馏出状态-目标-动作（SGA）原子；在线阶段通过混合符号-语义检索机制获取相关SGA并重新实例化到当前上下文作为软推理提示。该方法使冻结的开源模型能够匹配GPT-5等前沿系统的性能，同时实现System 2推理深度与System 1推理速度的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM规划方法面临推理延迟与泛化能力的权衡：推理时搜索（如MCTS）精度高但计算成本大、延迟高；监督微调泛化性受限且需任务特定的参数更新。SGA-MCTS旨在融合两种范式的优势，实现高效且可扩展的自主规划。

Method: 离线阶段：使用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）探索解空间，将高质量轨迹蒸馏为去词汇化的状态-目标-动作（SGA）原子。这些原子将具体实体抽象为符号槽位，保留可复用的因果逻辑而丢弃领域特定噪声。在线阶段：检索增强智能体采用混合符号-语义机制获取相关SGA，并将其软提示重载到当前上下文作为推理线索。

Result: 在复杂基准测试上的实验表明，该范式使冻结的开源模型能够匹配GPT-5等SOTA系统的性能，且无需任务特定的微调。通过有效摊销搜索的巨额计算成本，SGA-MCTS实现了System 2推理深度与System 1推理速度的兼顾。

Conclusion: SGA-MCTS通过将规划问题转化为检索问题并结合MCTS驱动的知识蒸馏，为LLM提供了一种可扩展且实时可行的自主规划方案。该框架验证了非参数化方法在复杂多步决策任务中的有效性，为构建高效通用智能系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM-powered systems require complex multi-step decision-making abilities to solve real-world tasks, yet current planning approaches face a trade-off between the high latency of inference-time search and the limited generalization of supervised fine-tuning. To address this limitation, we introduce \textbf{SGA-MCTS}, a framework that casts LLM planning as non-parametric retrieval. Offline, we leverage Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to explore the solution space and distill high-fidelity trajectories into State-Goal-Action (SGA) atoms. These atoms are de-lexicalized primitives that abstract concrete entities into symbolic slots, preserving reusable causal logic while discarding domain-specific noise. Online, a retrieval-augmented agent employs a hybrid symbolic-semantic mechanism to fetch relevant SGAs and re-ground them into the current context as soft reasoning hints. Empirical results on complex benchmarks demonstrate that this paradigm enables frozen, open-weights models to match the performance of SOTA systems (e.g., GPT-5) without task-specific fine-tuning. By effectively amortizing the heavy computational cost of search, SGA-MCTS achieves System 2 reasoning depth at System 1 inference speeds, rendering autonomous planning both scalable and real-time feasible.

</details>


### [132] [The Agentification of Scientific Research: A Physicist's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14718)
*Xiao-Liang Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI革命的核心意义在于改变复杂信息和人类专业知识承载、复制和共享的方式，而非仅仅实现自动化。AI for Science不仅会提升研究效率，还会重塑科学协作、发现、出版和评价的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对AI革命的理解过度聚焦于自动化层面，忽视了其在信息传递和知识共享模式上的根本性变革潜力。AI for Science作为重要应用场景，其深远影响尚未被充分认识。

Method: 本文采用概念性分析和方法，从AI作为研究工具到AI作为科学合作者的渐进路径进行阐述，分析AI对科学出版的影响，并强调持续学习和思想多样性在原创科学发现中的关键作用。

Result: 提出了AI从研究工具向科学合作者演进的渐进路径框架，揭示了AI将根本性重塑科学出版的运作方式。

Conclusion: AI革命的意义超越自动化，在于知识载体的根本变革。AI for Science的成功取决于持续学习和思想多样性的保持，AI最终可能成为原创科学发现的重要合作者。

Abstract: This article argues that the most important significance of the AI revolution, especially the rise of large language models, lies not simply in automation, but in a fundamental change in how complex information and human know-how are carried, replicated, and shared. From this perspective, AI for Science is especially important because it may transform not only the efficiency of research, but also the structure of scientific collaboration, discovery, publishing, and evaluation. The article outlines a gradual path from AI as a research tool to AI as a scientific collaborator, and discusses how AI is likely to fundamentally reshape scientific publication. It also argues that continuous learning and diversity of ideas are essential if AI is to play a meaningful role in original scientific discovery.

</details>


### [133] [Personalized and Context-Aware Transformer Models for Predicting Post-Intervention Physiological Responses from Wearable Sensor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14738)
*Esther Brown,Victoria Dean,Finale Doshi-Velez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Transformer的框架，利用消费者可穿戴设备数据预测个体化压力干预后15-120分钟内的心率(HR)、心率变异性(HRV)和跳动间期(BBI)的变化轨迹。该框架结合多 horizon 预测与方向性判断（正向、负向或中性），通过可穿戴传感器数据与用户标记的事件和干预进行实证验证，证明了个性化干预后预测的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可穿戴设备虽能连续采集与压力和恢复相关的生理数据，但用户通常不清楚特定干预措施在未来15-120分钟内会对HR、HRV或BBI产生何种影响。缺乏将生理数据流转化为个性化、可操作压力管理建议的能力。

Method: 1. 采用Transformer模型预测相对于干预前基线的多时间窗口百分变化轨迹；2. 在每个时间窗口进行方向性变化判断（positive/negative/neutral）；3. 使用可穿戴传感器采集的真实数据叠加用户标记的事件和干预数据。

Result: 概念验证阶段表明，个性化干预后生理指标预测是可行的。

Conclusion: 该框架可为压力管理工具提供基础，未来需在更大规模研究中进一步验证，必要时进行适当的监管审查后，可实现针对个人每日状态的个性化干预建议。

Abstract: Consumer wearables enable continuous measurement of physiological data related to stress and recovery, but turning these streams into actionable, personalized stress-management recommendations remains a challenge. In practice, users often do not know how a given intervention, defined as an activity intended to reduce stress, will affect heart rate (HR), heart rate variability (HRV), or inter-beat intervals (BBI) over the next 15 to 120 minutes. We present a framework that predicts post-intervention trajectories and the direction of change for these physiological indicators across time windows. Our methodology combines a Transformer model for multi-horizon trajectories of percent change relative to a pre-intervention baseline, direction-of-change calls (positive, negative, or neutral) at each horizon, and an empirical study using wearable sensor data overlaid with user-tagged events and interventions. This proof of concept shows that personalized post-intervention prediction is feasible. We encourage future integration into stress-management tools for personalized intervention recommendations tailored to each person's day following further validation in larger studies and, where applicable, appropriate regulatory review.

</details>


### [134] [Disentangle-then-Refine: LLM-Guided Decoupling and Structure-Aware Refinement for Graph Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14746)
*Zhaoxing Li,Hai-Feng Zhang,Xiaoming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SDM-SCR框架，通过LLM驱动的语义解耦和谱滤波正则化解决文本属性图中对比学习的噪声问题，实现"先解耦-后精炼"的信号净化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图对比学习在文本属性图上依赖盲随机增强，将任务相关信号与噪声混为一体，导致学习效果受限。

Method: 1) 语义解耦模块(SDM)：利用LLM的指令跟随能力，将原始属性主动解析为非对称的任务导向信号视图和噪声视图；2) 语义一致正则化(SCR)：基于语义信号拓扑平滑、噪声高频的谱特征，作为选择性谱滤波器，仅在信号子空间上强制一致性。

Result: 实验表明SDM-SCR在准确率和效率上均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: "解耦-精炼"机制确保了严格的信号净化，LLM幻觉得以消除且避免过平滑。

Abstract: Conventional Graph Contrastive Learning (GCL) on Text-Attributed Graphs (TAGs) relies on blind stochastic augmentations, inadvertently entangling task-relevant signals with noise. We propose SDM-SCR, a robust framework anchored in Approximate Orthogonal Decomposition. First, the Semantic Decoupling Module (SDM) leverages the instruction-following capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to actively parse raw attributes into asymmetric, task-oriented signal and noise views. This shifts the paradigm from random perturbation to semantic-aware disentanglement. Subsequently, Semantic Consistency Regularization (SCR) exploits the spectral observation that semantic signals are topologically smooth while residual noise is high-frequency. SCR functions as a selective spectral filter, enforcing consistency only on the signal subspace to eliminate LLM hallucinations without over-smoothing. This ``Disentangle-then-Refine'' mechanism ensures rigorous signal purification. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SDM-SCR achieves SOTA performance in accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [135] [CoTEvol: Self-Evolving Chain-of-Thoughts for Data Synthesis in Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14768)
*Zhuo Wang,Zhuo Zhang,Yafu Li,Yu Cheng,Lizhen Qu,Zenglin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoTEvol是一种基于遗传进化框架的思维链(CoT)合成方法，通过种群搜索和反射式全局交叉与不确定性驱动的局部变异来生成高质量数学推理轨迹，在8个数学基准上平均提升6.6%，效率显著优于现有的蒸馏和自合成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量思维链(CoT)的人工标注成本高昂，阻碍了LLM数学推理能力的进一步提升。现有解决方案（如蒸馏和自合成）存在收益递减或计算开销高的问题。

Method: 提出CoTEvol框架，将CoT生成建模为基于种群的轨迹搜索：1) 反射式全局交叉在轨迹层面进行整体重组；2) 不确定性驱动的局部变异在步骤层面进行细粒度优化；3) 设计轻量级、任务感知的适应度函数引导进化过程。

Result: CoTEvol将正确CoT合成成功率提升超过30%，增强了结构多样性，且效率显著提高。基于进化CoT数据微调的LLM在8个数学基准上平均提升6.6%，优于蒸馏和自合成方法。

Conclusion: 进化式CoT合成是数学推理任务中可扩展且有效的思维链生成方法，具有显著的实用价值和研究前景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit strong mathematical reasoning when trained on high-quality Chain-of-Thought (CoT) that articulates intermediate steps, yet costly CoT curation hinders further progress. While existing remedies such as distillation from stronger LLMs and self-synthesis based on test-time search alleviate this issue, they often suffer from diminishing returns or high computing overhead.In this work, we propose CoTEvol, a genetic evolutionary framework that casts CoT generation as a population-based search over reasoning trajectories.Candidate trajectories are iteratively evolved through reflective global crossover at the trajectory level and local mutation guided by uncertainty at the step level, enabling holistic recombination and fine-grained refinement. Lightweight, task-aware fitness functions are designed to guide the evolutionary process toward accurate and diverse reasoning. Empirically, CoTEvol improves correct-CoT synthesis success by over 30% and enhances structural diversity, with markedly improved efficiency. LLMs trained on these evolutionary CoT data achieve an average gain of 6.6% across eight math benchmarks, outperforming previous distillation and self-synthesis approaches. These results underscore the promise of evolutionary CoT synthesis as a scalable and effective method for mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [136] [MirrorBench: Evaluating Self-centric Intelligence in MLLMs by Introducing a Mirror](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14785)
*Shengyu Guo,Tongrui Ye,Jianbo Zhang,Zicheng Zhang,Chunyi Li,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MirrorBench是一个基于心理学中经典镜像自我识别(MSR)测试的模拟基准测试，用于系统评估多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的自我中心智能能力。该基准通过分层框架评估智能体从基本视觉感知到高级自我表征的逐步挑战性任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在交互环境中取得了显著进展，但当前基准测试主要关注感知、理解和对外部对象的交互能力，缺乏对自我中心智能的系统评估。

Method: MirrorBench受到心理学中经典镜像自我识别(MSR)测试的启发，通过一个分层框架来评估具身MLLMs，框架包含逐步挑战的任务层级，从基本视觉感知到高级自我表征。

Result: 对领先MLLMs的实验表明，即使在最低层级，它们的性能仍然显著低于人类表现，揭示了自我参照理解的根本局限性。

Conclusion: 该研究将心理学范式与具身智能联系起来，为评估大型模型中通用智能的出现提供了一个有原则的框架。

Abstract: Recent progress in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has demonstrated remarkable advances in perception and reasoning, suggesting their potential for embodied intelligence. While recent studies have evaluated embodied MLLMs in interactive settings, current benchmarks mainly target capabilities to perceive, understand, and interact with external objects, lacking a systematic evaluation of self-centric intelligence. To address this, we introduce MirrorBench, a simulation-based benchmark inspired by the classical Mirror Self-Recognition (MSR) test in psychology. MirrorBench extends this paradigm to embodied MLLMs through a tiered framework of progressively challenging tasks, assessing agents from basic visual perception to high-level self-representation. Experiments on leading MLLMs show that even at the lowest level, their performance remains substantially inferior to human performance, revealing fundamental limitations in self-referential understanding. Our study bridges psychological paradigms and embodied intelligence, offering a principled framework for evaluating the emergence of general intelligence in large models. Project page: https://fflahm.github.io/mirror-bench-page/.

</details>


### [137] [CogEvolution: A Human-like Generative Educational Agent to Simulate Student's Cognitive Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14786)
*Wei Zhang,Yihang Cheng,Zhirong Ye,Kezhen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CogEvolution框架，通过认知深度感知器(ICAP)、项目反应理论(IRT)记忆检索和进化算法动态认知更新机制，模拟学生认知演化过程，突破现有教育智能体静态人格和认知动态刻画不足的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育智能体主要依赖静态人格模拟学习行为，忽视深度认知能力在学习成效中的决定性作用，且难以表征知识内化、迁移和认知状态转变的动态流动性。这一瓶颈限制了教育智能体对复杂认知过程的精确建模与仿真能力。

Method: 提出三个核心方法：1) 基于ICAP分类学的认知深度感知器，实现学习者认知参与的精准量化；2) 基于项目反应理论(IRT)的记忆检索方法，模拟新旧知识的连接与同化；3) 基于进化算法的动态认知更新机制，实时整合学习行为与认知演化过程。

Result: 实验结果表明：CogEvolution在行为保真度和学习曲线拟合上显著优于基线模型，并能独特地复现符合教育心理学预期的合理且稳健的认知演化路径。

Conclusion: CogEvolution为构建高可解释性教育智能体提供了新范式，证实了融合认知心理学理论与计算方法在教育智能体设计中的有效性。

Abstract: Generative Agents, owing to their precise modeling and simulation capabilities of human behavior, have become a pivotal tool in the field of Artificial Intelligence in Education (AIEd) for uncovering complex cognitive processes of learners. However, existing educational agents predominantly rely on static personas to simulate student learning behaviors, neglecting the decisive role of deep cognitive capabilities in learning outcomes during practice interactions. Furthermore, they struggle to characterize the dynamic fluidity of knowledge internalization, transfer, and cognitive state transitions. To overcome this bottleneck, this paper proposes a human-like educational agent capable of simulating student cognitive evolution: CogEvolution. Specifically, we first construct a cognitive depth perceptron based on the Interactive, Constructive, Active, Passive (ICAP) taxonomy from cognitive psychology, achieving precise quantification of learner cognitive engagement. Subsequently, we propose a memory retrieval method based on Item Response Theory (IRT) to simulate the connection and assimilation of new and prior knowledge. Finally, we design a dynamic cognitive update mechanism based on evolutionary algorithms to simulate the real-time integration of student learning behaviors and cognitive evolution processes. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that CogEvolution not only significantly outperforms baseline models in behavioral fidelity and learning curve fitting but also uniquely reproduces plausible and robust cognitive evolutionary paths consistent with educational psychology expectations, providing a novel paradigm for constructing highly interpretable educational agents.

</details>


### [138] [Sequence Search: Automated Sequence Design using Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14788)
*Rokgi Hong,Hongjun An,Sooyeon Ji,Jongho Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"序列搜索"(Sequence Search)框架,一种基于神经架构搜索的自动化MRI脉冲序列设计方法。该方法将组织特性、成像参数和设计目标作为输入,通过可微分Bloch仿真器和基于梯度的学习来生成和优化脉冲序列,无需传统序列结构先验知识。实验成功复现了常规自旋回波、T2加权自旋回波和反转恢复序列,并发现了如减少RF能量的三脉冲序列等非常规配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MR序列设计主要依赖人类直觉,现有AI驱动方法存在两大局限:①需要初始序列进行参数优化;②需要大量训练数据集。这些限制严重制约了方法的通用性和实用性,因此需要一种无需先验知识且可端到端自动设计MR序列的通用框架。

Method: ①基于神经架构搜索的序列生成:迭代生成候选脉冲序列结构;②可微分Bloch仿真器:实现序列参数的梯度计算;③目标导向损失函数:根据不同成像目标(如对比度、信号强度等)进行优化。整个流程无需预设传统序列结构,可自主探索非常规配置。

Result: 成功复现了传统spin-echo、T2-weighted spin-echo和inversion recovery序列;发现了反直觉的解决方案,如三脉冲自旋回波序列,其在保持类似回波信号的同时降低了RF能量,以及相位偏离常规Hahn-echo的重聚焦方案。

Conclusion: 建立了可泛化的自动化MR序列设计框架,证明了基于神经架构搜索的方法能够探索超出人类直觉范畴的非常规序列配置,为未来MR序列创新设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Developing an MR sequence is challenging and remains largely constrained by human intuition. Recently, AI-driven approaches have been proposed; however, most require an initial sequence for parameter optimization or extensive training datasets, limiting their general applicability. In this study, we propose "Sequence Search," an automated sequence design framework based on neural architecture search. The method takes tissue properties, imaging parameters, and design objectives as inputs and generates pulse sequences satisfying the design objectives, without requiring prior knowledge of conventional sequence structures. Sequence Search iteratively generates candidate sequences through neural architecture search and optimizes them via a differentiable Bloch simulator and objective-specific loss functions using gradient-based learning. The framework successfully replicated conventional spin-echo, T2-weighted spin-echo, and inversion recovery sequences. Less intuitive solutions were also discovered, such as three-RF spin-echo-like sequences with reduced RF energy and refocusing phases deviating from the conventional Hahn-echo. This work establishes a generalizable framework for automated MR sequence design, highlighting the potential to explore configurations beyond conventional designs based on human intuition.

</details>


### [139] [A Comparative Study of CNN Optimization Methods for Edge AI: Exploring the Role of Early Exits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14789)
*Nekane Fernandez,Ivan Valdes,Steven Van Vaerenbergh,Idoia de la Iglesia,Julen Arratibel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在统一部署条件下（ONNX Runtime + 真实边缘硬件）对 CNN 边缘部署的三类优化技术——静态压缩（结构化剪枝、PTQ/DQ 量化）与动态推理（早期退出）——进行了系统性的硬件在环对比与联合评估。结论是：不存在普适最优方案，量化主导静态压缩与内存节省，剪枝最适用于大型过参数化骨干（CPU 上最高 2.53× 加速），早期退出是唯一能实现输入自适应计算节省的动态手段（ResNet-152 最高 2.24×，且 inf-opt 下早期退出率可达 99%+），但会增加 RAM 与分类头开销；而"早期退出 + PTQ"的组合最具协同效应（ResNet-152 达 3.12× 加速、3.95× 压缩、精度回升至 87.28%），是本文推荐的最优边缘部署路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘 AI（AIoT）场景中的 CNN 部署需要在精度、延迟与资源（算力/内存/存储/能耗）之间取得平衡，而云端卸载又带来延迟、隐私、带宽与可靠性问题。现有文献存在三个关键缺口：(1) 绝大多数对比研究在同一范式内部进行（剪枝对剪枝、量化对量化），缺少跨范式（静态 vs. 动态）的统一基准；(2) 多数性能评测在服务器级 GPU 上完成，掩盖了嵌入式真实的内存、功耗与调度约束，早期退出等动态机制的效果无法被真实刻画；(3) 已有涉及早期退出的工作（如剪枝+早退、量化+早退）多局限于 FPGA 单一平台，且未隔离各技术单独贡献，缺少严格的协同性分析。因此需要在相同硬件、相同推理引擎、相同指标体系下对主流优化范式做可复现的部署导向比较，为从业者提供选型依据。

Method: 以 FP32 基线为对照，选取四种架构（大型 ResNet-152、中型 EfficientNet-B2、轻量 MobileNet-V2 与 ShuffleNet-V2），在 ImageNet100 上训练并统一导出为 ONNX，使用 ONNX Runtime 1.19.0 在三类真实边缘设备（Jetson AGX Orin、Jetson Orin Nano、Raspberry Pi 5）上评测，batch size = 1，并刻意避开 TensorRT 等专用引擎以保持中性基线。

技术实现要点：
- 剪枝：结构化剪枝，按通道粒度 CG（16/32）与剪枝比例 PR（0.05–0.30）网格搜索，按精度–体积折中选取配置；因 ShuffleNet-V2 的 channel shuffle 与标准剪枝不兼容而将其排除出剪枝实验。
- 量化：UINT8 的 PTQ 与 DQ；PTQ 采用逐层选择性量化（对 EfficientNet-B2 仅量化 Conv，对 ShuffleNetV2 量化 Conv/Gemm/MatMul，ResNet-152 与 MobileNet-V2 全图量化），据此区分"量化友好"与"量化敏感"架构；为保持 ONNX 流水线统一与可部署性而排除 QAT。
- 早期退出：借鉴 BranchyNet 的辅助交叉熵，但采用两阶段/分类器级训练（冻结骨干，仅训练分支参数），避免分支间梯度干扰与主干表征退化；ResNet-152 采用简单分类头，轻量架构在分支中引入内部 block；分别导出 EEi（仅含单个早退点的截断模型）以隔离出口位置影响；通过扫描置信度阈值 T 选取 acc-opt（精度最优）与 inf-opt（效率最优）两个工作点。
- 部署：因 ONNX 静态图无法表达 torch.cond 等动态控制流，采用模型分区策略——将网络切分为序列子模型，运行时以置信度判据决定是否进入下一子模型，从而用 ONNX Runtime 的顺序执行模拟动态控制流；Jetson 用 CUDA EP，RPi5 用纯 CPU。

评测指标分两类：模型性能（accuracy、compression ratio、总/平均推理时间、早退率、label loyalty、probability loyalty）与系统资源（CPU 平均负载、GPU 负载与显存（jtop）、进程 RSS 内存）。最后在 Jetson Orin Nano CPU 上对"量化+早退"联合方案（对已插入早退的分区模型逐路径独立量化）做联合评估。

Result: 精度与压缩（Table 2）：剪枝强依赖架构——ResNet-152 达 2.37× 压缩、精度仅 −1.86%（89.08→87.22%）；而 EfficientNet-B2 仅 1.20×、精度 −4.06%，MobileNet-V2 仅 1.38×、精度 −8.98%（轻量架构本身冗余少）。量化压缩稳定在 3.47×–3.95×，但策略选择依赖架构：DQ 在量化友好架构上近乎无损（ResNet-152 88.96% vs 89.08%，MobileNet-V2 86.82% vs 87.80%，label/prob loyalty >95%/97%）；PTQ 的逐层选择性策略在敏感架构上更优（ShuffleNet-V2 PTQ 76.84% > DQ 75.74%），而 EfficientNet-B2 上 DQ 发生灾难性退化（精度 11.08%、label loyalty 11.00%），归因于深度可分离卷积与 Swish 造成的非对称激活分布与离群值。早期退出在 acc-opt 下几乎无损（ShuffleNet-V2 EE8 甚至 78.18% > 基线 77.86%），但早退率极低；inf-opt 下早退率可达 99%+ 但精度代价大（MobileNet-V2 EE3 51.60%，ShuffleNet-V2 EE2 40.34%），中间深度出口是最佳折中。阈值 T 跨架构跨出口差异达数个数量级（1e-7～0.85），必须逐出口独立标定。

硬件表现（Tables 3–6）：剪枝在 CPU 上收益最大（ResNet-152 RPi5 达 2.53×，RAM 下降约 30%），在 GPU 上受限（≤1.37×），因标准 GPU 运行时面向稠密算子优化；其 CPU/GPU 占用率变化幅度很小（≤1.1%）。量化在 CUDA 上普遍性能回退（如 ResNet-152 PTQ 0.35×），因为 CUDA 对多数量化算子支持不足、被静默回落到 CPU，形成混合执行与同步瓶颈；在 CPU 上 PTQ 稳定加速（1.17×–2.12×）而 DQ 在紧凑架构中反而变慢（0.70×–0.84×），源于运行时动态标定开销；RAM 方面两者互有胜负。早期退出在 acc-opt 下几乎不加速甚至变慢（0.85×–0.98×，因分支开销与早退率低），在 inf-opt 下收益与模型规模正相关：ResNet-152 达 1.37×–2.24×（早退样本 73.4ms vs 终退 269.3ms），EfficientNet-B2 1.52×–1.63×，MobileNet-V2 1.24×–1.26×，ShuffleNet-V2 仅 1.08×–1.15×；代价是所有早退配置的 RAM 占用均高于基线，且未早退样本需穿过全部分支，延迟甚至高于基线（ShuffleNet-V2 RPi5 终退 15.5ms vs 基线 10.2ms，+52%）。GPU 占用变化幅度仅 ±1–3%。

联合方案（Table 7）：PTQ-EE 在所有架构上优于 PTQ 单独使用——ResNet-152 PTQ-EE3 inf-opt 达 3.12× 加速、精度 87.28%（比 PTQ 单独 +2.98%）、压缩 3.94×；EfficientNet-B2 PTQ-EE8 inf-opt 1.78×、精度 77.86%（比 PTQ 单独 +13.0%）；ShuffleNet-V2 将 PTQ 的 0.66× 延迟劣化转为 1.30× 加速。DQ-EE 则因运行时动态缩放与早退分支叠加导致噪声放大，仅 ResNet-152 可用（2.30×、84.20%），EfficientNet-B2 崩塌至 4.70%、MobileNet-V2 降至 49.78%。

Conclusion: 不存在单一最优优化技术，选型取决于模型类别与目标指标：大型模型（ResNet-152）三类方法均可用，早退提供最高加速、量化主导静态压缩，除 inf-opt 早退外精度基本在基线 ±2% 内；量化敏感架构（EfficientNet-B2）以剪枝为最安全替代，但 inf-opt 早退仍能显著降时延；紧凑模型（MobileNet-V2、ShuffleNet-V2）以量化保静态压缩与内存，而置于中间层的早退在延迟上渐具竞争力甚至超过量化，适合电池供电与实时边缘应用。早期退出是唯一提高 RAM 占用的技术（开销随出口数量增长），其价值必须通过真实时延测量而非静态模型体积来评估。核心结论是"PTQ + 早期退出"的协同组合最具前景：它继承量化的 3–4× 压缩与硬件兼容性，同时借早退缓解精度损失并把加速推至单独使用任一技术之上（3.12× vs 2.12×/1.69×），在存储、电池与实时约束冲突的极端边缘场景中把折中变为真正的双赢。

未来工作：结合早退与结构化剪枝以同时利用动态层跳过与静态参数移除；跨后端部署评估（OpenVINO/TensorRT/TFLite NPU）以进行后端感知的收益衡量；探索早退训练策略（出口特定蒸馏、联合监督、课程学习）对内存与峰值推理成本的影响；以及基于时延预算、能耗约束或输入复杂度的自适应、输入感知的出口策略与动态阈值调整。局限包括：QAT 未纳入、ShuffleNet-V2 未做剪枝、量化联合实验仅在 Orin Nano CPU 上测量、评测仅基于 ImageNet100 与分类任务。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural networks on edge devices requires balancing accuracy, latency, and resource constraints under realistic execution conditions. To fit models within these constraints, two broad strategies have emerged: static compression techniques such as pruning and quantization, which permanently reduce model size, and dynamic approaches such as early-exit mechanisms, which adapt computational cost at runtime. While both families are widely studied in isolation, they are rarely compared under identical conditions on physical hardware. This paper presents a unified deployment-oriented comparison of static compression and dynamic early-exit mechanisms, evaluated on real edge devices using ONNX based inference pipelines. Our results show that static and dynamic techniques offer fundamentally different trade-offs for edge deployment. While pruning and quantization deliver consistent memory footprint reduction, early-exit mechanisms enable input-adaptive computation savings that static methods cannot match. Their combination proves highly effective, simultaneously reducing inference latency and memory usage with minimal accuracy loss, expanding what is achievable at the edge.

</details>


### [140] [Diffusion Crossover: Defining Evolutionary Recombination in Diffusion Models via Noise Sequence Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14790)
*Chisatao Kumada,Satoru Hiwa,Tomoyuki Hiroyasu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“Diffusion crossover（扩散交叉）”：在 DDPM 逆向去噪过程中，把两个父代图像对应的逐步加性高斯噪声序列做球面线性插值（Slerp）以生成子代图像，使交叉操作直接作用于生成轨迹而非仅操作初始种子；并通过插值时间步范围 t_interp 显式调节探索（多样性）与收敛（利用）之间的权衡。实验表明该方法能保持扩散过程几何结构、产生感知上平滑连续的父代间过渡，且有效支持人在回路的交互式进化图像探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式进化计算（IEC）适合优化难以形式化的主观标准（如人类偏好与审美），但在图像等高维生成空间中难以定义语义一致的交叉算子，导致搜索退化为以变异为主导。已有将扩散模型用于进化计算的工作（如 Diffusion Evolution）主要面向数值优化，且交叉操作多局限于提示词或初始隐变量（种子）的插值，未对生成过程本身进行重组；同时未考虑生成过程中逐步注入的加性噪声对结果的主导作用。因此需要在扩散生成过程内部显式定义可控制、多样性可调的交叉算子，以降低用户负担并提升搜索效率。

Method: 将扩散模型的逆向生成过程本身作为进化搜索空间：1) 基因型定义为初始噪声 x_T 与逆向去噪各步所加的高斯噪声序列 Z={z_1,…,z_T}（T=1000，线性噪声调度，U-Net 仅用残差块、无注意力块）；2) 交叉为逐时间步对父代噪声序列做 Slerp（保持范数、恒定角速度），并对初始噪声同样做 Slerp；3) 以 t_interp 把去噪过程分段：前 T→T−t_interp 步使用插值噪声（交叉），其后重新采样标准高斯噪声（等价于变异），每代后 t_interp 以步长 s 递增以向利用倾斜；4) 算法 1（带噪注入的生成）与算法 2（IGA 整体流程：N=10 初始种群、用户每次选 2 个父代、随机 λ~U(0,1) 生成 N 个子代）；5) 训练：MNIST（数字“5”，32×32）与 ModelNet40（sofa，256×256），AdamW、Optuna 调参、CMMD 选模型，数据增强含缩放/旋转/平移/水平翻转。评价：PCA 投影分析扩散轨迹几何、LPIPS 感知连续性（λ 与到父代距离的 Spearman 秩相关）、t_interp 与平均成对 LPIPS 多样性的秩相关、以及定性交互进化实验。

Result: 实验1（PCA）：插值噪声生成的中间图像在各时间步位于两条父代轨迹之间的中间位置，随 λ 连续变化，证明噪声空间的插值保持了扩散过程几何结构并实现语义级混合。实验2（LPIPS）：随 λ 增大，到父代 A 的 LPIPS 单调上升、到父代 B 单调下降，Spearman 相关在两数据集绝大多数运行中显著（p<0.01；仅 MNIST Run1 对 A 不显著，ρ=0.33）。实验3（多样性）：t_interp 从 100 增至 900 时平均成对 LPIPS 单调下降（MNIST 全部运行 p<0.05，ModelNet 除一次运行外 p<0.01），说明插值时长越长输出越受插值轨迹约束。实验4（定性 IGA）：MNIST“粗体数字5”与 ModelNet“L形软垫沙发”任务中，种群随代数推进逐步向目标形态收敛（Gen6/Gen7 达成），全局几何特征稳定继承而纹理细节变化较大。计算成本：生成一批 10 张约需 40–50 秒（GPU），作者认为可借助加速采样与 LDM 潜在空间以降低延迟。

Conclusion: 扩散模型不仅可作为生成器，也可作为可显式定义与控制重组的结构化进化搜索空间。Diffusion crossover 通过噪声序列插值使 IEC 中的交叉操作在生成轨迹层面实现，同时保留扩散模型的生成能力；通过插值时间步范围提供了探索与利用之间的原则性调控机制。局限与展望：尚未做第三方用户研究与收敛速度等定量评估；噪声插值对全局结构影响强、细粒度局部特征难以控制（早期去噪步主导结构）；推测空间拼接式（局部）交叉无效，噪声经 U-Net 后不具局部空间对应性，需用全局算子；初始噪声编辑作用弱于过程噪声；模型选择仅按 CMMD 质量，未兼顾可导航性/多样性；未来需更全面的超参优化，并扩展至加速采样与潜在扩散模型以提升交互响应速度。

Abstract: Interactive Evolutionary Computation (IEC) provides a powerful framework for optimizing subjective criteria such as human preferences and aesthetics, yet it suffers from a fundamental limitation: in high-dimensional generative representations, defining crossover in a semantically consistent manner is difficult, often leading to a mutation-dominated search. In this work, we explicitly define crossover in diffusion models. We propose Diffusion crossover, which formulates evolutionary recombination as step-wise interpolation of noise sequences in the reverse process of Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPMs). By applying spherical linear interpolation (Slerp) to the noise sequences associated with selected parent images, the proposed method generates offspring that inherit characteristics from both parents while preserving the geometric structure of the diffusion process. Furthermore, controlling the time-step range of interpolation enables a principled trade-off between diversity (exploration) and convergence (exploitation). Experimental results using PCA analysis and perceptual similarity metrics (LPIPS) demonstrate that Diffusion crossover produces perceptually smooth and semantically consistent transitions between parent images. Qualitative interactive evolution experiments further confirm that the proposed method effectively supports human-in-the-loop image exploration. These findings suggest a new perspective: diffusion models are not only powerful generators, but also structured evolutionary search spaces in which recombination can be explicitly defined and controlled.

</details>


### [141] [The LLM Fallacy: Misattribution in AI-Assisted Cognitive Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14807)
*Hyunwoo Kim,Harin Yu,Hanau Yi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"LLM谬误"（The LLM Fallacy）概念，指用户在LLM辅助工作流中将系统生成输出误归因于自身独立能力，从而造成"感知能力"与"实际能力"之间的系统性偏离（能力差异 ΔC）。论文属概念性/立场性理论工作，不含新实验数据，主要贡献为：形式化定义、与幻觉/自动化偏见/认知卸载的辨析、四项涌现机制（归因模糊、流利性幻觉、认知外包、管线不透明）、跨六大领域的表现类型学、对招聘/教育/认证等评估体系失效的分析，以及人机协作方法论披露与可检验的实证验证路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究主要聚焦系统层面的可靠性、幻觉、信任校准与对齐（Ji et al., 2023; Ouyang et al., 2022），而较少关注用户在持续与LLM交互后如何重塑对自身能力的感知。特别是在输出由人机迭代共创的场景中，"作者身份"与"能动性"难以分离，导致以可观测输出作为能力代理的评估体系可能将系统辅助表现误判为独立技能。作者由此切入，将分析焦点从"人与系统如何交互决策"转向"外部生成内容如何被内化为自我能力证据"。

Method: 采用理论建构与文献综合（conceptual/position paper）方法：以Kruger & Dunning的归因失当、Wilson & Dunn的元认知监控失败为上位框架，串联自动化偏见（Lee & See, 2004）、认知卸载与扩展心智（Risko & Gilbert, 2016; Clark, 2010）、人机协作与可解释性文献（Amershi et al., 2019; Shneiderman, 2020），并提出机制模型：系统属性（不透明性、流利性、交互即时性）→认知中介（归因模糊、认知外包）→误归因→能力差异ΔC（第4节图1）；另给出计算/语言/分析/创造/知识/专业表达六域类型学（第5节）与跨域观察性证据层（第6节）；第8节披露人机协作写作流程（以NLD-P自然语言声明式提示框架作为约束控制机制，人类作者保留全部概念与决策权）。

Result: 1）明确界定LLM谬误的三个前提条件：LLM中介的输出生成、交互足够无缝以至人机贡献边界不显著、输出达到"类熟练人类表现"的流利/连贯水平；并说明该现象与输出正确性无关，因而与幻觉相区分。2）与相邻构念划界：自动化偏见针对过度依赖系统正确性，认知卸载针对将心智努力外移，二者聚焦任务执行与决策；LLM谬误聚焦能力归因与自我知觉。3）提炼四项机制：人机归因模糊（提示词多为部分/欠定，产出由系统主导，用户以结果反推作者身份）、流利性幻觉（语法正确、风格一致成为能力启发式线索）、认知外包（自生推理减少，削弱自我评估基础）、管线不透明（检索与生成过程不可见，妨碍溯源）；双加工视角下快速直觉判断压制反思性评估。4）类型学显示六域均呈现"外部支架化表现 vs 内部扎根理解"的同一解离结构：编程（可运行但不懂架构与调试）、多语言写作（表层流利≠语义能力）、分析推理（复述推理结构而未经历推理过程）、创作（创意与作者身份再分配）、知识习得（信息可得≠概念掌握，对应"解释深度错觉"）、职业信号（简历/面试中的能力虚报）。5）指出评估层面后果：招聘中高质输出与独立专业能力背离、LLM评判器本身对不确定性措辞等表层特征敏感；教育中"可及性"与"评估效度"张力、削弱偶发学习与概念掌握；技能认证作为能力信号可靠性下降；主张从输出中心转向过程感知（process-aware）评估。6）第6节以跨域观察性模式（非受控实验）提供概念性证据层，并提出可检验假设方向：操纵辅助可见度的受控实验、自我评估与独立绩效的量化指标、纵向追踪、域特异性调节变量（任务复杂度、反馈可得性、中间推理可见性）、以及界面与教育干预。

Conclusion: 论文结论认为，LLM谬误是一种结构性、跨领域一致存在的归因失当，其根源在于LLM交互的三项系统属性与人类归因/监控机制耦合，而非单纯的信任不足或模型错误。随着LLM嵌入认知工作流，输出与能力之间的关联被削弱，传统以输出为指标的评价范式不可靠。研究主张：建立AI素养框架纳入元认知觉察与系统-独立贡献区分，制度层面从输出中心评测转向过程感知评测以显式刻画人机贡献分布；实证层面需通过受控研究、测量工具（主观自评与客观绩效双轨）、纵向研究与干预设计，将LLM谬误从概念构念转化为可实证检验的研究领域，从而在提升协作绩效的同时保证自我评估的准确性。

Abstract: The rapid integration of large language models (LLMs) into everyday workflows has transformed how individuals perform cognitive tasks such as writing, programming, analysis, and multilingual communication. While prior research has focused on model reliability, hallucination, and user trust calibration, less attention has been given to how LLM usage reshapes users' perceptions of their own capabilities.
