Rather than treating reduced KVCache as sufficient, PrfaaS combines model-side KV efficiency with system-side selective offloading, bandwidth-aware scheduling, and cache-aware request placement. This design removes the requirement that heterogeneous accelerators share the same low-latency RDMA fabric, enabling independent scaling of prefill and decode capacity across loosely coupled clusters. In a case study using an internal 1T-parameter hybrid model, a PrfaaS-augmented heterogeneous deployment achieves 54% and 32% higher serving throughput than homogeneous PD and naive heterogeneous baselines, respectively, while consuming only modest cross-datacenter bandwidth.

</details>


### [288] [Scepsy: Serving Agentic Workflows Using Aggregate LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15186)
*Marcel Wagenländer,Otto White,Britannio Jarrett,Pedro Silvestre,Yanda Tao,Guo Li,Huanzhou Zhu,Llúis Vilanova,Peter Pietzuch*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Scepsy 是一个面向多 LLM 智能体工作流的 GPU 集群服务系统。它的核心洞察是：虽然智能体工作流的端到端延迟极难预测，但每个 LLM 在工作流执行时间中所占的比例在不同请求间相当稳定。基于此，Scepsy 将工作流抽象为"聚合 LLM 流水线"（每个 LLM 一个阶段），用轻量的查表+算术模型预测任意 GPU 分配下的吞吐量与延迟，再在该预测器上联合搜索分数 GPU 份额、张量并行度与副本数，最后以拓扑感知的分层启发式把分配放置到集群上。实验显示其在 16 GPU 集群上比 Kubernetes 自动扩缩容提升最高 2.5× 吞吐、1.0–3.3× 更低延迟，比多模型复用系统 Aegaeon 提升最高 8.7× 吞吐/19.6× 延迟，比工作流感知的 Agentix 提升最高 2.0× 吞吐与 2.0× 延迟，并能在负载或结构变化后 138–369 秒内完成一次重配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体工作流通过编排多个 LLM 与工具完成复杂任务，常见于编码智能体、束搜索、多模型推理时扩展与 RAG 流水线。在自建（中小规模）GPU 集群上服务它们面临四类挑战：(1) 框架任意——LangChain、AutoGen、Camel 等编程模型各不相同，服务系统不能假设单一工作流表示或 API；(2) 执行不可预测——token 逐个生成、工具调用与数据依赖的分支/扇出/递归使单请求延迟与调用次数波动巨大（束搜索中端到端延迟跨度 9–264 s、生成器调用 24–844 次，单请求间约 30× 差异），无法基于单次执行预测吞吐与延迟；(3) 优化目标冲突——数据并行（多副本）提升吞吐但挤占张量并行资源、抬高延迟，多 LLM 之间相互耦合，调整一个 LLM 的分配会移动瓶颈，且异构互联限制张量并行；(4) GPU 过订阅——工作流内 LLM 种类异构（嵌入、生成、奖励模型等），粗粒度的一模型一卡分配导致 GPU 利用率低。
现有方案的缺陷：工作流感知的系统（Parrot、Ayo、TokenCake、Agentix、JITServe）要么依赖特定编程模型/抽象，要么只支持单 LLM，且仅在用户给定的固定分配内部做请求级调度，瓶颈 LLM 依然是瓶颈；多 LLM 服务系统（AlpaServe、MuxServe、Prism、Aegaeon、llm-d WVA 等）看不到 LLM 之间的依赖，只能按单模型指标做目标并让用户手工把工作流级目标拆成每模型 SLO。核心缺口是：没有系统在 GPU 分配层面联合利用工作流结构信息。

Method: 1) 建模为聚合 LLM 流水线（§4）：在每个推理引擎的 completions API 前放置 HTTP 代理，追踪每个 LLM 调用的提示/补全、起止时间戳与所属工作流请求，框架无需改动；从多请求 trace 中聚合出每个 LLM 的"每请求调用数 n_m"与"请求级并发度 p_m"（同时在飞的调用数），工具与编排时间被忽略（实测仅数毫秒，不影响分配排序）；再通过重放 trace 调用对每个 LLM 在各张量并行度下扫描到达率至饱和，得到吞吐–延迟曲线（均值及 p50/p90/p99），副本无需扫描（吞吐线性叠加、延迟不变）。合成为流水线：工作流延迟 L_w(λ_w)=Σ_m L_m(λ_w·n_m)·(n_m/p_m)，最大吞吐 T_w=min_m T_m/n_m；因求和与取最小对顺序不变，故无需建模控制流，与数据依赖的分支/循环/扇出兼容。
2) 联合 GPU 调度（§5）：以效用函数（预测吞吐低于目标到达率则 −∞，否则为负预测延迟）为排序依据，搜索空间为每个 LLM 的 (副本数 d_m, 张量并行度 TP_m, GPU 分数份额 f_m)，分数份额把 GPU 视为准连续资源以避免小模型（如 1 s 的嵌入模型）被过度配置。三步剪枝：按延迟比确定份额顺序（高延迟 LLM 不获得更小份额、份额边界可滑动）、按延迟序连续打包份额到 GPU、再枚举满足 TP×d=所跨 GPU 数的并行组合（TP 不超过 NVLink 域大小）。吞吐按 T_w=min_m (f_m/(n_m·TP_m))·T_m 计算，副本数因总份额已决定而消解；延迟曲线不随份额缩放。16 GPU、5 LLM 规模下搜索 <35 s。多工作流时先分配各工作流 GPU 预算，再在预算内搜索，以平等福利（最大化最差服务者效用）聚合。
3) 拓扑感知分数放置（§6）：在 Kubernetes 之上扩展 Nvidia device plugin，先按 TP 度与分数"最受约束优先"把 TP 副本置于同一 NVLink 域、域内连续打包（1.7 GPU 副本取整卡+0.7 份额而非 0.9/0.8 分散），再用 Nvidia MPS 强制每份额的 SM 与显存上限（引擎显存上限为份额的 90%）；错误放置直接失败而非退化到 PCIe；重配置时仅重启分配发生变化的副本，其余经 SGLang Model Gateway 继续服务。
4) 负载自适应（§5.2）：控制器在固定窗口内从 live trace 估计到达率与工作流统计量，与当前分配所依据的负载比较；连续多个窗口超阈值才判定为漂移；此时刷新流水线（仅需过一遍 trace，无需重新 profile）并对所有已部署工作流重跑联合搜索，仅当预测收益超过阈值才重配置，且只重启变化副本。

Result: 实验环境：4 节点 × 4 × RTX A6000（共 16 GPU，节点内两对三代 NVLink，节点间 100 Gbps InfiniBand），MicroK8s v1.32 + vLLM v0.17 + SGLang Model Gateway v0.3；工作负载为 RAG+reranker（e5-base-v2 + Llama-3-8B）、束搜索（Llama-3.2-1B + Llama3.1-8B-PRM）、编码智能体重放（Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B-Instruct 主智能体 + Qwen2.5-7B 子智能体）；对比 Kubernetes 自动扩缩容、Aegaeon（多模型复用）、Agentix（工作流感知）。
- 对比 K8s 自动扩缩容：束搜索吞吐 1.7–2.5×、RAG 1.1–2.2×、编码智能体 1.1–1.7×；延迟改善分别 1.0–1.9×、1.2–3.3×、1.1–2.7×。原因是自动扩缩容由负载信号驱动而振荡，且无法利用张量并行换延迟。
- 对比 Aegaeon：所有 P/D 切分下均胜出，束搜索吞吐 7.5–8.7×、RAG 2.6×，延迟 11.9–19.6× 与 3.2–9.1×；Aegaeon 缺少前缀缓存并在模型切换时更换 prefill 实例，而 Scepsy 保持各 LLM 常驻。
- 对比 Agentix：吞吐 1.1–2.0×，延迟 1.0–2.0×；束搜索的增益来自并行配置差异（RAG 另加分数分配收益，编码智能体仅靠并行度降延迟）。
- 组合工作流（8 GPU）：固定一个工作流速率、扫描另一个，被扫工作流延迟上升至饱和，而固定工作流吞吐保持、延迟上升 <2×，说明平等福利分配避免了饿死。
- 消融：RAG+reranker 主要受益于共置（分数分配）带来的吞吐提升，束搜索与编码智能体主要受益于张量并行带来的延迟下降；两者皆禁用则吞吐与延迟同时变差。
- 自适应：速率跃变检测 23–61 s、138–183 s 内完成重配置；结构（束宽 4→16）跃变检测 92–268 s、203–369 s 内完成；每次跃变恰好重配置一次（自动扩缩容则持续振荡），代价是每次约 100 s 的重启窗口。
- 搜索开销：16 GPU、5 LLM、每卡 10 份额时 <35 s；128 GPU 时约 70 s，仍小于一次副本重启时间，故搜索不是重配置瓶颈。

Conclusion: 智能体工作流虽然端到端延迟不可预测，但每个 LLM 在执行时间中的占比跨请求稳定，因此无需理解工作流控制流，只需刻画每个 LLM 的"聚合需求"即可做 GPU 分配。Scepsy 据此把任意框架的工作流抽象为聚合 LLM 流水线（阶段=LLM），用"追踪 + 独立 profile"获得框架无关的统计量与性能曲线，构造出可秒级评分的延迟/吞吐预测器；在其上联合搜索分数 GPU 份额、张量并行度与副本数，实现吞吐受约束、延迟为目标的分配；再用拓扑感知的两阶段分层启发式把分配落到集群上，并用反馈回路在负载与结构变化时只做必要的一次重配置。相比独立优化 LLM 或依赖用户手工分配的系统，它在吞吐、延迟、GPU 利用率与多工作流公平性上均显著占优，且搜索开销远低于部署开销。局限包括：等式 (1) 将跨不同 LLM 的扇出视为串行、同一 LLM 承担两种不同负载角色时平均统计量不成立；运行时主导的工具调用不被建模；预测基于稳态平均，结构检测依赖已完成请求的统计量因而较慢；实现依赖对 Kubernetes 与 Nvidia device plugin 的修改及 MPS 隔离，抽象深度尚未下沉到请求级调度（如 KV 共享与图感知批处理仍需框架专用方案）。

Abstract: Agentic workflows carry out complex tasks by orchestrating multiple large language models (LLMs) and tools. Serving such workflows at a target throughput with low latency is challenging because they can be defined using arbitrary agentic frameworks and exhibit unpredictable execution times: execution may branch, fan-out, or recur in data-dependent ways. Since LLMs in workflows often outnumber available GPUs, their execution also leads to GPU oversubscription.
  We describe Scepsy, a new agentic serving system that efficiently schedules arbitrary multi-LLM agentic workflows onto a GPU cluster. Scepsy exploits the insight that, while agentic workflows have unpredictable end-to-end latencies, the shares of each LLM's total execution times are comparatively stable across executions. Scepsy decides on GPU allocations based on these aggregate shares: first, it profiles the LLMs under different parallelism degrees. It then uses these statistics to construct an Aggregate LLM Pipeline, which is a lightweight latency/throughput predictor for allocations. To find a GPU allocation that minimizes latency while achieving a target throughput, Scepsy uses the Aggregate LLM Pipeline to explore a search space over fractional GPU shares, tensor parallelism degrees, and replica counts. It uses a hierarchical heuristic to place the best allocation onto the GPU cluster, minimizing fragmentation, while respecting network topology constraints. Our evaluation on realistic agentic workflows shows that Scepsy achieves up to 2.4x higher throughput and 27x lower latency compared to systems that optimize LLMs independently or rely on user-specified allocations.

</details>


### [289] [Wave-Based Dispatch for Circuit Cutting in Hybrid HPC--Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15279)
*Ricard S. García-Raigada,Josep Jorba,Sergio Iserte*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 DQR（Dynamic Queue Router）运行时框架，将量子线路切割后的"片段"作为一等可调度单元，通过后端无关的片段描述符与基于波次（wave-based）的非阻塞轮询调度器，解耦切割逻辑与执行编排，并在 CESGA Qmio 超级计算机上集成本地 QPU 与 IBM Torino 云端 QPU 进行生产级验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ 时代，线路切割（circuit cutting）可将大线路分解为独立片段并行执行，但现有框架（如 Qdislib、Kubernetes-native 工作流框架）把切割逻辑与执行编排紧耦合：片段往往按硬件类型整体移交厂商队列系统，调度与放置是静态的、依赖框架的，导致 HPC 中心无法对量子工作负载施加成熟的资源管理、容错与异构路由策略；同时已有工作多聚焦算法分解或小规模静态原型，缺少把"单个片段"暴露为可调度单元的细粒度运行时。此外，混合 HPC–QC 执行通常把 QPU 当作粗粒度外部加速器，经典 HPC 仅承担前后处理，未能利用其分布式/自适应能力。

Method: 1) 片段抽象：定义不可变元组 f_i=(SC_i, q_i, d_i, g_i, c_i, B_i)（子线路载荷、量子比特数、深度、二比特门数、准概率重构系数、可接受后端集合），执行层无需解析量子线路代码即可路由；切割层作为 gRPC 微服务封装 Qdislib，可替换。2) 标注策略（qcut_label_job，读取 .meta.json）：Budget（QC 包络硬约束 + HPC 多指标投票法定数）、Score（归一化加权 QC 压力分数 S_i 与双阈值死区）、Hybrid（两阶段）、Autobudget（按 (α_QC,α_HPC,α_U) 全局配额按压力分数排序重分配），输出 QC/HPC/Undecided 软提示。3) 波次调度（Algorithm 1）：协调器 rank 0 用 MPI_Iprobe 非阻塞轮询完成事件，处理瞬时失败重试/永久失败降级（QC→HPC 故障转移），刷新 Slurm/MPI 与 QC 槽位容量，plan(PENDING,C) 生成调度计划，提交并提交状态 PENDING→DISPATCHED；波次计数作为迭代感知策略索引而非全局屏障，实现流水并发。4) 实现：三层松耦合架构（切割层 / 运行时编排层 DQR+RMS / 执行后端层），经 GPFS 与 MPI 通信；QCore C++ 库 + C API 抽象 HPC（Qulacs 态矢量模拟，1024 shots）与 QPU（Python bridge 调用 qmio SDK 或 IBM Quantum）后端，统一 qcore.result.v1 JSON schema；结果不经 MPI 传输而直写 GPFS；worker 轮询环式选取，容量由 SLURM_NTASKS/NNODES 与 DQR_QC_SLOTS_TOTAL、DQR_QC_DEGRADED 等环境变量决定；指标定义 QPU-time、DQR-time、makespan、T_QC/T_HPC、平衡数 Φ=T_QC/T_HPC、重叠加速 σ、固定协调残差 C_fixed。5) 实验：32 比特 HEA（L=1，96 门，深度 34，k=2 门切割得 72 个子线路评估；L=2，192 门，深度 68，k=4 得 2592 个），对比 CPU 单体基线与 4 种 DQR 策略 A–D，改变标注比例、QC 槽位数、MPI 秩数与节点数；本地 Qmio QPU 与远程 IBM Torino 两种 QPU 情形；并做 t̄_QPU 敏感性投影分析。

Result: L=1 情形：CPU 单体基线 makespan 56.2 s（模拟 40.3 s + 采样 15.5 s，2^32 态矢量占 64 GB）；最佳策略 A（10/80/10，3 QC 槽，12+1 秩，2 节点）makespan 50.7 s，1.11× 加速，DQR-time 41.7 s，Φ=2.08（QC 受限），C_fixed=4.4 s；策略 B（3 槽、14 QC 片段）因槽位不足产生阶梯式串行，makespan 91.0 s（1.62× 变慢）；策略 C（同标注但 20 槽）将 5 个串行轮压缩为 2 轮，makespan 降至 59.6 s（降低 34.5%），Φ=0.96、σ=1.96、C_fixed=2.57 s 最优重叠；策略 D（72 槽、73 秩、4 节点）单波提交全部片段，DQR-time 45.6 s 最短、makespan 54.9 s（较基线快 2.4%），但因 IBM Cloud 往返时延方差（σ=8.4 s vs A 的 1.2 s），T_QC 由最慢片段决定，反而比 A 长 3.9 s。L=2 情形：2592 片段、193 秩、15 槽、3 节点，makespan 316.7 s（为 CPU 基线 96.4 s 的 3.29× 慢），DQR-time 190.4 s，其中 T_QC=181.3 s 占 95%，C_fixed=9.1 s 仅占 5%，setup 126.3 s 由 QCut 启动与子线路生成主导；将 t̄_QPU 从 18.5 s 换成 Qmio 实测 4.5–6.6 s 的投影可使 T_QC 降至 39.0–57.2 s，转为 HPC 受限，makespan 降至 212.1 s（2.20× 基线）。容错方面：本地 Qmio 上 7 个 QC 片段中 5 个成功、2 个因 Qiskit if_else 控制流不被 Qmio 原生栈支持而失败（QCORE_ERUNTIME），重试耗尽后经 DQR_ALLOW_FAILOVER_QC_TO_HPC 自动降级为 HPC 任务并在无全局重启的情况下完成全部 72 片段并完成张量重构（makespan 44.6 s）。重构可观测值 O 在 1024 shots 噪声底（σ≈0.031）内与 CPU 参考一致（IBM 侧 −2.7e−5…+1.8e−3，<0.06σ；本地 −6.6e−3 即 0.21σ），未见系统性偏差。

Conclusion: DQR 通过后端无关片段描述符、波次式动态调度与生产级实现，验证了"切割—执行解耦"能把量子执行转化为可复用 HPC 技术的经典异构调度问题：非阻塞波次调度实现 HPC 与 QPU 关键路径重叠，片段级故障隔离支持透明故障转移，协调开销有界（2592 片段下仅占 DQR-time 的 5%）。但作者明确指出波次调度本身并不保证优于基线——性能主要由 QC 片段比例与 QC 槽位数联合决定，必须按目标后端的 QPU 往返时延（含方差）校准，该校准目前仍需人工完成。已识别三大局限：(1) Qmio 编译器缺口（门切割注入的 if_else 中途经典分支不被支持，需控制流感知编译器或避免中途分支的切割策略）；(2) 供给缺口（槽位数与 MPI 秩在提交时固定，计划与 DMR 可塑性资源管理器集成以运行时调整）；(3) 基准覆盖缺口（规则 HEA 片段结构同质，Budget/Score/Hybrid 标注策略未在真实异质负载上验证）。后续工作包括自动策略选择与学习型路由、移植到任务式运行时、跨本地与云后端的重复端到端评测。

Abstract: Hybrid High-performance Computing (HPC)-quantum workloads based on circuit cutting decompose large quantum circuits into independent fragments, but existing frameworks tightly couple cutting logic to execution orchestration, preventing HPC centers from applying mature resource management policies to Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) workloads. We present DQR (Dynamic Queue Router), a runtime framework that bridges this gap by treating circuit fragments as first-class schedulable units. The framework introduces a backend-agnostic fragment descriptor to expose structural properties without requiring execution layers to parse quantum code, a wave-based coordinator that achieves pipeline concurrency via non-blocking polling, and a production-ready implementation on the CESGA Qmio supercomputer integrating both QPUs local on-premises (Qmio) and remote cloud (IBM Torino) backends. Experiments on a 32-qubit Hardware-Efficient Ansatz (HEA) circuit demonstrate not only makespan improvements over a monolithic CPU baseline but also transparent per-fragment failover recovery-specifically rerouting tasks from the local QPU to classical simulators upon encountering hardware-level incompatibilities-without pipeline restart. For deeper circuits, the coordination residual accounts for only 5% of the total execution time, highlighting the framework's scalability. These results show that DQR enables HPC centers to integrate NISQ workloads into existing production infrastructure while preserving the flexibility to adopt improved cutting algorithms or heterogeneous backend technologies.

</details>


### [290] [BitFlipScope: Scalable Fault Localization and Recovery for Bit-Flip Corruptions in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22174)
*Muhammad Zeeshan Karamat,Sadman Saif,Christiana Chamon Garcia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: BitFlipScope 是一个纯软件、可扩展的 LLM 位翻转故障定位与恢复框架。它在两种部署场景下工作：有干净参考模型时，通过差分隐状态分析（自适应变化点检测 ADCD）先定位 transformer 块，再定位子层，最后用哈希比对精确到权重与比特位，实现 O(L) 的细粒度定位与定向参数恢复；没有参考模型时，通过残差路径缩放扰动与损失敏感性分析（Block Sensitivity Score + 基于中位数/MAD 的鲁棒 z 分数 + 迭代中和）直接从受损模型推断故障块，并可用推理时残差衰减做轻量恢复，无需微调。实验在 8-bit 量化的 LLaMA 3.2 3B 与 LLaMA 3.1 8B 上进行，定位在所有测试块与子层上 100% 成功，干净模型假阳性率为 0%，比较次数比暴力差分降低两个数量级以上，自参考恢复可挽回 80% 以上精度损失，差分设定下可完全恢复基线精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 已在教育、金融、医疗等安全关键场景广泛部署，而硬件老化、宇宙射线或 Rowhammer 等故障注入攻击可静默翻转极少量权重的高位，造成灾难性性能退化：LLWRA 报告仅 10–128 个权重位翻转即可使 GPT-2 32-bit 精度下降 73%，GenBFA 用 3 位翻转让 LLaMA3 8B 的 MMLU 精度从 67.3% 跌至 0%，Rowhammer 仅 2 位即可使 MobileNet-V2 损失 75% 精度。现有防御工作存在两大空白：一是攻击侧（选择最脆弱位）研究充分，而恢复侧的“事后定位”缺乏实用工具——已有检测方法要么只给出全局退化指标，要么依赖逐参数穷举比对与冗余模型/校验位等预置容错结构，在十亿级参数 LLM 上不可行；二是实际部署（边缘端、内存受限、原地更新）往往拿不到干净参考模型，因此需要一种“仅凭自身即可诊断”的自参考方法。若不能定位受影响区域，就无法诊断退化根因、无法做定向修复，只能付出昂贵微调或完全重训的代价。

Method: 框架按“是否有干净参考模型”分为两条互补路径。①自参考定位（self-referential）：基于 transformer 残差结构 h_{i+1}=h_i+α_i·F_i(h_i)，对每个块施加 α∈{0.6,0.7,0.8,0.9,1.1,1.2,1.3,1.4} 的残差缩放（附录 A 通过 [0.2,1.8] 扫描确定该区间最具诊断性），度量 ΔLoss(ℓ,α)=Loss(ℓ,α)−Loss_base，并聚合为块敏感度分数 BSS(ℓ)=Σ|ΔLoss|；利用中位数/MAD 归一化得到鲁棒 z 分数并与阈值 τ 比较（τ≈6–8，由 union bound 给出 O(√log L) 量级的族系误报控制），采用迭代检测：选出 z 最大且超阈值的块后永久中和其残差贡献并重算分数，以避免强故障掩盖弱故障，从而支持多块故障定位。全过程无梯度、仅需前向传播（32 块×8 个 α=256 次前向，可缩减至 64 次）。②差分定位（differential）：第一阶段为块级定位，计算逐块隐状态余弦相似度 S_ℓ 与发散轨迹 d_ℓ=1−S_ℓ，并形式化为自适应变化点检测（ADCD）：先用 clean–clean 比较估计经验零分布并取 99.9 百分位作 τ_null，首个 d_ℓ>τ_null 的位置即首个故障块，其余故障通过对 Δ_ℓ=d_ℓ−d_{ℓ−1} 用 median+5·MAD 阈值寻找显著正局部极大值来识别；理论部分给出残差误差传播模型、Lipschitz 界、稀疏支撑恢复等价性与精确恢复的充分条件。第二阶段在可疑块内比较注意力/MLP 子层激活，发散最大者为故障子层。第三阶段对层内张量先做 SHA-1 哈希筛出不一致张量，再逐元素比对定位被翻转权重与比特位。恢复阶段：差分设定下用干净模型对应张量做定向替换（保留其余权重，避免全量参数搬运与 I/O 瓶颈）；自参考设定下将故障块残差贡献置零/衰减，抑制其异常计算而其余网络不变。

Result: 实验在 NVIDIA RTX 6000 Ada 上使用 8-bit 量化的 LLaMA 3.2 3B 与 LLaMA 3.1 8B，评测集为 MMLU（57 个学科），注入方式沿用 GenBFA 的敏感位选择但仅翻转其候选位的不到 3%。定位效果：自参考设定下，3B 模型在块 3/5/9/12、8B 模型在块 3/9/15/28 的注入中，损坏块的 BSS 与鲁棒 z 分数均为最大值，且呈现特征性的“非对称”损失响应（α>1 时损失骤升、α<1 时下降，健康块则变化平缓或单调）；仅需 64 条样本即可稳定选出损坏块，256/512 只是降低方差；多块注入（如 5&9、12&5）可通过迭代中和逐个暴露；对干净模型运行检测流程无任何块超阈（τ=3），假阳性率 0%。差分设定下，MLP 的 up/down 投影与注意力的 Q/K/V 投影在各块、各模型规模上均被 100% 正确定位到块与子层（如块 12 的 up_proj 相似度 0.38，块 9 的 down_proj 0.55，块 5 的 down_proj 0.59）。效率上，16 块模型暴力差分需约 1.88×10^9 次逐元素比较，BitFlipScope 层级过滤后仅约 1.68×10^7 次，降低两个数量级以上，且不需重训、梯度或组合搜索。恢复方面：3B 模型 MMLU 精度从 61% 掉到 3.2%，残差中和后回到 51%（挽回 82.7%）；8B 从 69% 掉到 3.9%，恢复到 56%（挽回 80.0%）；差分设定下直接替换损坏张量可完全恢复基线精度。

Conclusion: BitFlipScope 证明了大模型位翻转故障的软件化、规模化定位与无微调恢复是可行且有实际价值的：差分行为分析可在 O(L) 时间内把定位细化到块、层、权重与比特位，计算量远低于暴力比对；残差路径敏感性分析则使在完全没有干净参考模型的边缘/受限部署中也能以块级精度完成故障诊断，并通过推理时衰减挽回 80% 以上性能损失。该框架不依赖重训、辅助神经元或预置容错结构，为面向硬件易损与对抗环境的可信、抗故障 LLM 部署提供了可行路径。其局限在于：自参考设定仅支持块级定位（更细粒度需有参考模型），依赖损失敏感度对故障的响应幅度（低影响位翻转或极端 α 饱和区诊断性下降），阈值需按模型/层数调整（τ 取 6–8、深度趋势由鲁棒归一化处理），实验仅覆盖 LLaMA 3.2 3B/3.1 8B 的 8-bit 量化模型、且未涉及真实硬件故障或同时多故障叠加的更复杂场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed in practical and safety-critical settings are increasingly susceptible to bit-flip faults caused by hardware degradation, cosmic radiation, or deliberate fault-injection attacks such as Rowhammer. These faults silently corrupt internal parameters and can lead to unpredictable or dangerous model behavior. Localizing these corruptions is essential: without identifying the affected region, it is impossible to diagnose the source of degradation, apply targeted corrective measures, or restore model functionality without resorting to costly fine-tuning or full retraining. This work introduces BitFlipScope, a scalable, software-based framework for identifying fault-affected regions within transformer architectures under two deployment scenarios. When a clean reference model is available, BitFlipScope performs differential analysis of outputs, hidden states, and internal activations for detecting anomalous behavior indicative of corruption to pinpoint or localize faults. When no reference model exists, it uses residual-path perturbation and loss-sensitivity profiling to infer the fault-impacted region directly from the corrupted model. In both settings, the framework not only enables effective fault diagnosis but also supports lightweight performance recovery without fine-tuning, offering a practical path to restoring corrupted models. Together, these capabilities make BitFlipScope an important step toward trustworthy, fault-resilient LLM deployment in hardware-prone and adversarial environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [291] [PriHA: A RAG-Enhanced LLM Framework for Primary Healthcare Assistant in Hong Kong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14215)
*Richard Wai Cheung Chan,Shanru Lin,Ya-nan Ma,Hao Chen,Liangjun Jiang,Wenqi Fan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PriHA 是面向香港初级医疗的检索增强生成（RAG）大模型助手框架，采用三阶段流水线：查询优化器（意图分类、多轮澄清、意图泛化）→ DRAG 双路检索（本地初级医疗知识库 PHR 的父子分块+关键词/语义混合检索与 BGE 重排，以及带安全白名单、LLM 重排与爬虫校验的 ReAct 式网络搜索代理）→ 增强生成（Reconciler 冲突消解与引用溯源）。在自建 HK-PriHCQA 数据集（400 对，随机 80 题评测，LLM-as-a-judge 五维 0-5 分）上，DRAG 平均分 4.20，优于零样本 LLM（3.74）、Web-Only（3.19）与 Local-Only（3.87）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 香港人口快速老化（预计 2046 年 65 岁以上人口超 36%），慢性病负担与公共医疗支出攀升，港府推动"预防胜于治疗"、将重心转向初级医疗并鼓励市民利用社区资源自我管理。但官方临床指引与资助计划分散在不同政府部门与格式（多为 PDF 表格、图像，甚至仅有英文版本），造成显著的信息获取壁垒；超过一半受访者表示无法获得所需的健康指引。通用 LLM（ChatGPT、DeepSeek）在回答地区性、专业性问题时易产生事实错误、幻觉与无效链接，且对模糊提问（如"哪种运动对膝盖更好？"）缺乏主动澄清，输出无法交叉验证，导致信任问题。因此需要一个本地化、可溯源、且能主动引导用户转向社区初级医疗资源的可靠助手。

Method: 系统为三阶段流水线。（1）初级医疗知识库 PHR：将官方 PDF 转为 Markdown 保留结构，图像以链接+人工标注，并标注"文件来源""更新时间"元数据用于后续冲突消解。（2）查询优化器：意图分类器（IC）区分 SIMPLE/COMPLEX，COMPLEX 进入多轮澄清（MRC）以明确意图、背景与优先级，形成 I_clear；意图泛化器（IBG）生成原子化初级医疗导向子查询（如"香港牙科价格""深圳牙科价格"），并嵌入"社区优先"原则，自动覆盖地区健康中心与政府资助计划，绕开通用 LLM 默认导向急诊/医院二级三级服务的倾向。（3）DRAG 双路检索：本地检索采用父子分块（小块检索、大块供上下文）+关键词检索与语义检索混合合并候选池 +轻量 PLM（BGE-Reranker-V2）top-k 重排；网络搜索代理（WSA）以 ReAct 循环执行"规划—粗搜—白名单筛选—LLM 零样本重排—爬取实时校验可访问性/真实性—充分性自评—反思迭代"，剔除失效、伪造或跳转链接。（4）增强生成：Reconciler 联合评估本地上下文 C_Local、网络上下文 C_Web 与当前用户画像 T_current，按机构认证与时效性设定优先级规则，用推理模型单次完成冲突消解与报告式摘要，输出附参考文献 R_final 的可溯源回答。

Result: 在 80 题随机子集上，DRAG 在准确度（3.95）、完整性（4.03）、可信度（3.98）、清晰度（4.86）、相关性（4.18）与总体均值（4.20）上全面领先；Zero-shot LLM 均值 3.74、Local-Only RAG 3.87、Web-Only RAG 3.19。消融显示存在明显权衡：Web-Only 完整性最高但准确度最低（2.54），说明未过滤的信息量会稀释事实完整性；Local-Only 相关性高（4.34）但完整性最低（3.35），因静态库更新滞后。案例研究（长者医疗券在珠海人民医院的使用）显示：零样本模型误判为社保问题并产生幻觉，Web-Only 引入不相关上下文污染，Local-Only 内容陈旧，DRAG 通过"本地定调 + 网络验证时效"并由 Reconciler 优先权威且时间有效的条目，输出连贯且具同理心的答案。

Conclusion: PriHA 通过集中化整合碎片化政府指引、双源检索与结构化冲突消解，实现了准确、可溯源的初级医疗问答，并在用户未明确询问时主动推荐相关社区初级医疗服务，从而引导用户从"依赖医院"转向"预防导向的自我管理"，建立了可迁移至其他高风险本地化公共服务场景的责任型 AI 设计范式。局限方面，评测由 LLM-as-a-judge 在 80 题子集上完成，评测维度偏主观且样本有限；查询优化器在主实验中因需保持问答任务结构一致而被关闭，多轮澄清模块的实际增益未量化；数据与知识库构建、标注依赖较多人工。未来工作方向为自动化知识更新与引入语音交互以进一步提升可及性。

Abstract: To address the unsustainable rise in public health expenditures, the Hong Kong SAR Government is shifting its strategic focus to primary healthcare and encouraging citizens to use community resources to self-manage their health. However, official clinical guidelines are fragmented across disparate departments and formats, creating significant access barriers. While general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT and DeepSeek offer potential solutions for information accessibility, they are prone to generating factually inaccurate content due to a lack of localized and domain-specific knowledge. To this end, we propose a Retrieval-Augmented Generation-Enhanced LLM system as Primary Healthcare Assistant (PriHA) in Hong Kong. Specifically, a tri-stage pipeline is proposed that leverages a query optimizer to generalize user intent-oriented sub-queries, followed by a novel Dual Retrieval Augmented Generation (DRAG) architecture for mixed-source retrieval and context-reorganized generation. Comprehensive experiments and a detailed case study demonstrate that our proposed method can outperform both ablations and baseline in terms of accuracy and clarity. Our research provides a reliable and traceable dialogue retrieval framework for exploring other high-risk, localized application scenarios.

</details>


### [292] [Knowledge Graph RAG: Agentic Crawling and Graph Construction in Enterprise Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14220)
*Koushik Chakraborty,Koyel Guha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"Agentic Knowledge Graphs"结合递归爬取技术，解决传统RAG在复杂企业文档生态中无法有效处理层级关系和多跳引用的局限性。在CFR（联邦法规汇编）数据集上的基准测试表明，该方案相比标准向量检索RAG系统实现70%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG（检索增强生成）系统依赖语义向量搜索，在处理具有层级结构、相互引用和"替代逻辑"的复杂企业文档时存在明显不足。这些文档（如法规、代码等）中的信息高度互联，标准向量检索难以捕捉跨层级依赖关系和引用传递性。

Method: 提出Agentic Knowledge Graphs架构，结合递归爬取（Recursive Crawling）机制，能够识别并追踪文档间的多跳引用路径和层级替代关系。通过构建知识图谱显式建模这些复杂关联，为检索阶段提供结构化的上下文理解。

Result: 在CFR数据集上的基准测试显示，Agentic Knowledge Graphs相比传统向量RAG在复杂法规查询任务中达到70%的准确率提升，能够提供更全面、精确的答案。

Conclusion: 知识图谱增强的方法能够有效解决企业文档生态中的层级信息和交叉引用问题。递归爬取机制是实现精确多跳检索的关键。该方案在法规查询等高复杂度场景具有显著应用价值。

Abstract: This research paper addresses the limitations of semantic search in complex enterprise document ecosystems. Traditional RAG pipelines often fail to capture hierarchical and interconnected information, leading to retrieval inaccuracies. We propose Agentic Knowledge Graphs featuring Recursive Crawling as a robust solution for navigating superseding logic and multi-hop references. Our benchmark evaluation using the Code of Federal Regulations (CFR) demonstrates that this Knowledge Graph-enhanced approach achieves a 70% accuracy improvement over standard vector-based RAG systems, providing exhaustive and precise answers for complex regulatory queries.

</details>


### [293] [Don't Retrieve, Navigate: Distilling Enterprise Knowledge into Navigable Agent Skills for QA and RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14572)
*Yiqun Sun,Pengfei Wei,Lawrence B. Hsieh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 **Corpus2Skill**：把企业文档语料离线“编译”为一棵层级化的 skill 文件树（SKILL.md / INDEX.md + documents.json + entity_index.json），服务期由 LLM 智能体用 `code_execution`（ls/cat/grep 浏览目录）与 `get_document(doc_id)` 两个工具主动“导航”该树来定位证据，取代传统的向量检索。WixQA 上 Token F1 达 0.456（较最强基线 Agentic 0.378 相对提升 21%），Factuality 0.767、Context Recall 0.708 均领先 BM25/Dense/Hybrid/RAPTOR/Agentic；但 11 数据集实验表明导航并非普适替代（5 胜 3 平 3 负），在开放域事实型与同质表格型语料上反而劣于扁平检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 RAG 把模型当作检索结果的被动消费者：只拿到 top-k 片段，看不到语料如何组织、也不知道“还有哪些没被检索到”，因此无法回退（backtrack）或跨分支拼接散落证据；Agentic RAG 虽可多轮查询，但每次检索都是“盲猜”关键词，缺乏语料结构地图；RAPTOR/GraphRAG 等层级方法虽用 LLM 摘要建树，查询时仍通过嵌入检索间接消费结构，模型看不到产生结果的组织结构。同时，Agent Skills 生态（Anthropic Skills API 的 SKILL.md + progressive disclosure）已证明“结构化可复用知识模块 + 按需加载”能以低成本承载大语料，但该范式此前只用于“程序性知识”（如何做事），尚未用于“信息性知识”（语料里有什么）。作者据此提出“corpus navigability（语料可导航性）”这一未被充分探索的 RAG 轴线，主张把层级结构本身而非嵌入索引作为模型与语料之间的主要接口。

Method: **框架（compile-then-navigate，两阶段）**：
- *编译阶段（离线，一次性）*：① 文档表示——每篇文档先经一次轻量 LLM 调用产出 “summary card”（title / one_line / 2–4 个 distinctive phrases），与截断原文拼接后用 Qwen3-Embedding-8B 编码（4096 维），卡片复用为 INDEX.md 行元数据；② 自底向上建树——按分支比 p 与最大顶层簇 K 递归 K-Means（分支比 p=10、K=10 → WixQA 上 6 个顶层 skill、665 个导航文件、3 层），每簇由 LLM 生成“路由型”摘要（主题域/问题类型/关键术语），簇摘要拼接成员摘录后重新嵌入作为下一层输入，直至簇数 < K；③ verify-and-repartition——LLM 联合审视兄弟摘要，标记 confusable（主题重复）与 mixed（跨主题）簇并按 ID 重划分、重摘要（仅 1–2 层）；④ 富化——软分配（top1/top2 余弦差 < τ=0.05 时在次优簇 INDEX.md 写 @see 桩，共 2068 个）、非叶节点 top-3 邻近质心文档作 exemplars、批量 LLM 抽取命名实体与文档类型生成 entity_index.json（2328 条）并据此生成 “## Related skills”（实体 Jaccard 相似前 3）；⑤ 物化为 skill 森林（YAML frontmatter: name/description/level/num_documents），全文单独存 documents.json（13 MB），总产出 16 MB；层级深度 O(log_p N)，每查询仅需检视约 30 份摘要而非 O(N)。
- *服务阶段*：skill 目录上传 Anthropic Skills API，渐进式披露（预载 skill 名+描述约 200 token，按需读 SKILL.md→INDEX.md→文档）；智能体配两个工具（导航用 code_execution：ls/cat/grep；取证用 get_document(doc_id)）；系统提示强制 grounding 规则（每条断言必须溯源到 get_document 到的文档，摘要文件仅用于导航）、多候选策略（提交前至少保留 2 条候选路径，实体查询先 grep entity_index.json）、跨分支比较与 concise 答案格式；典型 2–3 轮完成（直接下潜 或 跨分支探索）。
- *对比与评估*：同 LLM/同评测协议下比较 BM25、Dense(Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B+FAISS)、Hybrid(RRF)、RAPTOR(UMAP+GMM+BIC，摘要器同为 Sonnet 4.6)、Agentic(最多 10 轮三检索工具)；七指标（Token F1、BERTScore-F1、Factuality、Faithfulness、CtxR、CtxP、Hallucination Rate<0.6）加 turns 与 $/query；跨方法 Context Recall 统一按“最后 5 篇文档、8k 字符窗口”标准化；胜/平/负按逐查询配对 bootstrap（10,000 次重采样，p<0.05）判定。

Result: **WixQA 主结果（200 查询）**：Corpus2Skill 在 F1(0.456)、BERT(0.862)、Factuality(0.767)、CtxR(0.708)、CtxP(0.829) 全面最优，对 RAPTOR 的 F1 领先经 bootstrap 显著（p<0.0001），三次独立服务运行方差仅 ±0.002；Faithfulness 0.859、Hallucination 4.5% 优于 Agentic（0.528 / 50%）但略逊单次检索器（0.83–0.91 / ≈0%，后者因只回喂一篇有据片段）。成本 $0.153/查询（启用 ephemeral prompt caching），约为 Agentic 的 1.9×、单次检索器的 13–22×；去缓存则升至 $0.302，排序不变。
**11 数据集泛化**：WixQA + 10 个 RAGBench 子集（构造 expanded retrieval pool，221–12,727 篇）上，配对检验结果为 5 胜（WixQA +0.059、Emanual +0.061、DelucionQA +0.034、ExpertQA +0.027、ClapNQ +0.135，p≤0.010）、3 平（CovidQA、FinQA、TechQA）、3 负（HAGRID −0.167、TatQA −0.116、CUAD −0.052，p<0.001）。差异由语料结构决定：单域、原子化文档语料（可恢复的分类体系）导航取胜；异质/数值表格语料为统计平局；开放域事实型（HAGRID 摘要塌缩为泛化标签、TatQA 单元近重复、CUAD 长文档被按条款切分）导航失败。
**消融**：p=20 的“宽树”崩塌至 F1 0.382、Hallucination 24.5%（复现 TatQA 失败模式）；探索预算 5/10/20 轮对质量影响 <0.005 F1，主要改善 grounding（幻觉 6.5%→4.5%）；服务侧换 Haiku 4.5 降本 39%（$0.093）仍保留 92% F1 但幻觉升至 4×；编译侧换 Haiku 保留 97% F1 且编译成本降至 1/5；组件分解显示增益主要来自导航结构——去掉 entity index F1 0.456→0.312、软分配→0.333、exemplars→0.401，而 verify-and-repartition 中性、summary cards/8B 编码器变化不显著；2×2 编码器交叉实验证明优势与编码器规模无关（8B 编译 0.458 vs 0.6B 编译 0.461，p=0.37），对 RAPTOR 的领先在 0.6B/8B 匹配下均显著。
**规模扩展**：CovidQA 池扩至 5 万篇（8×）时层级恰增一层、编译成本次线性（$1.08/1k 文档 vs $3.33/1k），全部查询无导航错误，F1 0.191 仍居首（与 Dense 并列 p=0.74，高于 BM25 p=0.04）。
**失败分析**：45/200（22%）低分查询中，19 例为顶层路由错误（导航缺失）、16 例为过度检索导致上下文稀释、5 例为综合推断错误、5 例为域外查询拒答。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是把“检索”重述为“对已编译 skill 树的智能体文件浏览”，并证明“可导航性”是独立于嵌入检索质量的一维能力：在边界清晰、主题层级可恢复的企业知识库上，导航式 RAG 显著优于扁平检索、RAPTOR 式层级检索与工具型 agentic RAG，且成本优势来自结构本身（用 Haiku 编译+服务仍胜扁平基线），50k 文档规模下结论稳定；同时明确界定其适用边界——导航是检索的补充原语而非普适替代，在开放域事实池与同质表格语料上应保留扁平检索/RAPTOR，并建议按查询做“导航 vs 检索”的混合路由。作者同时承认三点局限：token 成本随轮次累积（无缓存时近乎翻倍）、顶层路由错误是主要失败源、语料变更需重新编译且不支持增量更新。对企业落地的启示：编译产物为纯 skill 目录，无需向量数据库即可直接接入现有 agent 部署，适合高价值、强 grounding 要求的客服/合规类问答，量大低风险场景仍宜用单次检索。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds LLM responses in external evidence but treats the model as a passive consumer of search results: it never sees how the corpus is organized or what it has not yet retrieved, limiting its ability to backtrack or combine scattered evidence. We present Corpus2Skill, which distills a document corpus into a hierarchical skill directory offline and lets an LLM agent navigate it at serve time. The compilation pipeline iteratively clusters documents, generates LLM-written summaries at each level, and materializes the result as a tree of navigable skill files. At serve time, the agent receives a bird's-eye view of the corpus, drills into topic branches via progressively finer summaries, and retrieves full documents by ID. Because the hierarchy is explicitly visible, the agent can reason about where to look, backtrack from unproductive paths, and combine evidence across branches. On WixQA, an enterprise customer-support benchmark for RAG, Corpus2Skill outperforms dense retrieval, RAPTOR, and agentic RAG baselines across all quality metrics.

</details>


### [294] [Adaptive Query Routing: A Tier-Based Framework for Hybrid Retrieval Across Financial, Legal, and Medical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14222)
*Afshan Hashmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了三种检索架构（Vector RAG、Tree Reasoning、Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval）在金融、法律、医学领域的性能差异，发现Tree Reasoning总体最优（0.900），但不同复杂度的查询存在显著差异：Vector RAG擅长多文档综合，Hybrid AHR在交叉引用和多section查询上表现最佳，Tree-Vector差距在真实SEC文档上扩大至11.7个百分点。研究支持开发能根据查询复杂度和文档结构动态选择策略的自适应检索系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG系统在金融文档QA中表现不一，缺乏系统性比较；现有评估缺乏针对查询复杂度的分层分析和跨领域验证；需要理解不同检索范式各自的优劣边界。

Method: (1) 实现并评估三种检索架构：Vector RAG、Tree Reasoning、Hybrid AHR；(2) 构建四层查询复杂度基准；(3) 使用GPT-4驱动的LLM-as-judge评估；(4) 在FinanceBench（150个专家标注问题，基于真实SEC 10-K/10-Q文档）验证。

Result: Tree Reasoning总体得分最高（0.900），但无单一范式主导所有层级：Vector RAG在多文档综合（Tier 4）胜出（0.900），Hybrid AHR在交叉引用（0.850）和多section查询（0.929）表现最佳。树基和混合方法的交叉引用召回率达100%，而向量搜索仅91.7%。FinanceBench验证：Tree Reasoning 0.938，Hybrid AHR 0.901，Vector RAG 0.821，真实文档上Tree-Vector差距扩大至11.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 不同检索范式各有优势域，应根据查询复杂度和文档结构动态选择检索策略；交叉引用能力是关键差异点，向量搜索存在召回率瓶颈；研究为自适应检索系统的发展提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become the standard paradigm for grounding Large Language Model outputs in external knowledge. Lumer et al. [1] presented the first systematic evaluation comparing vector-based agentic RAG against hierarchical node-based reasoning systems for financial document QA across 1,200 SEC filings, finding vector-based systems achieved a 68% win rate. Concurrently, the PageIndex framework [2] demonstrated 98.7% accuracy on FinanceBench through purely reasoning-based retrieval. This paper extends their work by: (i) implementing and evaluating three retrieval architectures: Vector RAG, Tree Reasoning, and the proposed Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval (AHR) across financial, legal, and medical domains; (ii) introducing a four-tier query complexity benchmark; and (iii) employing GPT-4-powered LLM-as-judge evaluation. Experiments reveal that Tree Reasoning achieves the highest overall score (0.900), but no single paradigm dominates across all tiers: Vector RAG wins on multi-document synthesis (Tier 4, score 0.900), while the Hybrid AHR achieves the best performance on cross-reference (0.850) and multi-section queries (0.929). Cross-reference recall reaches 100% for tree-based and hybrid approaches versus 91.7% for vector search, quantifying a critical capability gap. Validation on FinanceBench (150 expert-annotated questions on real SEC 10-K and 10-Q filings) confirms and strengthens these findings: Tree Reasoning scores 0.938, Hybrid AHR 0.901, and Vector RAG 0.821, with the Tree--Vector quality gap widening to 11.7 percentage points on real-world documents. These findings support the development of adaptive retrieval systems that dynamically select strategies based on query complexity and document structure. All code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [295] [TRACE: A Conversational Framework for Sustainable Tourism Recommendation with Agentic Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14223)
*Ashmi Banerjee,Adithi Satish,Wolfgang Wörndl,Yashar Deldjoo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TRACE是一个基于多智能体LLM的可持续旅游推荐框架，通过反事实解释和交互式追问引导用户选择环保目的地，同时保持推荐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统旅游推荐系统过度优化相关性和便利性，导致热门目的地过度拥挤、碳密集型出行选择被强化的问题。

Method: 采用模块化编排器-工作器架构的TRACE框架，包含专门智能体用于：1) 挖掘潜在可持续性偏好；2) 构建结构化用户画像；3) 生成兼顾相关性与环境影响的推荐。核心技术为智能体反事实解释和LLM驱动的澄清问题。

Result: 用户研究和语义对齐分析表明，TRACE有效支持可持续决策，同时保持推荐质量和交互响应性。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了通过非强制性引导（nudging）可促进旅游业的绿色转型。

Abstract: Traditional conversational travel recommender systems primarily optimize for user relevance and convenience, often reinforcing popular, overcrowded destinations and carbon-intensive travel choices. To address this, we present TRACE (Tourism Recommendation with Agentic Counterfactual Explanations), a multi-agent, LLM-based framework that promotes sustainable tourism through interactive nudging. TRACE uses a modular orchestrator-worker architecture where specialized agents elicit latent sustainability preferences, construct structured user personas, and generate recommendations that balance relevance with environmental impact.
  A key innovation lies in its use of agentic counterfactual explanations and LLM-driven clarifying questions, which together surface greener alternatives and refine understanding of intent, fostering user reflection without coercion. User studies and semantic alignment analyses demonstrate that TRACE effectively supports sustainable decision-making while preserving recommendation quality and interactive responsiveness. TRACE is implemented on Google's Agent Development Kit, with full code, Docker setup, prompts, and a publicly available demo video to ensure reproducibility.
  A project summary, including all resources, prompts, and demo access, is available at https://ashmibanerjee.github.io/trace-chatbot.

</details>


### [296] [FRESCO: Benchmarking and Optimizing Re-rankers for Evolving Semantic Conflict in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14227)
*Sohyun An,Hayeon Lee,Shuibenyang Yuan,Chun-cheng Jason Chen,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Vijai Mohan,Alexander Min*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FRESCO是一个用于评估RAG重排序器在时间动态环境中的基准测试，通过配对时效性查询与历史Wikipedia修订版，揭示了现有重排序器对旧文档的偏差问题，并通过指令优化框架实现了27%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG基准测试主要在静态设置下评估重排序器，无法充分评估信息演变时的性能——这是一个关键缺口，因为现实世界的系统常常需要在时间上不同的证据中进行选择。

Method: 通过将时效性查询与历史Wikipedia修订版配对，构建FRESCO基准测试；进一步使用指令优化框架识别Pareto最优指令，在演化和非演化知识任务间取得平衡。

Result: 揭示了现有重排序器的一致性失败模式：对旧文档存在强烈偏差，即使这些文档在事实上已过时；通过指令优化可获得高达27%的演化知识任务提升。

Conclusion: FRESCO有效填补了RAG重排序器时序动态评估的空白，指令优化框架能够缓解重排序器对过时信息的偏好问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is a key approach to mitigating the temporal staleness of large language models (LLMs) by grounding responses in up-to-date evidence. Within the RAG pipeline, re-rankers play a pivotal role in selecting the most useful documents from retrieved candidates. However, existing benchmarks predominantly evaluate re-rankers in static settings and do not adequately assess performance under evolving information -- a critical gap, as real-world systems often must choose among temporally different pieces of evidence. To address this limitation, we introduce FRESCO (Factual Recency and Evolving Semantic COnflict), a benchmark for evaluating re-rankers in temporally dynamic contexts. By pairing recency-seeking queries with historical Wikipedia revisions, FRESCO tests whether re-rankers can prioritize factually recent evidence while maintaining semantic relevance. Our evaluation reveals a consistent failure mode across existing re-rankers: a strong bias toward older, semantically rich documents, even when they are factually obsolete. We further investigate an instruction optimization framework to mitigate this issue. By identifying Pareto-optimal instructions that balance Evolving and Non-Evolving Knowledge tasks, we obtain gains of up to 27% on Evolving Knowledge tasks while maintaining competitive performance on Non-Evolving Knowledge tasks.

</details>


### [297] [Evaluation of Agents under Simulated AI Marketplace Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14256)
*To Eun Kim,Alireza Salemi,Hamed Zamani,Fernando Diaz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"市场评估"（Marketplace Evaluation）框架，针对信息访问系统的竞争性部署场景，通过模拟市场环境中的重复交互和用户/智能体偏好演化，实现超越传统静态准确率指标的纵向评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估方法假设系统独立运行，采用静态基准测试和准确率指标，无法反映真实市场中的竞争效应（如早期采用优势、市场垄断）和动态交互（如用户切换、路由决策），导致难以预测部署后的实际表现。

Method: 引入基于仿真的"市场评估"范式，将信息访问系统作为竞争市场的参与者，通过模拟反复交互和演化的偏好，实现纵向评估，补充传统准确性指标。

Result: 可生成市场级指标（如用户留存率、市场份额），扩展并补充传统准确率指标，涵盖TREC等评估活动的采纳与优化。

Conclusion: 框架已形式化定义，并提出围绕市场仿真、指标体系、优化方法及评估campaign应用的研究议程，为信息检索系统评估提供新范式。

Abstract: Modern information access ecosystems consist of mixtures of systems, such as retrieval systems and large language models, and increasingly rely on marketplaces to mediate access to models, tools, and data, making competition between systems inherent to deployment. In such settings, outcomes are shaped not only by benchmark quality but also by competitive pressure, including user switching, routing decisions, and operational constraints. Yet evaluation is still largely conducted on static benchmarks with accuracy-focused measures that assume systems operate in isolation. This mismatch makes it difficult to predict post-deployment success and obscures competitive effects such as early-adoption advantages and market dominance. We introduce Marketplace Evaluation, a simulation-based paradigm that evaluates information access systems as participants in a competitive marketplace. By simulating repeated interactions and evolving user and agent preferences, the framework enables longitudinal evaluation and marketplace-level metrics, such as retention and market share, that complement and can extend beyond traditional accuracy-based metrics. We formalize the framework and outline a research agenda, motivated by business and economics, around marketplace simulation, metrics, optimization, and adoption in evaluation campaigns like TREC.

</details>


### [298] [A Unified Model and Document Representation for On-Device Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14403)
*Julian Killingback,Ofer Meshi,Henry Li,Hamed Zamani,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一模型，将检索与上下文压缩融合，使用共享表示同时完成检索和生成任务。该方法仅需传统RAG十分之一的上下文长度即可达到相当性能，且存储需求不增加，开创了统一模型进行检索与上下文压缩的先河。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG依赖云端服务器，存在隐私泄露、运维成本高、延迟大、需联网等局限。虽然本地部署可解决这些问题，但设备端面临内存和磁盘空间的严格约束：上下文窗口需限制以控制KV缓存，存储嵌入需精简以节省空间。

Method: 提出统一模型压缩RAG上下文，并复用相同表示进行检索。采用单一模型同时处理检索和生成任务，实现表示共享。

Result: 在仅使用平均1/10上下文长度的情况下，模型性能与传​​统RAG阅读器相当，且相比多向量检索模型不增加存储需求。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现检索与上下文压缩的统一模型与表示共享，为进一步整合不同模型以优化设备端性能提供了新思路。

Abstract: Traditional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) approaches generally assume that retrieval and generation occur on powerful servers removed from the end user. While this reduces local hardware constraints, it introduces significant drawbacks: privacy concerns regarding data access, recurring maintenance and storage costs, increased latency, and the necessity of an internet connection. On-device RAG addresses these challenges by executing the entire pipeline locally, making it ideal for querying sensitive personal information such as financial documents, contact details, and medical history. However, on-device deployment necessitates a delicate balance between limited memory and disk space. Specifically, the context size provided to the generative model must be restricted to manage KV cache and attention memory usage, while the size of stored embeddings must be minimized to preserve disk space. In this work, we propose a unified model that compresses the RAG context and utilizes the same representations for retrieval. This approach minimizes disk utilization compared to using separate representations, while significantly reducing the context size required for generation. With an average of 1/10 of the context, our model matches the performance of a traditional RAG reader without increasing storage requirements compared to a multi-vector retrieval model. This approach represents the first model to unify retrieval and context compression using a shared model and representation. We believe this work will inspire further consolidation of distinct models to optimize on-device performance.

</details>


### [299] [Controlling Authority Retrieval: A Missing Retrieval Objective for Authority-Governed Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14488)
*Andre Bacellar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Controlling Authority Retrieval (CAR) 问题，用于解决知识积累领域（法律、药品监管、软件安全）中后期文件形式上废除早期文件但语义可能相距甚远的情况。核心贡献包括：CAR正确性特征的必要充分条件定理，以及基于对抗性置换论证的范围可识别性上界。通过安全公告、SCOTUS判决和FDA药品记录三个真实语料库验证，并开源了基准数据集和代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在法律、药品监管、软件安全等领域，后期文件可以正式废除早期文件，但语义上可能相差甚远。传统检索方法（如 argmax_d s(q,d)）无法处理这种"权威性替代"关系，导致Dense RAG在39%的查询中产生"未修补"的错误声明。

Method: 1. 将CAR形式化为恢复权威闭包的活跃边界 front(cl(A_k(q)))；2. 提出两阶段检索框架（Two-Stage）；3. 定理4给出充分必要条件（边界包含+无忽略替代者）；4. 命题2建立phi(q)作为最坏情况上界，通过对抗性置换论证证明。

Result: Dense方法：安全公告TCA@5=0.270，SCOTUS=0.172，FDA=0.064；Two-Stage方法大幅提升至0.975、0.926、0.774。GPT-4o-mini实验显示，Two-Stage将"未修补"错误声明率从39%降至16%。

Conclusion: CAR问题具有独特的数学结构，不同于传统argmax检索。通过识别边界和替代者关系，两阶段方法可有效解决权威性文档检索难题，具有重要的下游应用价值。

Abstract: In any domain where knowledge accumulates under formal authority -- law, drug regulation, software security -- a later document can formally void an earlier one while remaining semantically distant from it. We formalize this as Controlling Authority Retrieval (CAR): recovering the active frontier front(cl(A_k(q))) of the authority closure of the semantic anchor set -- a different mathematical problem from argmax_d s(q,d). The two central results are: Theorem 4 (CAR-Correctness Characterization) gives necessary-and-sufficient conditions on any retrieved set R for TCA(R,q)=1 -- frontier inclusion and no-ignored-superseder -- independent of how R was produced. Proposition 2 (Scope Identifiability Upper Bound) establishes phi(q) as a hard worst-case ceiling: for any scope-indexed algorithm, TCA@k <= phi(q) * R_anchor(q), proved by an adversarial permutation argument. Three independent real-world corpora validate the proved structure: security advisories (Dense TCA@5=0.270, two-stage 0.975), SCOTUS overruling pairs (Dense=0.172, two-stage 0.926), FDA drug records (Dense=0.064, two-stage 0.774). A GPT-4o-mini experiment shows the downstream cost: Dense RAG produces explicit "not patched" claims for 39% of queries where a patch exists; Two-Stage cuts this to 16%. Four benchmark datasets, domain adapters, and a single-command scorer are released at https://github.com/andremir/car-retrieval.

</details>


### [300] [NewsTorch: A PyTorch-based Toolkit for Learner-oriented News Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14510)
*Rongyao Wang,Veronica Liesaputra,Zhiyi Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NewsTorch是一个基于PyTorch的新闻推荐工具包，旨在帮助学习者获取新闻推荐的概念理解和实践经验。该工具包采用模块化、解耦、可扩展的架构设计，提供友好的GUI平台，支持数据集下载预处理、模型训练验证测试以及标准化评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏专门面向学习者的新闻推荐工具包，制约了新闻推荐研究的进展。研究人员需要一个能够降低入门门槛、提供标准化评估的解决方案。

Method: 基于PyTorch开发，采用模块化、解耦、可扩展的框架设计；集成GUI平台实现数据集下载与预处理；支持最先进的神经新闻推荐模型训练与评估。

Result: 提供了一套完整的新闻推荐实验环境，包含数据集管理、模型训练、验证测试等全流程功能，支持标准化评估指标以确保公平比较和实验可复现性。

Conclusion: NewsTorch作为一个开源工具包，可帮助学习者快速掌握新闻推荐技术，同时为研究人员提供公平、可复现的实验基准。

Abstract: News recommender systems are devised to alleviate the information overload, attracting more and more researchers' attention in recent years. The lack of a dedicated learner-oriented news recommendation toolkit hinders the advancement of research in news recommendation. We propose a PyTorch-based news recommendation toolkit called NewsTorch, developed to support learners in acquiring both conceptual understanding and practical experience. This toolkit provides a modular, decoupled, and extensible framework with a learner-friendly GUI platform that supports dataset downloading and preprocessing. It also enables training, validation, and testing of state-of-the-art neural news recommendation models with standardized evaluation metrics, ensuring fair comparison and reproducible experiments. Our open-source toolkit is released on Github: https://github.com/whonor/NewsTorch.

</details>


### [301] [Behavior-Aware Dual-Channel Preference Learning for Heterogeneous Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14581)
*Jing Xiao,Dongqi Wu,Liwei Pan,Yawen Luo,Weike Pan,Zhong Ming*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出行为感知双通道偏好学习框架 BDPL，用于异构序列推荐（HSR）：通过定制化行为感知子图 + 级联式 GNN 编码器刻画多行为转移关系，并以长短期双通道对比学习（短-term 用目标物品作正样本、长-term 用子序列交换增强）分别建模短期与长期偏好，最后用自适应门控融合后预测下一个购买物品。在 Tmall、UB、JD 三个数据集上全面超越 15 个 SOTA 基线（HR/NDCG 提升 0.25%~17.61%），整体时间复杂度与 SASRec 相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构序列推荐（HSR）需从浏览、加购、购买等多行为交互中学习动态行为依赖，但仍存在两大问题：(1) 真实数据的内在稀疏性削弱推荐性能，辅助行为虽能部分缓解却引入噪声，且目标行为（如购买）的稀疏性未解决；(2) 现有对比学习增强方法多依赖单一行为类型或显式数据增强/结构改造，忽略了细粒度用户偏好，容易引入噪声与数据不一致，导致偏好信号不够语义化。此外，已有对比学习方法未区分不同行为类型的异质语义差异。

Method: 1）嵌入初始化：物品、行为、位置三类可学习嵌入。2）行为感知图构建：构建 G_e 与 G_p 两张异构图，转移类型含 e2e±、p2p±、e2p±；其中异质转移只取「辅助行为→目标行为」方向，并将原序列按购买行为切分为「以辅助行为起始、以购买结束」的子序列，使一次购买与该子序列中其前所有辅助行为建立多跳 e2p 连接（而非仅相邻项），以捕捉长程依赖。3）行为感知图编码器：对每个节点按各转移类型做均值池化聚合，再用注意力对转移类型自适应加权，逐层传播并累加各层表示；随后以目标行为表示经特征变换 W_p 作为辅助行为第 0 层输入，实现「目标→辅助」的级联（先做 p2p 再做 e2e/e2p），最后对各行为表示平均池化得多行为嵌入。4）短期偏好：行为融合注意力编码器（SAB = MSA + FFN + LayerNorm/Dropout/残差）取最后一行作为 z_u^s；以目标物品表示与 z_u^s 构成正样本对，batch 内其他目标物品为负样本，构成短期对比损失 L_cl^s。5）长期偏好：目标行为导向门控 H' = H ⊗ σ(HW_g1 + W_g2 h_b) 融合目标行为信息；再用堆叠序列捕获块 SCB（沿序列维非线性映射 + GELU/LayerNorm/残差）提取序列级模式，对序列所有物品表示平均池化得 z_u^l；采用 MBASR 的子序列交换策略（按位置邻近度加权采样目标子序列并互换）生成增广序列 Ŝ_u，以 z_u^l 与 ẑ_u^l 为正样本对、batch 内其他序列为负样本，构成长期对比损失 L_cl^l。6）偏好感知融合：β = σ([z_u^s || z_u^l] W_f)，o_u = β·z_u^s + (1-β)·z_u^l；与物品嵌入做点积后 softmax，交叉熵排序损失；总损失 L = L_rec + λ1 L_cl^s + λ2 L_cl^l，多任务联合优化。

Result: 在 Tmall、UB、JD 三个真实电商数据集上与 15 个基线（GRU4Rec、Caser、NextItNet、SASRec、RIB、BAR、GPG4HSR、NextIP、BMLP、GHTID、M-GPT、GEAR、CL4SRec、DuoRec、DCRec）对比，BDPL 在三个数据集上几乎所有 HR@{5,10,20} 与 NDCG@{5,10,20} 指标均取得最好结果，相对最强基线的平均提升为 0.25%~17.61%（在 Tmall 上优势更明显，说明更擅长捕捉长程依赖）。消融实验显示：去掉行为感知图编码器(BGE)、仅保留单一偏好通道(SPL/LPL)、去掉短期或长期对比学习都会导致性能明显下降，其中去掉任一偏好通道下降最大，说明长短期互补与图编码器均有贡献；级联方向实验(tar2aux vs aux2tar)表明由目标行为向辅助行为级联几乎在所有数据集与指标上更优，验证了「用更纯的目标行为引导/净化辅助行为」的设计；参数敏感性显示 SAB 与 SCB 数量增加持续提升 HR@10，而 GNN 层数取 1 即已饱和（因行为感知图特征已较丰富，可缓解过拟合）；整体时间复杂度 O(|S_u|d²+|S_u|²d) 与 SASRec 相同，效率良好。

Conclusion: BDPL 通过「行为感知多跳子图 + 目标→辅助级联 GNN 编码器 + 长短期双通道偏好级对比学习 + 自适应门控融合」的组合，在异构序列推荐中有效刻画了行为异质性与行为转移关系，并显著缓解了数据稀疏问题；偏好级（而非样本级）对比学习被视为提升表示学习质量的关键。局限性方面：仅在电商三数据集验证、以购买为唯一目标行为、对辅助行为噪声的抑制依赖级联启发式设计（未显式去噪机制）、未来工作将扩展到借助 LLM 的多模态异构序列推荐。

Abstract: Heterogeneous sequential recommendation (HSR) aims to learn dynamic behavior dependencies from the diverse behaviors of user-item interactions to facilitate precise sequential recommendation. Despite many efforts yielding promising achievements, there are still challenges in modeling heterogeneous behavior data. One significant issue is the inherent sparsity of a real-world data, which can weaken the recommendation performance. Although auxiliary behaviors (e.g., clicks) partially address this problem, they inevitably introduce some noise, and the sparsity of the target behavior (e.g., purchases) remains unresolved. Additionally, contrastive learning-based augmentation in existing methods often focuses on a single behavior type, overlooking fine-grained user preferences and losing valuable information. To address these challenges, we have meticulously designed a behavior-aware dual-channel preference learning framework (BDPL). This framework begins with the construction of customized behavior-aware subgraphs to capture personalized behavior transition relationships, followed by a novel cascade-structured graph neural network to aggregate node context information. We then model and enhance user representations through a preference-level contrastive learning paradigm, considering both long-term and short-term preferences. Finally, we fuse the overall preference information using an adaptive gating mechanism to predict the next item the user will interact with under the target behavior. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority of our BDPL over the state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [302] [CPGRec+: A Balance-oriented Framework for Personalized Video Game Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14586)
*Xiping Li,Aier Yang,Jianghong Ma,Kangzhe Liu,Shanshan Feng,Haijun Zhang,Yi Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CPGRec+ 是作者在 CPGRec 基础上的增强版个性化游戏推荐框架，针对 GNN 推荐中"准确率优先导致推荐同质化"与"忽视玩家-游戏交互粒度差异"两大痛点，新提出 PER（Preference-informed Edge Reweighting）模块与 PRG（Preference-informed Representation Generation）模块：前者用 Box-Cox 变换 + Z-score 归一化将游玩时长与游戏平均评分映射到同一标准正态空间，借助 Fisher 分布检验判定"显著兴趣/显著厌恶"并赋符号，用信息内容量化偏好强度以抑制过平滑；后者用 LLM（Qwen2.5）结合平均评分生成游戏描述、结合个人-全局兴趣对比生成玩家描述，经 M3-Embedding 与 MLP 融合回表示。在两个 Steam 数据集上，CPGRec+ 在准确率上超越所有 SOTA，同时保持 CPGRec 的多样性优势（长尾覆盖率等指标更优），实现准确-多样平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1) 现有 GNN 类游戏推荐方法重准确轻多样，易产生"过滤气泡"，推荐结果集中于头部游戏；2) CPGRec 假设所有历史交互同等反映玩家偏好，忽略了"玩家游玩时长（个人兴趣）"与"游戏平均评分（全局兴趣）"之间的差异性，而这种差异恰是刻画个人兴趣/厌恶的关键信号；3) 非交互特定（non-interaction-specific）的边权重配合 GCN 的低通平滑性会加剧过平滑，导致玩家与游戏表示趋于同质、建模次优；4) 现有 LLM 推荐方法多忽视平均评分这一全局兴趣信号，也未充分利用 LLM 的推理能力从交互数据中推断个人偏好；5) 数据显示 Steam 存在显著长尾效应（top-30% 热门游戏即覆盖 93% 玩家），如何在准确率与多样性间取得平衡仍是开放问题。

Method: 整体在 CPGRec 四模块（SGC 严格连接提升准确、CNA 连通性增强提升多样、PENR 流行度引导重加权、NSR 负样本分数重加权训练）之上叠加两个新模块。\n\n(1) PER 模块（准确驱动）：两步。① 符号判定——先用 Box-Cox 变换（λ 由 SciPy 极大似然估计，固定超参）再接 Z-score 归一化，把游玩时长 T 与游戏平均评分 R 映射到同一标准正态空间（用单样本 KS 检验验证：时长 p=0.5950、评分 p=0.5024，均不拒绝标准正态）；取统计量 F=T²/R² 服从 Fisher(d1,d2)=(1,1) 分布，用其上分位点 Q_α 判定该交互是否显著偏离全局偏好，若显著且 T>0 记为"显著兴趣"赋 +1，显著且 T≤0 记为"显著厌恶"赋 −1，否则置 0。② 幅度量化——引入 Shannon 信息内容 I=−log P(T=t,R=r)，得到偏好感知边权 Θ_e^Pr = sign · I；最终与流行度引导边权 Θ_e^Po 相加形成平衡边权 Θ_e^RWT，代入 LightGCN 卷积公式。\n\n(2) PRG 模块（准确驱动）：① 评分驱动的游戏描述生成——提示词含游戏标题、平均评分（反映全局兴趣）、价格、发行时间（缺失评分以全局均值替代），由 Qwen2.5 生成文本描述；② 偏好驱动的玩家描述生成——将游戏描述 + 该玩家的归一化时长 t_{u,i} 与归一化评分 r_i 组织成提示，指示 LLM 对比个人兴趣与全局兴趣推断个人偏好；③ 用 M3-Embedding 嵌入文本描述，MLP^D-Align 对齐语义空间与原表示空间，MLP^D-Integ 融合更新玩家/游戏表示。描述生成与嵌入可离线预计算，仅对齐与融合在推理时执行。\n\n此外给出 Fisher 分布相关的理论推导、PENR 的 2×2 玩具案例影响指数分析、50 节点子图的谱能量分布分析、α 敏感性分析、MLP/线性/门控融合对比与具体玩家案例研究。

Result: 两个 Steam 数据集（Steam I：390.9 万玩家/2675 游戏/9521 万交互；Steam II：33.5 万玩家/13047 游戏/368.6 万交互）上：\n- 准确率：CPGRec+ 在 NDCG/Recall/Hit/Precision@5、@10 上全面超过 LightGCN、SURGE、BIGCF、MVGNN、SCGRec 及 MMR/EDUA/DDGraph/DGCN/DGRec/EXPLORE。例如 Steam I 权衡版 NDCG@10 0.4691（CPGRec 0.4547，SCGRec 0.4660），Steam II NDCG@10 0.3427。\n- 多样性：多样性版 CPGRec+ 整体与 CPGRec 相当并优于其他多样性模型；CPGRec+ 相对 CPGRec 在 Conventional Coverage、Tail Coverage、Tail 等长尾/全局指标上更优（Steam I Tail Coverage@10 0.2617 vs 0.2336），而分类别 Coverage/Entropy 略降，体现"偏好感知"带来的长尾收益与类别多样性的轻微折衷。\n- 消融：移除 PER 或 PRG 均导致各准确率指标明显下降，同时 CC 与 Tail 指标显著回落，验证两模块对准确与平衡的贡献；严格连接优于 raw 连接。\n- 效率：PER 与 PENR 权重可离线预计算，PRG 文本生成与嵌入可预计算，在线复杂度与 LightGCN/DGCN 等相当、低于 DGRec/SURGE，唯一更低的是 MVGNN。\n- 解释性分析：谱能量分布显示无 PER 时表示能量集中于低频（过平滑），加入 PER 后保留高频成分；案例中仅 PER 或仅 PRG 都能召回目标游戏，二者协同效果最佳。

Conclusion: CPGRec+ 通过在 CPGRec 之上引入 PER 与 PRG 两个偏好感知模块，把"个人兴趣 vs 全局兴趣"的比较显式地建模到图结构（符号化边权）与语义表示（LLM 生成描述）中，既缓解了 GNN 的过平滑问题，又提升了个性化精度，并在两个 Steam 数据集上同时取得优于 SOTA 的准确率与可比/更优的多样性，说明准确率与多样性的二元对立并非不可避免，良好校准的推荐系统可实现二者平衡。局限与未来方向：图结构为静态构建，未处理偏好随时间的漂移与冷启动；LLM 生成玩家画像的可靠性存疑，需引入不确定性量化与自演化机制；可探索跨模态游戏理解以弥合语义鸿沟。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of gaming industry requires advanced recommender systems tailored to its dynamic landscape. Existing Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based methods primarily prioritize accuracy over diversity, overlooking their inherent trade-off. To address this, we previously proposed CPGRec, a balance-oriented gaming recommender system. However, CPGRec fails to account for critical disparities in player-game interactions, which carry varying significance in reflecting players' personal preferences and may exacerbate over-smoothness issues inherent in GNN-based models. Moreover, existing approaches underutilize the reasoning capabilities and extensive knowledge of large language models (LLMs) in addressing these limitations. To bridge this gap, we propose two new modules. First, Preference-informed Edge Reweighting (PER) module assigns signed edge weights to qualitatively distinguish significant player interests and disinterests while then quantitatively measuring preference strength to mitigate over-smoothing in graph convolutions. Second, Preference-informed Representation Generation (PRG) module leverages LLMs to generate contextualized descriptions of games and players by reasoning personal preferences from comparing global and personal interests, thereby refining representations of players and games. Experiments on \textcolor{black}{two Steam datasets} demonstrate CPGRec+'s superior accuracy and diversity over state-of-the-art models. The code is accessible at https://github.com/HsipingLi/CPGRec-Plus.

</details>


### [303] [Category-based and Popularity-guided Video Game Recommendation: A Balance-oriented Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14598)
*Xiping Li,Jianghong Ma,Kangzhe Liu,Shanshan Feng,Haijun Zhang,Yutong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CPGRec是一种面向视频游戏的新型推荐框架，通过准确度驱动、多样性驱动和综合模块三个组件解决现有游戏推荐系统中准确度与多样性难以兼顾的问题，并提出负样本评分重加权方法平衡二者。在Steam数据集上验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频游戏推荐系统存在三个主要问题：(1)过度关注准确度而忽视多样性，导致推荐结果同质化；(2)游戏间关联建模不够严格；(3)多样性方法在邻居建模和消息传播中未能有效利用物品类别和流行度等关键信息。

Method: 提出CPGRec框架，包含三个模块：(1)准确度驱动模块通过更严格的方式连接游戏以提升推荐准确度；(2)多样性驱动模块在游戏图中连接不同类别的邻居节点，并利用热门游戏节点增强长尾游戏的影响力；(3)综合模块结合前两个模块，并采用负样本评分重加权方法实现准确度与多样性的平衡。实验在Steam数据集上进行。

Result: 实验结果表明CPGRec在游戏推荐任务上有效提升了推荐性能。

Conclusion: CPGRec框架通过多模块协同和负样本重加权策略，成功实现了准确度与多样性的有效平衡，为视频游戏推荐提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, the video game industry has experienced substantial growth, presenting players with a vast array of game choices. This surge in options has spurred the need for a specialized recommender system tailored for video games. However, current video game recommendation approaches tend to prioritize accuracy over diversity, potentially leading to unvaried game suggestions. In addition, the existing game recommendation methods commonly lack the ability to establish strict connections between games to enhance accuracy. Furthermore, many existing diversity-focused methods fail to leverage crucial item information, such as item category and popularity during neighbor modeling and message propagation. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel framework, called CPGRec, comprising three modules, namely accuracy-driven, diversity-driven, and comprehensive modules. The first module extends the state-of-the-art accuracy-focused game recommendation method by connecting games in a more stringent manner to enhance recommendation accuracy. The second module connects neighbors with diverse categories within the proposed game graph and harnesses the advantages of popular game nodes to amplify the influence of long-tail games within the player-game bipartite graph, thereby enriching recommendation diversity. The third module combines the above two modules and employs a new negative-sample rating score reweighting method to balance accuracy and diversity. Experimental results on the Steam dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method in improving game recommendations. The dataset and source codes are anonymously released at: https://github.com/CPGRec2024/CPGRec.git.

</details>


### [304] [Uncertainty-aware Generative Learning Path Recommendation with Cognition-Adaptive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14613)
*Xiangrui Xiong,Hang Liang,Baiyang Chen,Zifei Pan,Yanli Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: U-GLAD是一种基于扩散模型的不确定性感知学习路径推荐框架，通过高斯LSTM建模认知状态概率分布，并利用多头注意力机制实现目标导向的个性化推荐，从判别式排序转向生成式预测范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有学习路径推荐系统忽视历史交互不确定性（如猜测、失误）和缺乏对多样化学习目标适应能力的问题。

Method: 1) 高斯LSTM将认知状态建模为概率分布；2) 多头注意力目标导向概念编码器；3) 扩散模型生成下一最优概念的潜在表示

Result: 三个公开数据集上显著优于代表性基线方法，具备优秀的不确定性感知和目标驱动推荐能力

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了交互不确定性问题，通过生成式扩散模型实现了稳定、个性化的学习路径推荐。

Abstract: Learning Path Recommendation (LPR) is critical for personalized education, yet current methods often fail to account for historical interaction uncertainty (e.g., lucky guesses or accidental slips) and lack adaptability to diverse learning goals. We propose U-GLAD (Uncertainty-aware Generative Learning Path Recommendation with Cognition-Adaptive Diffusion). To address representation bias, the framework models cognitive states as probability distributions, capturing the learner's underlying true state via a Gaussian LSTM. To ensure highly personalized recommendation, a goal-oriented concept encoder utilizes multi-head attention and objective-specific transformations to dynamically align concept semantics with individual learning goals, generating uniquely tailored embeddings. Unlike traditional discriminative ranking approaches, our model employs a generative diffusion model to predict the latent representation of the next optimal concept. Extensive evaluations on three public datasets demonstrate that U-GLAD significantly outperforms representative baselines. Further analyses confirm its superior capability in perceiving interaction uncertainty and providing stable, goal-driven recommendation paths.

</details>


### [305] [Federated User Behavior Modeling for Privacy-Preserving LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14833)
*Lei Guo,Hongyun Yang,Pengjie Ren,Tong Chen,Hui Liu,Zhumin Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SF-UBM方法，解决隐私保护跨域推荐(PPCDR)中LLM应用的三大挑战：通过语义增强的联邦架构实现跨域对齐，通过事实-反事实知识蒸馏整合跨模态知识，通过软提示空间对齐融合协同过滤信号与LLM学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域推荐方法假设领域重叠且可访问，忽视了隐私保护问题。LLM在PPCDR场景中面临三大挑战：1)用户身份/行为数据不可跨域共享阻碍对齐；2)跨域数据模态异构性；3)传统协同过滤信号与LLM特征空间不一致。

Method: SF-UBM包含三个核心模块：1)语义增强联邦架构——用自然语言作为跨域桥梁，加密共享文本型物品表征，用户数据保留本地；2)Fact-counter知识蒸馏模块——整合领域无关与领域特定知识；3)软提示空间投影——将预学习用户偏好与跨域物品表征映射到软提示空间，对齐行为与语义空间。

Result: 在3对真实领域数据集上进行广泛实验，结果表明SF-UBM相比近期SOTA方法具有优越性。

Conclusion: SF-UBM通过语义增强的联邦架构结合知识蒸馏与软提示对齐，有效解决了隐私保护跨域推荐中LLM应用的核心挑战，实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models have shown great success in recommender systems. However, the limited and sparse nature of user data often restricts the LLM's ability to effectively model behavior patterns. To address this, existing studies have explored cross-domain solutions by conducting Cross-Domain Recommendation tasks. But previous methods typically assume domains are overlapped and can be accessed readily. None of the LLM methods address the privacy-preserving issues in the CDR settings, that is, Privacy-Preserving Cross-Domain Recommendation. Conducting non-overlapping PPCDR with LLM is challenging since: 1)The inability to share user identity or behavioral data across domains impedes effective cross-domain alignment. 2)The heterogeneity of data modalities across domains complicates knowledge integration. 3)Fusing collaborative filtering signals from traditional recommendation models with LLMs is difficult, as they operate within distinct feature spaces. To address the above issues, we propose SF-UBM, a Semantic-enhanced Federated User Behavior Modeling method. Specifically, to deal with Challenge 1, we leverage natural language as a universal bridge to connect disjoint domains via a semantic-enhanced federated architecture. Here, text-based item representations are encrypted and shared, while user-specific data remains local. To handle Challenge 2, we design a Fact-counter Knowledge Distillation module to integrate domain-agnostic knowledge with domain-specific knowledge, across different data modalities. To tackle Challenge 3, we project pre-learned user preferences and cross-domain item representations into the soft prompt space, aligning behavioral and semantic spaces for effective LLM learning. We conduct extensive experiments on three pairs of real-world domains, and the experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of SF-UBM compared to the recent SOTA methods.

</details>


### [306] [Well Begun is Half Done: Training-Free and Model-Agnostic Semantically Guaranteed User Representation Initialization for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14839)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Hewei Wang,Jianheng Tang,Wei Wang,Xiping Hu,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-URInit方法，通过整合用户交互物品的模态特征和对应聚类的全局特征，为用户构建语义丰富的初始表示，从而弥合多模态推荐系统中用户与物品表示之间的语义鸿沟。该方法无需训练、模型无关，可无缝集成到现有多模态推荐模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽视了用户表示初始化的关键作用。由于物品天然关联丰富的多模态信息可被有效初始化，而用户只能随机初始化，导致两者之间存在显著的语义鸿沟，影响推荐性能。

Method: SG-URInit通过两个层面构建用户初始表示：(1) 局部层面：聚合用户交互物品的模态特征；(2) 全局层面：引入对应聚类的全局特征。采用聚类方法识别用户社区，确保初始表示同时捕获局部（物品级）和全局（聚类级）语义信息。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，将SG-URInit集成到先进多模态推荐模型中可显著提升推荐性能。消融实验验证了局部和全局语义融合的有效性。

Conclusion: SG-URInit可有效缓解物品冷启动问题，加速模型收敛，且无需额外训练计算开销。该方法具有高效性和实用性，为多模态推荐系统的用户表示学习提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal recommendations, which leverage diverse modality information to mitigate data sparsity and improve recommendation accuracy, have gained significant attention. However, existing multimodal recommendations overlook the critical role of user representation initialization. Unlike items, which are naturally associated with rich modality information, users lack such inherent information. Consequently, item representations initialized based on meaningful modality information and user representations initialized randomly exhibit a significant semantic gap.
  To this end, we propose a Semantically Guaranteed User Representation Initialization (SG-URInit). SG-URInit constructs the initial representation for each user by integrating both the modality features of the items they have interacted with and the global features of their corresponding clusters. SG-URInit enables the initialization of semantically enriched user representations that effectively capture both local (item-level) and global (cluster-level) semantics.
  Our SG-URInit is training-free and model-agnostic, meaning it can be seamlessly integrated into existing multimodal recommendation models without incurring any additional computational overhead during training. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that incorporating SG-URInit into advanced multimodal recommendation models significantly enhances recommendation performance. Furthermore, the results show that SG-URInit can further alleviate the item cold-start problem and also accelerate model convergence, making it an efficient and practical solution for multimodal recommendations.

</details>


### [307] [GenRec: A Preference-Oriented Generative Framework for Large-Scale Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14878)
*Yanyan Zou,Junbo Qi,Lunsong Huang,Yu Li,Kewei Xu,Jiabao Gao,Binglei Zhao,Xuanhua Yang,Sulong Xu,Shengjie Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GenRec是京东App上部署的偏好导向生成式推荐框架，通过Page-wise NTP任务、不对称Token Merger压缩和GRPO-SR强化学习方法，解决了生成式检索在工业级部署中的分页不一致、长序列编码成本高和策略对齐三个核心挑战，在生产环境中实现点击率提升9.5%、交易量提升8.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索(GR)通过下一token预测(NTP)为推荐系统带来新范式，但在工业级大规模部署时面临三大挑战：(1)分页请求机制导致相同模型输入产生不一致输出；(2)多token语义ID编码长用户行为序列成本过高；(3)生成策略难以与细粒度用户偏好信号对齐。

Method: 提出三大核心技术创新：(1)Page-wise NTP任务——对整个交互页面而非单个item监督，提供更密集梯度信号，解决point-wise训练的一对多歧义；(2)不对称线性Token Merger——压缩多token语义ID同时保留完整分辨率解码，输入长度降低约2倍；(3)GRPO-SR——结合组相对策略优化与NLL正则化的强化学习方法，配合融合密集奖励模型与相关性门控的混合奖励机制以缓解奖励黑客。

Result: Token Merger实现输入长度约2倍压缩，精度损失可忽略；月级在线A/B测试中，GenRec相较现有管线点击量提升9.5%，交易量提升8.7%。

Conclusion: GenRec成功在单一decoder-only架构内系统性解决了生成式检索的工业级部署挑战，验证了生成式推荐范式在真实生产环境中的有效性，为推荐系统从传统召回-排序架构向端到端生成式范式的演进提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Generative Retrieval (GR) offers a promising paradigm for recommendation through next-token prediction (NTP). However, scaling it to large-scale industrial systems introduces three challenges: (i) within a single request, the identical model inputs may produce inconsistent outputs due to the pagination request mechanism; (ii) the prohibitive cost of encoding long user behavior sequences with multi-token item representations based on semantic IDs, and (iii) aligning the generative policy with nuanced user preference signals. We present GenRec, a preference-oriented generative framework deployed on the JD App that addresses above challenges within a single decoder-only architecture. For training objective, we propose Page-wise NTP task, which supervises over an entire interaction page rather than each interacted item individually, providing denser gradient signal and resolving the one-to-many ambiguity of point-wise training. On the prefilling side, an asymmetric linear Token Merger compresses multi-token Semantic IDs in the prompt while preserving full-resolution decoding, reducing input length by ~2X with negligible accuracy loss. To further align outputs with user satisfaction, we introduce GRPO-SR, a reinforcement learning method that pairs Group Relative Policy Optimization with NLL regularization for training stability, and employs Hybrid Rewards combining a dense reward model with a relevance gate to mitigate reward hacking. In month-long online A/B tests serving production traffic, GenRec achieves 9.5% improvement in click count and 8.7% in transaction count over the existing pipeline.

</details>


### [308] [SAGER: Self-Evolving User Policy Skills for Recommendation Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14972)
*Zhen Tao,Riwei Lai,Chenyun Yu,Weixin Chen,Li Chen,Beibei Kong,Lei Cheng,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li,Qingqiang Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SAGER提出首个个性化推理策略框架，通过为每位用户配备可进化的自然语言策略文档，解决LLM推荐系统中个性化记忆与静态推理逻辑之间的不对称性问题，实现推理过程的动态演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM推荐智能体存在根本性瓶颈：虽能通过用户交互累积个性化语义记忆，但其决策推理逻辑始终保持静态、统一。导致推荐失败时，智能体仅更新记忆但无法改进决策过程，错误累积无法被结构性修正。

Method: 1) 双表征策略架构：将丰富的演化基质与极简的推理时注入解耦；2) 增量对比链式思维引擎：通过对比已选与未选商品诊断推理缺陷，同时保留累积的先验知识；3) 技能增强的列表式推理：在演化策略产生真正区分价值的位置创建细粒度决策边界。

Result: 在4个公开基准数据集上达到SOTA性能，改进与记忆累积正交，证实了个性化推理过程本身是独立于记忆增强的差异化改进来源。

Conclusion: SAGER首次证明推理过程本身可被个性化演进，为推荐系统提供了一条独立于记忆积累的性能提升路径，开创了"推理个性化"这一新研究方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based recommendation agents personalize what they know through evolving per-user semantic memory, yet how they reason remains a universal, static system prompt shared identically across all users. This asymmetry is a fundamental bottleneck: when a recommendation fails, the agent updates its memory of user preferences but never interrogates the decision logic that produced the failure, leaving its reasoning process structurally unchanged regardless of how many mistakes it accumulates. To address this bottleneck, we propose SAGER (Self-Evolving Agent for Personalized Recommendation), the first recommendation agent framework in which each user is equipped with a dedicated policy skill, a structured natural-language document encoding personalized decision principles that evolves continuously through interaction. SAGER introduces a two-representation skill architecture that decouples a rich evolution substrate from a minimal inference-time injection, an incremental contrastive chain-of-thought engine that diagnoses reasoning flaws by contrasting accepted against unchosen items while preserving accumulated priors, and skill-augmented listwise reasoning that creates fine-grained decision boundaries where the evolved skill provides genuine discriminative value. Experiments on four public benchmarks demonstrate that SAGER achieves state-of-the-art performance, with gains orthogonal to memory accumulation, confirming that personalizing the reasoning process itself is a qualitatively distinct source of recommendation improvement.

</details>


### [309] [Metric-agnostic Learning-to-Rank via Boosting and Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15101)
*Camilo Gomez,Pengyang Wang,Yanjie Fu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的列表式LTR框架，通过设计可微分的排序损失函数，结合梯度提升机的高效优化，解决传统LTR方法中排序指标不可微和泛化性受限的问题，实现高效且可迁移的排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LTR方法依赖单一排序指标（如NDCG、MAP）进行优化，存在两个主要局限：1）排序指标本身不可微分，导致训练过程不稳定且效率低下；2）单一指标优化难以泛化到其他排序评估指标。这些问题限制了LTR方法在实际信息检索系统中的应用效果。

Method: 1）提出一种新的可微分排序损失函数，融合了排序算子的平滑近似与每个查询的平均均方损失；2）将梯度提升机（GBM）适配为针对每个列表最小化所提损失函数的新型方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在信息检索评测指标上优于当前最优方法，同时保持了相近的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该列表式LTR框架通过可微分损失函数设计有效解决了排序指标不可微问题，通过多指标泛化能力拓展了传统LTR方法的适用范围，在保持高效性的前提下提升了排序性能。

Abstract: Learning-to-Rank (LTR) is a supervised machine learning approach that constructs models specifically designed to order a set of items or documents based on their relevance or importance to a given query or context. Despite significant success in real-world information retrieval systems, current LTR methods rely on one prefix ranking metric (e.g., such as Normalized Discounted Cumulative Gain (NDCG) or Mean Average Precision (MAP)) for optimizing the ranking objective function. Such metric-dependent setting limits LTR methods from two perspectives: (1) non-differentiable problem: directly optimizing ranking functions over a given ranking metric is inherently non-smooth, making the training process unstable and inefficient; (2) limited ranking utility: optimizing over one single metric makes it difficult to generalize well to other ranking metrics of interest. To address the above issues, we propose a novel listwise LTR framework for efficient and generalizable ranking purpose. Specifically, we propose a new differentiable ranking loss that combines a smooth approximation to the ranking operator with the average mean square loss per query. Then, we adapt gradient-boosting machines to minimize our proposed loss with respect to each list, a novel contribution. Finally, extensive experimental results confirm that our method outperforms the current state-of-the-art in information retrieval measures with similar efficiency.

</details>
